### [1] [Telomere damage enhances immunogenicity of neuroblastoma and accelerates response to anti-PD-L1 treatment.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41948996/)
*Stefanie Höppner,Lisa Werr,Beata Szilagyi,Christoph Bartenhagen,Anna-Maria Hellmann,Carolina Rosswog,Emma Linden,Gerrit Primke,Yvonne Kahlert,Nadliv Ibruli,Nadine Hemstedt,Witali Lorenz,Johannes Brägelmann,Michael Hölzel,Matthias Fischer*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究证实，在高风险神经母细胞瘤中，利用6-thio-dG诱导端粒功能障碍可激活cGAS-STING通路，并与抗PD-L1免疫检查点阻断疗法产生协同作用，显著促进肿瘤内免疫细胞浸润，克服单一免疫治疗的滞后效应，从而延缓肿瘤生长并延长生存期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险神经母细胞瘤患者预后较差，且多数肿瘤中端粒酶活跃。鉴于在其他癌症类型中，诱导端粒功能障碍（使用6-thio-dG）已成功克服对免疫检查点阻断的耐药性，本研究旨在探索该策略是否也能增强免疫检查点抑制剂在神经母细胞瘤中的抗肿瘤疗效。

Method: 1. **体外实验**：在小鼠神经母细胞瘤细胞中处理6-thio-dG，检测cGAS-STING通路激活及PD-L1表达情况。
2. **体内实验**：使用MYCN;ALKF1174L驱动的转基因神经母细胞瘤小鼠模型，分别评估单独使用6-thio-dG、单独使用抗PD-L1抗体以及两者联合治疗对肿瘤生长和生存率的影响。
3. **机制分析**：分析联合治疗对肿瘤组织中cGAS和PD-L1表达水平以及免疫细胞浸润情况的影响。

Result: 1. 6-thio-dG处理在体外诱导了小鼠神经母细胞瘤细胞中cGAS-STING通路的激活及PD-L1的表达。
2. 在体内模型中，6-thio-dG单药治疗延缓了肿瘤生长并延长了生存期。
3. 抗PD-L1单药治疗虽能延缓生长和改善生存，但存在初始滞后阶段。
4. 6-thio-dG与抗PD-L1联合治疗不仅增强了抗肿瘤效果，还克服了抗PD-L1治疗的初始滞后阶段。
5. 联合治疗在机制上诱导了cGAS和PD-L1的表达，并显著促进了肿瘤内的免疫细胞浸润。

Conclusion: 6-thio-dG治疗能够激活神经母细胞瘤中的cGAS-STING通路。诱导端粒功能障碍与免疫检查点阻断（抗PD-L1）相结合，能够通过增强肿瘤内免疫细胞浸润，显著改善高风险神经母细胞瘤小鼠模型的生存结局，为克服免疫治疗耐药提供了新的潜在策略。

Abstract: Telomerase is active in the majority of high-risk neuroblastomas, a pediatric tumor associated with poor patient outcomes. In other cancer types, resistance to immune checkpoint blockade was overcome by induction of telomere dysfunction using the telomerase substrate precursor 6-thio-2'-deoxyguanosine (6-thio-dG). Here, we explored whether induction of telomere dysfunction improves the anti-tumor efficacy of immune checkpoint inhibition in neuroblastoma. 6-thio-dG treatment induced the cyclic GMP-AMP synthase-stimulator of interferon genes (cGAS-STING) pathway and programmed cell death ligand-1 (PD-L1) expression in murine neuroblastoma cells in vitro. In a MYCN;ALKF1174L-driven transgenic neuroblastoma mouse model, 6-thio-dG treatment delayed tumor growth and prolonged survival. Treatment with anti-PD-L1 also led to growth delay and improved survival, which occurred; however, only after an initial lag phase. Combination with anti-PD-L1 improved the anti-tumor effects of 6-thio-dG and overcame the initial lag phase of anti-PD-L1 treatment. Mechanistically, 6-thio-dG combined with anti-PD-L1 treatment induced cGAS and PD-L1 expression and promoted immune cell infiltration in the tumors. Our findings suggest that 6-thio-dG treatment activates the cGAS-STING pathway in neuroblastoma and that induction of telomere dysfunction in combination with immune checkpoint blockade boosts intratumoral immune cell infiltration and improves survival in a high-risk neuroblastoma mouse model.

</details>


### [2] [A rationally designed bispecific antibody targeting GPC3 and PD-L1 induces tumor-directed immune activation and cytotoxicity.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41951084/)
*Rui Yuan,Yuhan Zhang,Simo Liu,Xueying Li,Zimeng Zhang,Hui Guo,Feng Wang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种针对GPC3和PD-L1的双特异性抗体（bsAb），旨在解决免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）的系统性免疫相关不良反应。该抗体通过降低对膜结合PD-L1的亲和力，实现了仅在GPC3高表达肿瘤细胞上的选择性PD-1/PD-L1阻断，从而在保持抗肿瘤效力的同时显著降低系统性毒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管免疫检查点抑制剂（ICIs）显著改善了多种恶性肿瘤的临床预后，但系统性免疫相关不良反应（irAEs）仍是当前治疗策略的主要局限。现有的抗PD-L1单克隆抗体缺乏肿瘤特异性，容易攻击表达PD-L1的正常组织，因此亟需开发一种能够提高肿瘤特异性并减少全身毒性的新型靶向免疫疗法。

Method: 研究团队设计并开发了一种GPC3 × PD-L1双特异性抗体。该方法的核心机制是利用肝细胞癌相关抗原GPC3作为肿瘤定位锚点，同时显著降低抗体对膜结合PD-L1的亲和力（相比阿替利珠单抗降低约300倍）。通过体外实验评估其在GPC3高表达肿瘤细胞中的免疫检查点抑制能力，并在GPC3阳性肿瘤模型中评估其体内抗肿瘤反应、选择性及生物分布特征。

Result: 1. **亲和力调节**：GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb保留了对GPC3的高亲和力，但对膜PD-L1的亲和力显著降低，实现了GPC3依赖性的PD-1/PD-L1阻断。
2. **体外效力**：在GPC3高表达的肿瘤细胞中表现出强效且选择性的免疫检查点抑制作用。
3. **体内疗效与安全性**：在GPC3阳性肿瘤模型中引发强烈的抗肿瘤反应；与抗PD-L1单克隆抗体不同，该bsAb仅针对GPC3/PD-L1双阳性肿瘤，避免了非靶向组织的损伤。
4. **生物分布**：证实了该抗体在GPC3阳性肿瘤中的优先蓄积。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了概念验证（POC），表明这种肿瘤靶向的免疫检查点抑制剂在表达GPC3的恶性肿瘤（如肝细胞癌）中具有高效性和安全性。通过增强肿瘤特异性并减少系统性毒性，GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb有望克服传统ICI疗法的局限性，为临床治疗提供更具精准性的新策略。

Abstract: Immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) have transformed cancer treatment by significantly improving clinical outcomes across a range of malignancies. However, systemic immune-related adverse events (irAEs) remain a major limitation of current ICI strategies. To address this challenge, we developed a bispecific antibody (bsAb) to selectively target tumor cells co-expressing hepatocellular carcinoma-associated antigen glypian-3 (GPC3) and programmed Cell Death Ligand 1 (PD-L1). The GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb retained high affinity for GPC3 while exhibiting a ~ 300-fold reduced affinity for membrane PD-L1 compared to atezolizumab, enabling GPC3-dependent PD-1/PD-L1 blockade. The GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb demonstrated potent and selective immune checkpoint inhibition in GPC3high tumor cells in vitro and elicited robust antitumor responses in GPC3-positive tumor models. Importantly, the GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb, unlike the anti-PD-L1 monoclonal antibody, exhibited selectivity for GPC3/PD-L1 double-positive tumors while sparing PD-L1-positive but non-targeting tissues. Biodistribution studies further confirmed preferential accumulation of the GPC3 × PD-L1 bsAb in GPC3-positive tumors. Collectively, these data provide proof-of-concept (POC) validation for the efficacy and safety of a tumor-targeted immune checkpoint inhibitor, highlighting its potential to enhance tumor specificity and reduce systemic toxicity for GPC3-expressing malignancies such as hepatocellular carcinoma.

</details>


### [3] [LDHA-driven lactate metabolism promotes MDSC activation and immunosuppressive microenvironment in prostate cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41922574/)
*Xiyi Wei,Xiao Li,Yitong Pan,Yuwei Zhang,Da Zhong,Weiyu Kong,Yue Wang,Zijie Yu,Wenchuan Shao,Yuxiang Dong,Silin Jiang,Zige Qiu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiang Li,Yize Li,Shouyong Gu,Chenxi Tian,Chao Qin,Qingyi Zhu,Ninghan Feng,Ninghong Song,Bing Yao,Zhou Yang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究揭示前列腺癌中LDHA驱动的乳酸代谢通过促进PMN-MDSCs浸润和活化，导致免疫抑制和治疗抵抗；抑制LDHA可恢复T细胞活性并增强抗PD-L1疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌（PCa）免疫治疗疗效有限，主要归因于其高度免疫抑制的肿瘤微环境（TME），但支撑这种免疫抵抗的具体代谢机制尚不明确。

Method: 整合代谢组学、单细胞转录组学和空间转录组学分析；利用小鼠模型进行LDHA基因敲除实验；使用药理学抑制剂FX-11联合抗PD-L1抗体进行治疗评估。

Result: LDHA在PCa恶性上皮细胞中高表达，与乳酸产生增加和免疫排斥相关；LDHA高表达肿瘤富集多形核髓系来源抑制性细胞（PMN-MDSCs），且两者空间共定位；乳酸通过MCT1摄取促进PMN-MDSC分化并上调Arg1和NOS2，抑制T细胞；敲除LDHA减少PMN-MDSC浸润，恢复CD8+ T细胞活性并抑制肿瘤生长；FX-11抑制LDHA与抗PD-L1疗法协同作用，产生持久的肿瘤消退。

Conclusion: LDHA驱动的乳酸代谢是前列腺癌免疫逃逸的关键代谢检查点；联合LDHA抑制与免疫检查点阻断为克服免疫治疗抵抗提供了理论依据和新策略。

Abstract: Immunotherapy has achieved limited efficacy in prostate cancer (PCa), largely due to its profoundly immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME). However, the metabolic mechanisms underpinning this immune resistance remain poorly defined. Here, we identify lactate dehydrogenase A (LDHA)-driven lactate metabolism as a critical regulator of myeloid-derived suppressor cell (MDSC) activation in PCa. Integrated metabolomic, single-cell, and spatial transcriptomic analyses revealed that LDHA is highly expressed in PCa malignant epithelial cells and correlates with increased lactate production and immune exclusion. LDHA-high tumors exhibited enriched infiltration of polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs), which were spatially co-localized with LDHA-positive tumor regions. Mechanistically, lactate uptake through monocarboxylate transporter 1 (MCT1) enhanced PMN-MDSC differentiation and upregulated Arg1 and NOS2, reinforcing T cell suppression. Genetic ablation of LDHA in murine models markedly reduced PMN-MDSC infiltration, restored CD8+T cell activity, and inhibited tumor growth. Pharmacological inhibition of LDHA with FX-11 synergized with anti-PD-L1 therapy, producing durable tumor regression. Collectively, these findings define LDHA-driven lactate metabolism as a key metabolic checkpoint in PCa immune evasion and provide a rationale for combining LDHA inhibition with immune checkpoint blockade to overcome immunotherapy resistance.

</details>


### [4] [An SPP1-SOCS1 pathway constrains interferon responses in tumor-associated macrophages and shapes an immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42049035/)
*Liangzhan Sun,Xiaojing Chu,Tingting Kong,Xirui Chen,Jianing Ru,Qianqian Gao,Wei Zhou,Xiliang Wang,Sijin Cheng,Linnan Zhu,Zemin Zhang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究鉴定出SPP1+肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）是导致免疫检查点阻断（ICB）无反应的关键亚群。通过删除巨噬细胞中的Spp1，可稳定SOCS1并增强IFN-γ信号，从而重塑肿瘤微环境，减少Treg细胞，增加效应T细胞，最终抑制肿瘤生长并增强抗PD-L1疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）会抑制抗肿瘤免疫并降低对免疫检查点阻断（ICB）的反应。研究旨在阐明TAM程序如何导致对ICB治疗的无反应，以寻找改善免疫治疗效果的潜在靶点。

Method: 1. 整合12种癌症类型的公共单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）数据集，鉴定肿瘤富集的巨噬细胞亚群。
2. 比较ICB无反应者与有反应者中TAMs的SPP1表达水平。
3. 构建小鼠模型，通过删除巨噬细胞中的Spp1基因，观察其对肿瘤生长、生存期及肿瘤微环境（TME）的影响。
4. 机制研究：分析SPP1与TRIM21的相互作用及其对SOCS1泛素化和IFN-γ-STAT1-ISG信号通路的调控作用。
5. 体内实验评估靶向SPP1对抗PD-L1疗法疗效的影响。

Result: 1. SPP1+ TAMs是具有免疫抑制特征的肿瘤富集亚群，且在多种肿瘤的ICB无反应者中SPP1表达更高。
2. 巨噬细胞Spp1缺失可抑制肿瘤生长并延长生存期。
3. Spp1缺失重塑了TME：调节性T细胞（Treg）频率降低，干扰素（IFN）-γ+ CD4+ T细胞和GZMB+ CD8+ T细胞增加，免疫及恶性隔室中干扰素刺激基因（ISG）表达增强。
4. 机制上，胞内SPP1与TRIM21相互作用，限制SOCS1泛素化，稳定SOCS1介导的负反馈，从而抑制TAMs中的IFN-γ-STAT1-ISG信号。
5. 靶向SPP1在体内增强了抗PD-L1治疗的疗效。

Conclusion: 靶向TAM中的SPP1-IFN-γ轴可重塑肿瘤微环境，是增强对免疫检查点阻断（ICB）治疗反应的有效治疗策略。

Abstract: Tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) can suppress antitumor immunity and reduce responses to immune checkpoint blockade (ICB). Here, we asked how TAM programs contribute to ICB non-response. Integration of public single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) datasets across 12 cancer types identified SPP1+ TAMs as a tumor-enriched macrophage subset with immunosuppressive features. TAMs from ICB non-responders across multiple tumor types exhibited higher SPP1 expression. In murine models, macrophage Spp1 deletion suppressed tumor growth and prolonged survival and was associated with a remodeled tumor microenvironment featuring reduced T regulatory cell (Treg) frequencies, increased interferon (IFN)-γ+ CD4+ and GZMB+ CD8+ T cells, and augmented interferon-stimulated gene (ISG) expression across immune and malignant compartments. Mechanistically, intracellular SPP1 interacted with TRIM21 to limit SOCS1 ubiquitination, stabilizing SOCS1-mediated negative feedback and dampening IFN-γ-STAT1-ISG signaling in TAMs. Consistently, SPP1 targeting enhanced the efficacy of anti-PD-L1 therapy in vivo. Thus, remodeling the TME via targeting the TAM SPP1-IFN-γ axis presents a therapeutic avenue for enhancing responses to ICB.

</details>


### [5] [Phase I trial of Ipatasertib plus Atezolizumab enhances PI3K/AKT pathway immune responses in solid tumors and refractory glioblastoma.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42043781/)
*Crescens Tiu,Wing Yau,Diogo Silva,Nick Waldron,Malaka Ameratunga,Mariana Scaranti,Andrea Biondo,Liam Welsh,Antonia Creak,Timothy L Jones,Andrew J Martin,Leslie Bridges,Anna Zachariou,Mateus Crespo,Ana Ferreira,Ruth Riisnaes,Bora Gurel,Ines Figueiredo,Denisa Bogdan,Wei Yuan,Ricardo Morilla,Karen Swales,Shaun Decordova,Toby Prout,Mona Parmar,Bindu Baikady,Mihaela Rata,Matthew Blackledge,Nina Tunariu,Philip Benjamin,Philip Rich,Robert Daly,Xinjie Hu,Christina Yap,Igor Vivanco,Alec Paschalis,Adam Sharp,Udai Banerji,Anna Minchom,Johann de Bono,Juanita Lopez*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 这项Ib期临床研究评估了PI3K/AKT抑制剂ipatasertib联合PD-L1抑制剂atezolizumab治疗难治性晚期实体瘤（包括复发性胶质母细胞瘤）的安全性和初步疗效。结果显示该组合耐受性良好，能显著减少肿瘤微环境中的调节性T细胞并增加效应CD8+ T细胞浸润，部分难治性胶质母细胞瘤患者获得持久缓解，证实了该联合疗法克服免疫治疗耐药的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PI3K/AKT信号通路的激活通过抑制效应T细胞浸润和增强调节性T细胞活性，促进肿瘤免疫逃逸并导致对免疫检查点抑制剂（如PD-1/PD-L1阻断剂）的耐药。临床前研究表明，抑制该通路可恢复抗肿瘤免疫力并与PD-1/PD-L1阻断产生协同作用。因此，本研究旨在探索靶向PI3K/AKT通路联合atezolizumab在克服复发性胶质母细胞瘤和晚期实体瘤免疫治疗耐药方面的临床协同潜力。

Method: 这是一项研究者发起的Ib期、开放标签研究（NCT03673787）。研究分为两部分：Part A为概念验证剂量递增阶段，采用3+3设计，评估ipatasertib联合atezolizumab的安全性；Part B为探索性阶段，纳入6个队列。患者分为两个主要队列：Cohort A1纳入治疗难治性晚期癌症成人患者，Cohort A2纳入复发性胶质母细胞瘤患者。研究主要终点是评估联合用药的安全性、免疫调节作用及初步疗效。

Result: 联合治疗方案耐受性良好，在推荐二期剂量（ipatasertib 400 mg/天 + atezolizumab 1200 mg/每3周）下未出现剂量限制性毒性。药效动力学分析显示，肿瘤微环境中FOXP3+调节性T细胞耗竭，CD8+效应T细胞浸润增加。在某些治疗难治性或复发性胶质母细胞瘤患者中观察到了持久的例外反应（exceptional responses）。

Conclusion: 这是首份在临床样本中证实ipatasertib能有效耗竭FOXP3+调节性T细胞并增加效应CD8+ T细胞在肿瘤微环境中浸润的报告。这种免疫微环境的改变与治疗难治性胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）亚组患者的初步疗效相关，表明该联合策略具有克服免疫耐药的临床前景。

Abstract: PURPOSE: Activation of the phosphatodylinositol-3-kinase/AKT (PI3K/AKT) signalling pathway promotes tumor immune evasion by suppressing effector T-cell infiltration and enhancing regulatory T-cell activity contributing to resistance to immune checkpoint inhibitors. Preclinical studies have demonstrated that inhibition of this pathway can restore anti-tumor immunity and synergize with PD-1/PD-L1 blockade. We explore the synergistic clinical potential of targeting the PI3K/AKT pathway in combination with atezolizumab to overcome immunotherapy resistance in recurrent glioblastoma and advanced solid tumors.

EXPERIMENTAL DESIGN: Phase 1b, investigator-initiated, open-label study (NCT03673787) composed of a proof-of-concept dose escalation Part A of ipatasertib plus atezolizumab in a 3+3 design. Adult patients with treatment refractory advanced cancers were enrolled into Cohort A1 and recurrent glioblastoma onto Cohort A2. Part B enrolled patients into 6 exploratory cohorts. Study aims to evaluate the safety, immune-modulatory effects and preliminary efficacy of the combination of ipatasertib with atezolizumab.

RESULTS: The combination was well tolerated, with no dose-limiting toxicities at the recommended phase 2 dose of ipatasertib 400 mg/daily plus atezolizumab 1200mg every 3 weeks. Pharmacodynamic analysis demonstrated depletion of FOXP3+ regulatory T cells and increased infiltration of CD8+ effector T cells within the tumor microenvironment. Durable exceptional responses were seen in some patients with treatment-refractory or recurrent glioblastoma.

CONCLUSION: This is the first report in clinical samples showing that ipatasertib efficiently depletes FOXP3+ regulatory T cells and results in increased infiltration of effector CD8+ T cells in the tumor microenvironment. This was associated with preliminary efficacy in a subset of patients with treatment-refractory glioblastoma (GBM).

</details>


### [6] [First-line treatments for metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer with sugemalimab plus chemotherapy: a China-based cost-effectiveness analysis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42032906/)
*Zixuan Zhang,Kehui Meng,Meiyu Wu,Andong Li,Yangke Yi,Chaohao Shi,Xiaomin Wan,Chongqing Tan,Zhongbiao Jiang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究评估了舒格利单抗联合化疗作为中国转移性非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）一线治疗的经济性。结果显示，尽管该方案具有长期临床获益，但在当前价格下，其增量成本效果比（ICER）高于支付意愿阈值，不具备成本效益。提高PD-L1高表达人群的治疗比例、调整报销政策或降低药价可能改善其经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估舒格利单抗（sugemalimab）联合化疗作为转移性非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）一线治疗方案在中国医疗环境下的成本效益，为医保决策和临床资源分配提供经济学依据。

Method: 构建了一个周期为21天、时间跨度为10年的三状态马尔可夫模型，对比舒格利单抗联合化疗与安慰剂联合化疗的效果。模型输入数据源自GEMSTONE-302试验的4年生存数据及公开来源。主要结局指标包括质量调整生命年（QALYs）和增量成本效果比（ICERs）。支付意愿（WTP）阈值设定为2024年中国人均GDP的三倍（40,062.34美元/QALY），并进行敏感性分析以验证模型稳健性。

Result: 基础案例分析显示，鳞状和非鳞状NSCLC的ICER分别为65,766.82美元/QALY和70,117.72美元/QALY，均超过WTP阈值。亚组分析表明，PD-L1表达≥50%的患者群体经济性最佳（ICER为60,516.14美元/QALY），而PD-L1 < 1%的患者群体经济性最差（ICER为80,599.12美元/QALY）。敏感性分析指出，药物价格和效用值是影响ICER的最关键参数。

Conclusion: 舒格利单抗联合铂类化疗虽能为转移性NSCLC患者带来长期临床获益，但以当前价格在中国并不具备成本效益。建议通过针对高PD-L1表达患者进行精准治疗、调整医保报销政策以及降低药物价格来提升其成本效益。

Abstract: OBJECTIVE: To assess the cost-effectiveness of sugemalimab plus chemotherapy as first-line treatment for metastatic Non-small cell lung cancer(NSCLC) in China.

METHODS: A three-state Markov model with 21-day cycles over 10 years was developed to compare sugemalimab plus chemotherapy versus placebo plus chemotherapy. Key model inputs were derived from the 4-year survival data of the GEMSTONE-302 trial and publicly available sources. Outcomes were measured in quality-adjusted life years(QALYs) and incremental cost-effectiveness ratios(ICERs). The willingness-to-pay(WTP) threshold was set at three times the per capita GDP of China in 2024(40,062.34$/QALY). Sensitivity analyses were performed to assess model robustness.

RESULTS: Base-case ICERs were 65,766.82$/QALY for squamous and 70,117.72$/QALY for non-squamous NSCLC, both above the WTP threshold. Subgroup analyses indicated that patients with PD-L1 expression ≥50% had the most favorable ICER(60,516.14$/QALY), while those with PD-L1 < 1% had the least favorable outcome(80,599.12$/QALY). Sensitivity analyses identified drug price and utility values as the most influential parameters affecting ICERs.

CONCLUSION: Sugemalimab plus platinum-based chemotherapy offers long-term clinical benefits for metastatic NSCLC but is not cost-effective at current prices in China. Improving cost-effectiveness may require targeting patients with high PD-L1 expression, adjusting reimbursement policies, and reducing drug prices.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 共入组60例患者（37例iCCA，23例GBC），85%为经治患者。总体ORR为12%（完全缓解2%，部分缓解10%），其中iCCA亚组为3%，GBC亚组为26%。总体6个月PFS为27%（iCCA 19%，GBC 39%），mOS为7个月。在免疫治疗初治人群中，总体ORR为19%（iCCA 10%，GBC 38%）。20%的患者观察到严重的免疫相关不良事件。

Abstract: PURPOSE: Anti-PD-1/PD-L1 blockade combined with chemotherapy has become first line treatment for advanced biliary tract cancers (BTC). Combined anti-PD-1/CTLA-4 blockade using nivolumab and ipilimumab has shown encouraging activity in patients with intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (iCCA) and gallbladder carcinoma (GBC) in two trials (CA209-538, SWOG1609). MoST-CIRCUIT further evaluated combined checkpoint blockade using nivo/ipi in patients with advanced iCCA and GBC.

PATIENTS AND METHODS: Patients with a maximum of 1 line of prior systemic therapy were enrolled as cohort B into MoST-CIRCUIT, a single arm, non-randomised phase 2 trial. Patients received nivolumab 3mg/kg and ipilimumab 1mg/kg q3 weekly for four doses, followed by nivolumab 480mg q4 weekly for 96 weeks. Response (RECIST 1.1) was assessed every 12 weeks. Co-primary endpoints were objective response rate (ORR) and 6 month-progression free-survival (6-PFS) with the secondary endpoints being median overall survival (mOS), progression-free survival (PFS) and treatment related toxicity.

RESULTS: 60 patients (37 iCCA and 23 GBC) were enrolled with 85% being pre-treated, including 13 patients with durvalumab. ORR was 12% (2% CR, 10% PR): 3% and 26% in iCCA and GBC subgroups respectively. The 6-month-PFS was 27% (iCCA 19%; GBC 39%) and mOS 7 months. In the immunotherapy-naïve population ORR was 19% (iCCA 10%; GBC 38%). Severe immune-related adverse events were observed in 20% of patients.

CONCLUSIONS: Efficacy was limited in what is the largest BTC cohort treated to date with combined anti-CTLA-4/PD-1 blockade. Encouraging activity was observed in the GBC subgroup. Further evaluation of checkpoint inhibition in BTC should focus on GBC patients.

### [8] [MMRN1-EGFR drives sialylglycan-Siglec immune evasion in AML leukemia stem cells.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42030946/)
*Meixi Peng,Yongxiu Huang,Mengyun Zhang,Qinrong Yan,Lulu Li,Yaoqi Gui,Jingsong Cheng,Yanni Sun,Yi Mo,Wenqiong Xiang,Yongjie Zhang,Li Wang,Qin Wen,Xi Zhang,Yu Hou*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究鉴定出白血病干细胞（LSC）特异性高表达蛋白MMRN1，其通过激活EGFR/STAT1通路促进唾液酸聚糖积累，形成类似PD-1/CTLA-4的糖免疫检查点以介导免疫逃逸，并通过EGFR/STAT5/CD9通路维持LSC自我更新。靶向MMRN1或联合使用EGFR抑制剂（厄洛替尼）与化疗/去甲基化药物可显著抑制AML进展并增强免疫治疗疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性髓系白血病（AML）的复发和治疗耐药主要由白血病干细胞（LSC）驱动，其核心机制在于免疫逃逸。然而，LSC特异性调控免疫逃逸的关键分子机制尚不明确，亟需发现新的治疗靶点以克服耐药性并改善预后。

Method: 1. 分子鉴定：识别LSC中特异性高表达的Multimerin 1 (MMRN1)。
2. 机制解析：阐明MMRN1通过EGF样结构域激活EGFR/STAT1通路，抑制Neu5Ac降解，导致唾液酸聚糖积累，进而激活Sialylglycan-Siglec免疫检查点轴；同时通过EGFR/STAT5/CD9通路维持LSC自我更新。
3. 功能验证：通过基因敲除MMRN1评估其对AML进展及抗PD-L1/CTLA-4治疗的协同作用。
4. 临床转化：在临床试验（ChiCTR2500097714）中评估EGFR抑制剂（厄洛替尼）联合阿扎胞苷及HAG方案治疗复发/难治性AML的疗效。

Result: 1. MMRN1在LSC中特异性高表达，通过EGFR/STAT1通路驱动唾液酸聚糖积累，形成糖免疫检查点，抑制T细胞和NK细胞活性，介导免疫逃逸。
2. MMRN1通过EGFR/STAT5/CD9通路维持LSC的自我更新能力。
3. 基因敲除MMRN1显著抑制AML进展，并与抗PD-L1/CTLA-4治疗产生协同效应。
4. 临床试验显示，厄洛替尼联合阿扎胞苷及HAG方案在复发/难治性AML患者中达到75%的缓解率，证实了阻断MMRN1/EGFR轴的临床潜力。

Conclusion: MMRN1是维持LSC生存和介导免疫逃逸的双重功能靶点。破坏MMRN1或抑制EGFR可重塑免疫抑制性肿瘤微环境，为AML免疫治疗提供了新的策略，特别是EGFR抑制剂联合现有疗法在复发/难治性AML中展现出显著的临床疗效。

Abstract: Leukemia stem cells (LSCs) drive acute myeloid leukemia (AML) relapse and therapy resistance, predominantly through immune evasion. Here, we identify multimerin 1 (MMRN1) as being highly and specifically expressed in LSCs. Mechanistically, MMRN1 activates the epidermal growth factor receptor (EGFR)/signal transducer and activator of transcription 1 (STAT1) pathway via its epidermal growth factor (EGF)-like domain, suppressing Neu5Ac degradation to drive sialylglycan accumulation, which forms glycoimmune checkpoints functionally akin to programmed death 1 (PD-1)/the cytotoxic T-lymphocyte antigen-4 (CTLA-4). These sialylglycans activate the sialylglycan-Siglec immune checkpoint axis, impairing T/natural killer (NK) cell activity and enabling LSC immune evasion. Additionally, MMRN1 sustains LSC self-renewal via the EGFR/STAT5/CD9 pathway. Genetic ablation of MMRN1 markedly suppresses AML progression and synergizes with anti-PD-L1/CTLA-4 therapy. In a clinical trial (ChiCTR2500097714), erlotinib (an EGFR inhibitor) combined with azacitidine plus the HAG regimen, which consists of homoharringtonine, a low dose of cytarabine, and granulocyte colony-stimulating factor priming, achieves a remission rate of 75% in relapsed/refractory AML, likely via MMRN1/EGFR axis blockade. Our findings establish MMRN1 as a dual-functional target for LSC maintenance and immune evasion and propose that disrupting MMRN1 or EGFR remodels the immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment, offering a promising strategy for AML immunotherapy.

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究基于J-TAIL-2试验的359名日本非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）和广泛期小细胞肺癌（ES-SCLC）患者血浆样本，通过邻近延伸分析技术评估了约560种蛋白质作为阿替利珠单抗联合化疗疗效及安全性的生物标志物。研究发现IL-6、MUC-16和KRT-19与较短的无进展生存期相关，颗粒酶A和B升高与治疗反应相关，基线免疫刺激蛋白高水平与免疫相关不良事件（irAEs）相关，提示这些蛋白质具有作为预测性生物标志物的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PD-L1表达在某些情况下可预测阿替利珠单抗的疗效，但临床仍需额外的微创、基于血液的生物标志物来更准确地预测治疗反应及免疫相关不良事件（irAEs），以优化非小细胞肺癌和广泛期小细胞肺癌患者的个体化治疗。

Method: 这是一项探索性生物标志物研究，纳入359名参与J-TAIL-2临床试验的患者（包括NSCLC和ES-SCLC队列）。研究人员在三个时间点（基线、第二次阿替利珠单抗给药前、irAEs发作时）采集血浆样本，利用邻近延伸分析技术（Proximity Extension Assay）检测约560种癌症或免疫相关蛋白的表达水平。通过蛋白质组学比较（P < 0.05且log2折叠变化 > 0.5），分析蛋白质水平变化与J-TAIL-2报告中临床结果（如无进展生存期、治疗反应、irAEs）之间的关联。

3. 基线时期高水平的免疫刺激蛋白与各种治疗方案中免疫相关不良事件（irAEs）的发生相关。
4. 亚组分析（如老年患者、EGFR突变肿瘤患者）显示蛋白质表达变化具有多样性且依赖于具体治疗方案。

Conclusion: 鉴定出的特定血浆蛋白（如IL-6、MUC-16、KRT-19及颗粒酶等）与阿替利珠单抗联合治疗的疗效及安全性密切相关。这些数据支持进一步在不同癌症类型和阿替利珠单抗联合方案中验证这些生物标志物的临床相关性，以期改善患者分层管理及irAEs的预测能力。

Abstract: J-TAIL-2 evaluated the efficacy and safety of atezolizumab, an anti-programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1), plus chemotherapy in patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) and extensive-stage small cell lung cancer (ES-SCLC) in Japanese clinical practice, demonstrating comparable outcomes to those in the corresponding Phase 3 trials. Although PD-L1 expression is predictive of atezolizumab efficacy in some settings, additional minimally invasive, blood-based biomarkers are needed. This exploratory biomarker study included 359 J-TAIL-2 patients. The NSCLC cohort received atezolizumab combined with carboplatin and nab-paclitaxel (CnP, n = 42), cisplatin/carboplatin and pemetrexed (n = 72), or bevacizumab plus carboplatin and paclitaxel (bev + CP, n = 135). The ES-SCLC cohort received atezolizumab plus carboplatin and etoposide (n = 100). Approximately 560 cancer- or immune-related proteins were evaluated using the Proximity Extension Assay from blood plasma collected at three timepoints: baseline, before the second atezolizumab dose, and at the onset of immune-related adverse events (irAEs). Protein-wise comparisons were made to evaluate significant changes (P < 0.05 and > 0.5 log2 fold change) and linked to clinical outcomes reported in J-TAIL-2. IL-6, MUC-16, and KRT-19 were associated with shorter progression-free survival across multiple regimens. Granzyme A and B, immune activation markers, were elevated in responders who received atezolizumab + CnP and atezolizumab + bev + CP. High levels of baseline immune stimulation proteins were associated with irAEs across regimens. Protein expression changes were varied and regimen-dependent in subgroup analyses including older patients and those with EGFR-mutant tumors. These data warrant further investigation across different cancer types and atezolizumab-containing regimens to determine their relevance to efficacy and irAE occurrence of atezolizumab combination therapy. Trial Registration: ClinicalTrials.gov ID, NCT04501497 (J-TAIL-2) and NCT04818983 (biomarker study).

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 基于恩沃利单抗的联合疗法在既往接受过治疗的晚期胃癌患者中显示出有前景的疗效，尤其是在那些此前未接受过免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）治疗的患者中。该方案为HER2阴性、MSS晚期G/GEJ腺癌的二线治疗提供了新的潜在选择。

Patients in both groups received envafolimab (200 mg, subcutaneous injection (sc), days 1 and 15, Q4W) combined with lenvatinib and albumin-bound paclitaxel (100 mg/m2, IV, days 1, 8 and 15, Q4W, up to 6 cycles) until disease progression, unacceptable toxicity, or refusal of continuation. The primary endpoint was objective response rate (ORR).

RESULTS: A total of 30 patients were included for safety and efficacy analysis. As of data cutoff (Sep 14, 2024), the median follow-up was 17.0 months (IQR: 8.0-18.8) in Group A. The ORR was 60.0%, and the DCR was 100.0%. The mPFS was 8.2 months (95% CI 6.1-10.4) with mOS of 14.8 months (95% CI 7.4-22.2). With a median follow-up of 9.0 months (IQR: 6.1-16.2) in Group B, the ORR was 46.7%, and the DCR was 100.0%. The mPFS was 5.9 months (95% CI 3.8-8.1), and the mOS was 11.5 months (95% CI 3.1-19.9). The overall incidence of adverse events (AEs) of any grade was 100%. While Group A showed numerically better outcomes than Group B, there was no statistically significant difference in PFS (P = 0.629) or OS (P = 0.873) between the two groups.

CONCLUSIONS: Second-line envafolimab-based combination therapy demonstrated promising effects in previously treated advanced GC, particularly in those who have not previously received ICIs.

</details>

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: SKYROCKET是一项单中心、单臂II期研究，评估了立体定向体部放疗（SBRT）联合阿替利珠单抗和替拉戈鲁单抗在PD-L1阳性非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）中的疗效。结果显示中位无进展生存期（PFS）为9.3个月，安全性可控。单细胞测序揭示临床获益组中CD8前体耗竭T细胞扩增且调节性T细胞（Tregs）减少，而无效组则维持抑制性Tregs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证在PD-L1阳性转移性非小细胞肺癌治疗中，加入立体定向体部放疗（SBRT）是否能通过增强免疫原性死亡或改变肿瘤微环境，从而提升阿替利珠单抗（抗PD-L1）与替拉戈鲁单抗（抗TIGIT）联合疗法的抗肿瘤功效。

Method: 采用单中心、单臂II期临床试验设计。41名患者接受针对所有转移灶的SBRT，随后在SBRT完成后7天内开始每3周一次阿替利珠单抗联合替拉戈鲁单抗治疗。主要终点为研究者评估的从SBRT开始计算的无进展生存期（PFS）。探索性终点包括对基线和治疗中肿瘤活检样本进行单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq），并根据临床获益（CB）与无临床获益（NCB）进行亚组分析。

Result: 中位随访9.8个月时，中位PFS为9.3个月（95% CI, 6.0-NR），未观察到新的安全信号。scRNA-seq分析显示，治疗后CD8前体耗竭T细胞（TPEX）显著扩增，且CD8 TPEX/TEX亚群高表达TIGIT和PD-1。在临床获益组中，CD4调节性T细胞（Tregs）减少，Treg抑制作用及NECTIN-TIGIT信号通路减弱；而在无临床获益组中，增殖性CTLA4high/TIGIT high Tregs增加，并由ICAM/Galectin-CTLA4通路支持。

Conclusion: SBRT序贯阿替利珠单抗联合替拉戈鲁单抗在PD-L1阳性转移性NSCLC患者中显示出令人鼓舞的临床活性和可控的安全性。机制上，临床获益可能与CD8 T细胞扩增及免疫抑制性Tregs群体的减少有关。

Abstract: PURPOSE: SKYROCKET is a single-center, single-arm phase II study that evaluated the addition of SBRT to atezolizumab plus tiragolumab enhances anti-tumor efficacy in PD-L1-positive NSCLC.

PATIENTS AND METHODS: Patients received SBRT to all metastatic sites, and subsequently received atezolizumab and tiragolumab every 3 weeks on day 1 of each 21-day cycle with 7 days of completion of SBRT. The primary endpoint was investigator-assessed progression-free survival (PFS) from the start of SBRT. Exploratory endpoints included single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) obtained from tumor biopsies at baseline (BL) and on-treatment (OT), and were further characterized as clinical benefit (CB, PR or SD ≥6 months) and no clinical benefit (NCB, PD or SD <6 months).

RESULTS: A total of 41 patients were enrolled. At a median duration of follow-up of 9.8 months, median PFS at the start of SBRT was 9.3 months (95% CI, 6.0-NR). No new safety signal was observed. ScRNA-seq of paired BL and OT (n=3) and single-time point (n=2) revealed that CD8 progenitor-exhausted (TPEX) cells markedly expanded after treatment, with high TIGIT and PD-1 expression in CD8 TPEX/TEX subsets. CD4 Tregs were reduced in the CB group but increased in the NCB group. The CB group showed reduced Treg suppression and NECTIN-TIGIT signaling, whereas NCB maintained proliferative CTLA4high/TIGIT high Tregs supported by ICAM/Galectin-CTLA4 pathways.

CONCLUSIONS: SBRT followed by atezolizumab plus tiragolumab showed encouraging clinical activity with manageable safety in PD-L1-positive metastatic NSCLC.

</details>

*Francesca Jackson-Spence,James Larkin,Poulam Patel,Begoña P Valderrama,Alejo Rodriguez-Vida,Hilary Glen,Fiona C Thistlethwaite,Christy Ralph,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan,Maria Jose Mendez-Vidal,Merrida Childress,Wenshu Li,Patrick Boyle,Amaya Gascó,Aleksandra Markovets,Ryan Hartmaier,Charlotte Ackerman,Bernadett E Szabados,Garima Priyadarshini,Fahmida Jamal,Thomas Powles,Cristina Suárez*

TL;DR: CALYPSO研究最终结果显示，赛沃替尼联合度伐利尤单抗在MET驱动的乳头状肾细胞癌（PRC）中显示出显著的生存获益（中位OS 27.4个月），且ctDNA清除与改善的总生存期相关，支持正在进行的关键性III期SAMETA试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于前期CALYPSO研究证实了赛沃替尼和度伐利尤单抗在MET驱动的乳头状肾细胞癌（PRC）中的活性，本研究旨在报告该单臂II期研究的最终疗效结果，并进行探索性的循环肿瘤DNA（ctDNA）生物标志物分析，以进一步验证治疗价值及寻找预测性生物标志物。

Method: 这是一项针对初治或经治PRC患者的单臂II期研究，评估赛沃替尼联合度伐利尤单抗的疗效。主要终点包括客观缓解率（ORR）、无进展生存期（PFS）和总生存期（OS）。使用FoundationOne CDx检测DNA改变及MET/PD-L1或MET/TMB共阳性状态。收集基线和治疗期间的ctDNA，并将其与临床结局进行相关性分析。

Result: 中位随访41个月时，意向治疗人群（ITT, N=41）的ORR为34%，而MET驱动患者（n=17）的ORR为53%。ITT人群与MET驱动人群的中位PFS分别为6.5个月和13.9个月，中位OS分别为18.3个月和27.4个月。PD-L1状态和TMB与疗效无相关性。基线ctDNA阳性与较短的OS相关（中位7.3 vs 33.3个月），而ctDNA清除和平均变异等位基因频率降低与改善的OS显著相关。

Conclusion: 赛沃替尼联合度伐利尤单抗在MET驱动的PRC患者中显示出具有临床意义的总生存期获益，这一结果支持正在进行的SAMETA III期临床试验。此外，ctDNA动态变化可能作为有用的预测性生物标志物，用于评估治疗反应和预后。

Abstract: PURPOSE: The CALYPSO study demonstrated activity of savolitinib and durvalumab in MET-driven papillary renal cancer (PRC). We report final efficacy outcomes and exploratory circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) biomarker analysis.

METHODS: This single-arm phase II study evaluated savolitinib and durvalumab in treatment-naïve or pretreated PRC. End points included overall response rate (ORR), progression-free survival (PFS), and overall survival (OS). FoundationOne CDx assessed DNA alterations and MET/PD-L1 or MET/tumor mutational burden (TMB) copositivity. ctDNA collected at baseline and on treatment was correlated with outcomes.

RESULTS: At 41-months median follow-up, ORR was 34% (95% CI, 20.0 to 51.0) in the intention-to-treat (ITT) population (N = 41) and 53% (95% CI, 28.0 to 77.0) in MET-driven patients (n = 17). Median PFS was 6.5 (95% CI, 2.7 to 12.0) versus 13.9 months (95% CI, 2.9 to 23.8), and OS was 18.3 (95% CI, 7.3 to 30.7) versus 27.4 months (95% CI, 9.3 to 37.4), in the ITT population and the MET-driven population, respectively. PD-L1 (66% positive) and TMB (median 2.5 mut/Mb) status did not correlate with response. Baseline ctDNA positivity (10/21) correlated with shorter OS (median 7.3 v 33.3 months), while ctDNA clearance and mean variant allele frequency reduction correlated with improved OS (median 31.3 v 7.2 and 31.3 v 15.5 months, respectively).

CONCLUSION: Savolitinib plus durvalumab shows OS in MET-driven PRC, supporting the ongoing SAMETA RIII trial (ClinicalTrials.gov identifier: NCT05043090). ctDNA may be a useful predictive biomarker.

</details>


### [13] [Biomimetic nanodecoys remodel the mechano-immune microenvironment to potentiate checkpoint blockade in colorectal cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42002768/)
*Nannan Wang,Ke Wang,Yuepeng Cao,Guoqing Su,Jinghang Qian,Jingru Shen,Jiayuan Jiang,Zian Chen,Xing Huang,Chengshuai Si,Jun Bao,Lei Ding,Liu Yang*

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种仿生纳米平台（MDPAs），通过抑制Integrin-FAK-YAP机械转导轴来软化结直肠癌的细胞外基质，从而促进细胞毒性T细胞浸润，并与PD-L1抗体协同增强抗肿瘤免疫，有效克服免疫治疗耐药性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌（CRC）中致密且坚硬的细胞外基质（ECM）构成了物理屏障，阻碍了免疫细胞的浸润，限制了免疫检查点抑制剂（ICIs）的临床疗效。为解决这种由机械微环境引起的免疫治疗耐药性问题，亟需开发能够调节肿瘤力学特性并增强免疫浸润的新策略。

Method: 构建了一种包含负载Defactinib的介孔二氧化硅纳米颗粒并包裹PD-L1抗体修饰血小板膜的仿生纳米平台（MDPAs）。利用血小板模拟粘附实现肿瘤蓄积，通过抑制Integrin-FAK-YAP机械转导轴来调节细胞骨架张力。结合单细胞RNA测序技术分析免疫-上皮-基质间的相互作用，并在原位瘤、肝转移及术后复发模型中评估其疗效和生物安全性。

Result: MDPAs导致YAP胞质隔离并减弱细胞骨架张力，缓解了机械微环境的抑制作用，显著增强了细胞毒性T细胞的浸润。单细胞测序显示，MDPAs重塑了细胞间通讯，驱动肿瘤细胞从间充质样表型向具有免疫易感性的抗原呈递状态转变。在多种小鼠模型中，MDPAs治疗诱导了肿瘤消退，延长了生存期，并显示出良好的生物安全性。

Conclusion: 调节肿瘤力学微环境是一种有效的策略，能够使难治性结直肠癌对免疫治疗增敏。MDPAs通过机械调节与免疫检查点阻断的协同作用，为克服CRC免疫治疗耐药提供了新的治疗思路。

Abstract: The clinical efficacy of immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) in colorectal cancer (CRC) is restricted by a dense, rigid extracellular matrix (ECM) that acts as a physical barrier to immune infiltration. To address this resistance, a biomimetic nanoplatform (MDPAs) was developed, comprising Defactinib-loaded mesoporous silica nanoparticles encapsulated in PD-L1 antibody-conjugated platelet membranes. Leveraging platelet-mimetic adhesion for tumor accumulation, MDPAs were shown to inhibit the Integrin-FAK-YAP mechanotransduction axis, leading to YAP cytoplasmic sequestration and attenuated cytoskeletal tension. This alleviation of the mechanical niche is associated with enhanced cytotoxic T cell infiltration, which synergizes with co-delivered PD-L1 antibodies to activate antitumor immunity. Single-cell RNA sequencing reveals that MDPAs remodel immune-epithelial-stromal crosstalk, driving tumor cells from a mesenchymal-like phenotype toward an immunologically susceptible, antigen-presenting state. In orthotopic, liver metastasis, and post-surgical recurrence models, MDPA treatment elicited tumor regression and extended survival with a favorable biosafety profile. These findings suggest that modulating tumor mechanics may represent an effective strategy to sensitize refractory CRC to immunotherapy.

</details>

TL;DR: 本研究开发了ICD相关风险特征（ICDRS）以预测肝细胞癌（HCC）免疫治疗反应，并发现舒尼替尼可通过eIF2α磷酸化诱导免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD），与抗PD-L1疗法协同增强抗肿瘤免疫应答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝细胞癌（HCC）因免疫抑制性肿瘤微环境导致预后不良，亟需通过诱导免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD）重塑微环境以改善免疫治疗效果。

Method: 1. 构建ICD相关风险特征（ICDRS）对患者分层及预测免疫治疗反应；2. 通过数据库筛选重用舒尼替尼作为ICD诱导剂；3. 体外检测ICD标志物（CRT暴露、ATP释放、HMGB1分泌）及树突状细胞（DC）成熟；4. 体内评估舒尼替尼预处理细胞的疫苗保护作用及与抗PD-L1的协同效应；5. 机制研究验证eIF2α磷酸化通路（使用抑制剂ISRIB逆转）。

Result: 1. 舒尼替尼有效诱导HCC细胞发生ICD并抑制类器官生长；2. 舒尼替尼预处理促进DC成熟，并在小鼠模型中产生保护性免疫记忆；3. 机制上通过eIF2α磷酸化介导ICD；4. 舒尼替尼与抗PD-L1联用显著增加细胞毒性T细胞浸润，减少免疫抑制细胞，实现强效肿瘤控制。

Conclusion: 舒尼替尼作为ICD诱导剂与免疫检查点抑制剂具有协同作用，结合ICDRS风险特征，为个性化增强HCC免疫治疗疗效提供了临床可行的策略。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prognosis remains poor due to its immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment. Inducing immunogenic cell death (ICD) represents a promising strategy to remodel this environment. Herein, we developed an ICD-related risk signature (ICDRS) to stratify patients and predict immunotherapy response. Through integrated database screening, sunitinib was repurposed as an ICD inducer. It elicited hallmark features of ICD, including calreticulin exposure, ATP release, and HMGB1 secretion, and effectively suppressed organoid growth. Sunitinib-pretreated HCC cells promoted dendritic cell (DC) maturation in vitro, and these pretreated cells protected mice from tumor rechallenge as a vaccine. Mechanistically, sunitinib induced ICD via eIF2α phosphorylation, as confirmed by reversal with the inhibitor ISRIB. Moreover, sunitinib synergized with anti-PD-L1 therapy by enhancing DC activation and increasing intratumoral cytotoxic T cells, while reducing immunosuppressive cell populations, resulting in potent tumor control. Together, our work provides a clinically actionable strategy to personalize and enhance immunotherapy outcomes in HCC.

</details>


### [15] [Infusion-Related Reactions from Immune Checkpoint Inhibitors in Solid Tumors: A Proportional and Network Meta-Analysis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41996022/)
*Yu Fujiwara,Toshiaki Takahashi,Kazuya Tsuchiya,Mako Koseki,Anneliese Markus,Evelyn Elias,Yoshito Nishimura,Igor Puzanov*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过网状荟萃分析比较了不同免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）的输液相关反应（IRR）发生率。结果显示，Avelumab的IRR风险最高，其次是Atezolizumab和双药联合ICI疗法；CTLA-4单药抑制剂风险最低。该结果有助于临床评估不同ICI方案的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有报道指出高达20%的免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）给药会出现输液相关反应（IRR），但不同ICI药物之间的IRR发生率存在差异。目前缺乏对各特定ICI疗法之间IRR发生率的系统性比较数据，因此需要明确不同ICI方案的具体风险特征，以指导临床治疗选择及联合用药时的风险评估。

Method: 研究者在PubMed/MEDLINE、Embase和Web of Science数据库中检索评估实体瘤中ICI（包括CTLA-4、PD-1、PD-L1和LAG-3抑制剂）的三期随机对照试验（RCT）。采用随机效应模型进行网状荟萃分析，比较双药ICI、ICI单药与安慰剂/观察组之间IRR的优势比（OR）。同时，进行比例荟萃分析以汇总ICI单药和双药治疗的IRR发生率。研究已在PROSPERO注册（CRD42023433659）。

Result: 共纳入61项三期RCT中的40,872名患者。比例荟萃分析显示，治疗相关IRR的汇总发生率为：PD-1抑制剂2.31%，PD-L1抑制剂8.27%（其中Avelumab为13.53%，非Avelumab为1.79%），PD-1联合LAG-3抑制剂5.70%，PD-1联合CTLA-4抑制剂5.55%，CTLA-4抑制剂1.79%。网状荟萃分析（包含12项RCT）表明，与安慰剂/观察组相比，Avelumab（OR 79.26）和Atezolizumab（OR 43.99）的治疗相关IRR风险显著更高。网络排名显示，Avelumab、Atezolizumab以及Relatlimab联合Nivolumab的IRR风险最大。

Conclusion: Avelumab具有最高的IRR风险，其次是Atezolizumab和双药联合ICI疗法。这项比较研究揭示了不同ICI方案IRR发生率的差异，为评估特别是与其他药物联合使用时哪种全身疗法导致IRR提供了重要依据。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Infusion-related reactions (IRRs) to immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) have been reported in up to 20% of administrations, but the incidence varies among ICIs.

OBJECTIVE: We aimed to report and compare the incidence of IRRs among each ICI therapy.

PATIENTS AND METHODS: We searched PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, and Web of Science to identify phase 3 randomized controlled trials (RCTs) evaluating ICIs (CTLA-4, PD-1, PD-L1, and LAG-3 inhibitors) in solid tumors. We performed a random-effects model network meta-analysis to compare the odds ratios (ORs) of IRRs by using RCTs comparing dual ICIs, ICI monotherapy, and placebo/observation. A proportional meta-analysis was also performed to pool the incidence of IRRs of ICI monotherapy and dual ICIs. The study was registered in the International Prospective Register of Systematic Reviews (PROSPERO) registry (CRD42023433659).

RESULTS: Overall, 40,872 patients from 61 phase 3 RCTs were included in the analysis. Proportional meta-analyses revealed that the pooled incidence of treatment-related IRRs was 2.31% for PD-1 inhibitors, 8.27% for PD-L1 inhibitors (avelumab: 13.53%, non-avelumab: 1.79%), 5.70% for PD-1 plus LAG-3 inhibitors, 5.55% for PD-1 plus CTLA-4 inhibitors, and 1.79% for CTLA-4 inhibitors. A network meta-analysis of treatment-related IRRs, including 12 RCTs, showed that avelumab (OR 79.26, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 4.84-1297.49) and atezolizumab (OR 43.99, 95% CI 2.65-729.89) had a significantly higher risk of treatment-related IRRs compared with placebo/observation. Network ranking revealed that avelumab, atezolizumab, and relatlimab plus nivolumab had the greatest risk of treatment-related IRRs.

CONCLUSIONS: Avelumab has the highest risk of IRRs followed by atezolizumab and dual ICIs. This comparative study provides insight into the incidence of IRRs with ICI regimens. These results are useful in assessing which systemic therapies are responsible for IRRs, particularly when ICIs are combined with other agents.

</details>


### [16] [Radiotherapy and DNA damage response inhibitors modestly sensitize HNSCC to NK cell killing, with ATM inhibition more effective than ATR inhibition.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41995841/)
*Leonie Steinsdörfer,Lilli Zülch,Anna Schäfer,Benjamin Frey,Stefanie Corradini,Rainer Fietkau,Udo S Gaipl,Tina Jost*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了放疗（RT）联合ATM或ATR抑制剂对HPV阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌（HNSCC）自然杀伤（NK）细胞介导杀伤作用的影响。结果显示，ATM抑制剂（而非ATR抑制剂）联合放疗能显著增强特定HPV阴性肿瘤细胞系（如HSC4）的NK细胞杀伤效果，而PD-L1抑制剂无显著增效，NKG2A抑制剂仅有轻微增效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPV阴性HNSCC因放射敏感性较低而治疗困难。靶向DNA损伤反应（DDR）激酶可能提高肿瘤对放疗的敏感性。尽管已知放疗会改变HNSCC的免疫表型，但其对先天免疫反应（特别是NK细胞）的具体影响尚不明确，因此需要探究RT联合DDR抑制剂如何影响NK细胞的杀伤和激活。

Method: 使用HPV阴性（HSC4, Cal33）和HPV阳性（UM-SCC-47, UD-SCC‑2）肿瘤细胞系，分别给予DDR抑制剂（ATR抑制剂VE-822，ATM抑制剂AZD0156）及大分割放疗（2 × 5 Gy）处理。随后与原代人NK细胞共培养，通过流式细胞术测量24小时后的肿瘤细胞死亡情况。同时检测NK细胞激活标志物及共培养上清液，并评估免疫检查点抑制剂（durvalumab, monalizumab）的附加效应。

Result: 1. HPV阴性HSC4细胞经ATM抑制剂或“放疗+ATM抑制剂”预处理后，NK细胞介导的杀伤作用显著增加。
2. 在ATR抑制剂处理的HPV阴性HNSCC共培养体系中，主要观察到NK细胞颗粒酶分泌减少。
3. PD-L1抑制剂（durvalumab）未显著增强NK细胞诱导的肿瘤杀伤。
4. NKG2A抑制剂（monalizumab）仅带来轻微的增加效果。

Conclusion: 总体而言，NK细胞对经放疗或“放疗+DDR抑制剂”预处理的HNSCC细胞的杀伤贡献有限。然而，特定亚群患者（如HSC4模型所代表的类型）可能从放疗联合ATM抑制剂（而非ATR抑制剂）的治疗策略中获益，以增强NK细胞介导的肿瘤杀伤。

Abstract: PURPOSE: Human papillomavirus (HPV)-negative head and neck squamous cell carcinomas (HNSCC) remain challenging due to their reduced radiosensitivity. Targeting the DNA damage response (DDR) with kinase inhibitors may enhance tumor susceptibility to radiotherapy (RT). While RT is known to alter the immune phenotype of HNSCC cells, its impact on innate immune responses, particularly on natural killer (NK) cells, remains unclear. We therefore investigated how RT combined with ATM or ATR inhibition-key DDR kinases-affects NK cell-mediated killing and activation.

METHODS: The tumor cell lines HSC4 and Cal33 (both HPV-negative) and UM-SCC-47 and UD-SCC‑2 (both HPV-positive) were treated with the DDR inhibitors VE-822 (ATRi) and AZD0156 (ATMi) and hypofractionated RT (2 × 5 Gy). After co-cultivation with primary human NK cells, tumor cell death was measured using flow cytometry after 24 h. Further, NK cell activation markers and the supernatant of tumor cell-NK cell co-cultures were measured and the effect of immune checkpoint inhibitors (durvalumab, monalizumab) was additionally investigated.

RESULTS: Particularly the HPV-negative HSC4 cells pretreated with either ATMi or RT + ATMi showed significantly increased killing by NK cells. Reduced secretion of granulysin by NK cells was mainly found in the co-cultures of HPV-negative HNSCC treated with ATRi. Treatment with the immune checkpoint inhibitor durvalumab (anti-PD-L1) did not result in significant augmentation of NK cell-induced tumor cell killing in this setting. Conversely, treatment with monalizumab (anti-NKG2A) resulted in a modest increase.

CONCLUSION: Natural killer cells showed only a limited contribution to the killing of HNSCC cells pretreated with RT or RT + DDRi. However, a subset of patients with head and neck tumors-such as those represented by the HSC4 model-might still benefit from combining RT with ATMi rather than ATRi to enhance NK cell-mediated tumor killing.

</details>


### [17] [Extracellular Vesicle-Transferred ATP-Citrate Lyase Induces Monocyte Differentiation Toward Tumor-Associated Macrophages and Fuels Hepatocellular Carcinoma Progression.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41995119/)
*Zhijun Liu,Haihong Lai,Qing Yang,Chaoyue Zhang,Bin Jia,Qiyi Chen,Yuling Deng,Fen Cao,Yuyu You,Zhe Li,Dongming Kuang,Bo Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）是实体瘤免疫抑制微环境的主要贡献者，但调控TAM分化及其免疫抑制活性的具体环境信号和分子机制尚不完全清楚，特别是在肝细胞癌（HCC）中。

Method: 1. 机制探索：分析HCC来源的EVs成分及其对单核细胞摄取、分化和代谢的影响，重点研究ACLY酶、棕榈酸生物合成及免疫检查点蛋白的S-棕榈酰化修饰。

3. 干预实验：分别装载ACLY蛋白或ACLY抑制剂（SB204990）的CD81装饰LVs，评估其对TAM表型、HCC进展及免疫治疗联合效果的影响。

Result: 1. HCC细胞分泌的EVs优先被单核细胞摄取，其中包裹的ACLY促进棕榈酸生物合成，增强多种免疫检查点蛋白的S-棕榈酰化和稳定性，从而诱导具有免疫抑制特征的TAM分化。
2. 负载ACLY的CD81装饰LVs足以诱导免疫抑制性TAM并促进HCC进展。
3. 负载ACLY抑制剂SB204990的CD81装饰LVs显著降低TAM介导的免疫抑制活性，抑制HCC进展。
4. 靶向EV转移的TAM特异性ACLY，尤其是与抗PD-1/PD-L1抗体联用时，能显著提高免疫治疗效果且无明显副作用。

Conclusion: 揭示了HCC通过EVs传递ACLY重塑免疫微环境的新机制，证明了靶向EV-ACLY轴是一种增强肝癌免疫治疗疗效且有前景的策略，为克服免疫治疗耐药提供了新靶点。

Abstract: Tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) arise from monocytes and represent major contributors to the immunosuppressive microenvironment of solid tumors. However, the environmental cues that govern TAM differentiation and immunosuppressive activity remain incompletely understood. Here we demonstrate that hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) cells secrete extracellular vesicles (EVs) that are preferentially taken up by monocytes, inducing their differentiation to TAMs characterized by a distinct immune-inhibitory signature. Mechanistically, HCC-derived EVs encapsulate the lipogenic enzyme ATP-citrate lyase (ACLY), promote palmitate biosynthesis in targeted monocytes, thereby enhancing the S-palmitoylation and stability of multiple immune checkpoint proteins. To validate this, we synthesized liposomal vesicles (LVs) decorated with an EV-marker protein CD81, which mimicked the targeting specificity of endogenous EVs for monocytes and differentiated macrophages. When loaded with ACLY proteins as interior cargo, these LVs were sufficient to induce immunosuppressive TAMs and promote HCC progression. Conversely, CD81-decorated LVs encapsulating the ACLY inhibitor SB204990 markedly reduced the TAM-mediated immunosuppressive activity, leading to restrained HCC progression. Importantly, we further demonstrated that targeting EV-transferred, TAM-specific ACLY represents a promising strategy to enhance immunotherapeutic efficacy without notable side effects, particularly when combined with anti-PD-1/PD-L1 antibodies for HCC treatment.

</details>


### [18] [FDG-PET/CT based small volume accelerated immuno chemoradiotherapy in locally advanced NSCLC (PACCELIO) - a randomized, open-label, multicenter phase II trial protocol.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41992146/)
*Rami El Shafie,Jonas Willmann,Eleni Gkika,Tanja Schimek-Jasch,Matthias Miederer,Farastuk Bozorgmehr,Frank Griesinger,Farkhad Manapov,Petros Christopoulos,Thomas Hehr,Markus Hecht,Rebecca Bütof,Martin Stuschke,Matthias Guckenberger,Gerald Schmid-Bindert,Jan Meiners,Sascha Herzer,Sonja Hartmann,Joana Lamché,Stefan Rieken,Ursula Nestle*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: PACCELIO是一项针对不可切除的III期非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）的多中心、随机、二期临床试验。该研究旨在比较基于FDG-PET/CT的体积缩减、低分割加速放化疗联合度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗，与标准体积、常规分割放化疗联合度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗的安全性和有效性，主要目标是提高放化疗完成率并顺利过渡到免疫巩固治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前不可切除的III期NSCLC标准治疗为放化疗（CRT）后接续PD-L1靶向免疫检查点抑制剂（IO）。然而，传统治疗方案可能存在毒性较大、治疗时间长等问题。整合FDG-PET/CT进行放疗计划可减少毒性并改善CRT结果，而低分割加速CRT能缩短治疗时间、提高依从性，从而使更多患者有资格接受巩固性免疫治疗。因此，需要验证这种综合策略是否能优化治疗流程并改善临床结局。

Method: 这是一项在德国、奥地利和瑞士进行的跨国、多中心、随机、二期试验（PACCELIO）。计划招募110名患者，按1:1比例随机分组，并按年龄、NSCLC分期和中心进行分层。实验组接受基于FDG-PET/CT的体积缩减、低分割加速CRT联合度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗；对照组接受标准体积、常规分割CRT联合度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗。主要终点使用Fisher精确检验分析，次要终点（如生存率、安全性等）使用Kaplan-Meier法分析。

Result: 由于该试验于2024年7月开始招募，目前仍在进行中，因此摘要中未提供具体的临床数据结果。研究设计预设的主要结果是评估两种方案在“完成CRT并成功过渡到巩固免疫治疗”这一指标上的差异。

Conclusion: PACCELIO试验通过结合基于FDG-PET/CT的放疗体积缩减、低分割加速CRT和度伐利尤单抗免疫治疗，旨在解决III期NSCLC的临床需求。该研究预期能提高治疗完成率和依从性，增强肿瘤控制并降低毒性。其结果将为这种联合治疗模式的有效性和安全性提供关键见解，并可能影响未来III期NSCLC的治疗标准。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Standard treatment for unresectable stage III non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) involves chemoradiation (CRT) followed by PD-L1 targeting immune checkpoint inhibition (IO). Integrating 18 F-fluorodeoxyglucose-positron emission tomography (FDG-PET)/CT into radiotherapy planning reduces toxicity, improves CRT outcomes, and potentially enhances immunotherapy response. Hypofractionated, accelerated CRT shortens treatment time, improves compliance, and may increase CRT completion rates, qualifying more patients for consolidative IO.

METHODS/DESIGN: PACCELIO is a multinational, multicenter, randomized, phase II trial comparing the safety and efficacy of FDG-PET/CT based, volume-reduced, hypofractionated CRT with consolidation durvalumab to standard-volume, conventionally fractionated CRT with consolidation durvalumab in inoperable stage III NSCLC. One hundred and ten patients will be enrolled in Germany, Austria, and Switzerland, randomized 1:1, stratified by age, NSCLC stage, and site. The primary objective is to increase completion of CRT with successful transition to consolidation immunotherapy. Secondary outcomes include locoregional and distant tumor control rates, overall survival, safety, and health-related quality of life (HR-QoL). Exploratory outcomes involve prognostic immunomarkers and PET parameters. The primary endpoint of treatment completion will be analyzed using Fisher's Exact test, with safety and efficacy outcomes analyzed via Kaplan-Meier.

DISCUSSION: The PACCELIO trial addresses a clinical need in stage III NSCLC by combining FDG-PET/CT based RT volume reduction with hypofractionated accelerated CRT and durvalumab immunotherapy. This approach aims to improve treatment completion, compliance, tumor control, and reduce toxicity. The trial will provide insights into the efficacy and safety of this combined treatment, potentially influencing future standards in stage III NSCLC treatment. Recruitment began in July 2024, with ongoing enrollment.

TRIAL REGISTRATION: Clinicaltrials.gov identifier: NCT06102057 (Date of initial registration: 26th October 2023), https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06102057.

SUPPLEMENTARY INFORMATION: The online version contains supplementary material available at 10.1186/s12885-026-15901-x.

</details>


### [19] [AK5 suppresses breast cancer progression and modulates anti-PD-L1 efficacy via the miR-182-5p/PD-L1 axis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41907408/)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腺苷酸激酶5（AK5）是一种表征不足的代谢酶，其在乳腺癌中的具体功能及临床意义尚不明确。鉴于单细胞转录组学显示AK5在恶性上皮细胞中富集且低表达与患者预后不良相关，研究旨在阐明AK5在乳腺癌进展中的作用机制及其治疗潜力。

Method: 结合单细胞转录组学分析、体外细胞功能实验（过表达与敲低模型检测增殖、迁移和侵袭）、分子机制探究（激酶活性依赖性对miR-182-5p成熟及下游靶点的调控）以及体内动物模型实验（评估肿瘤生长及对免疫治疗的响应）。

Result: 1. AK5在乳腺癌中发挥抑癌作用：过表达抑制而敲低促进癌细胞的增殖、迁移和侵袭。
2. 机制明确：AK5依赖其激酶活性，转录后抑制miR-182-5p的成熟，从而解除对PD-L1表达的抑制（即上调PD-L1）。
3. 治疗协同效应：体内实验证明，AK5过表达可减弱肿瘤生长，并与抗PD-L1疗法产生协同抗肿瘤效果。

Conclusion: AK5被定义为一种新型乳腺癌抑癌因子，揭示了其通过调节miR-182-5p/PD-L1轴影响免疫检查点的非经典激酶依赖性功能。AK5有望成为增强乳腺癌免疫治疗敏感性的潜在治疗靶点。

Abstract: Adenylate kinase 5 (AK5) is a poorly characterized metabolic enzyme with unknown roles in breast cancer. Single-cell transcriptomics revealed AK5 enrichment in malignant epithelial cells, and its low expression correlated with poor patient prognosis, suggesting tumor-suppressive functions. Functionally, AK5 overexpression inhibited, while its knockdown promoted, breast cancer cell proliferation, migration, and invasion. Mechanistically, AK5, dependent on its kinase activity, post-transcriptionally suppressed miR-182-5p maturation, thereby de-repressing -L1 expression. In vivo experiments have proved that AK5 overexpression attenuated tumor growth and synergized with anti-PD-L1 therapy. Our work defines AK5 as a novel tumor suppressor, unveils a kinase-dependent non-canonical role in regulating an immune checkpoint via miR-182-5p, and nominates it as a potential therapeutic target to sensitize tumors to immunotherapy.

</details>


### [20] [Tolerance to extracellular acidic pH facilitates tumor plasticity.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41997144/)
*Manami Hasegawa,Bo Xu,Keisuke Maeda,Motoaki Seki,FeiFei Cai,Runmei Cui,Ritsuko Ando,Suzuka Nakagawa,Ayana Sakamoto,Cayla Boycott,Hiroyuki Yatabe,Miyuki Nishida,Ken Matsumoto,Chisato Iwabuchi-Yoshida,Sho Aki,Kazuyuki Yamagata,Rika Tsuchida,Mami Takahashi,Futoshi Kuribayashi,Hiroyasu Kidoya,Hiroshi Hirata,Shingo Matsumoto,Shinsuke Sando,Hideyuki Yanai,Nozomu Yachie,Tsuyoshi Osawa*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 研究发现中度酸性环境（pH 6.8）通过激活FAM129C抑制坏死性凋亡，促进癌细胞存活和肿瘤起始；FAM129C下调PIGR表达，影响免疫浸润，抗PD-L1联合补体抑制剂在PIGR高表达肿瘤中显示协同抗肿瘤效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌细胞在有氧条件下仍维持糖酵解，导致微环境酸化，但癌细胞如何在酸性应激下存活尚不清楚。

Method: 通过不同pH值处理细胞观察死亡模式，利用RNA测序分析悬浮细胞在pH 6.8下的基因表达变化，进行全基因组CRISPR-Cas9敲除筛选鉴定酸耐受调节因子，并通过异种移植模型验证FAM129C与PIGR轴对肿瘤生长及免疫治疗反应的影响。

Result: 严重酸化（pH 5.6）诱导坏死性凋亡，而中度酸化（pH 6.8）阻止死亡并支持无锚定生存；FAM129C被鉴定为酸耐受的关键调节因子，其过表达降低PIGR水平；抗PD-L1与补体抑制剂联用在PIGR过表达肿瘤中具有协同疗效。

Conclusion: 酸性应激通过特定通路使癌细胞逃避坏死性凋亡并增强肿瘤可塑性，靶向pH依赖性细胞死亡通路为癌症治疗提供了新策略。

Abstract: Cancer cells sustain glycolysis despite oxygen availability, creating an acidic microenvironment via proton and lactate export, but how they survive acid stress is unclear. We show that severe acidification (pH 5.6) induces necroptosis, whereas moderate acidity (pH 6.8) prevents death and enables anchorage-independent survival and tumor initiation. RNA sequencing of suspended cells at pH 6.8 revealed activation of respiratory chain complex and complement pathways, consistent with adaptation to this pH. A genome-wide CRISPR-Cas9 knockout screen in PANC1 cells under chronic acidity identified FAM129C as a regulator of acid tolerance and survival. In xenografts, FAM129C overexpression reduced PIGR expression, implicating this axis in tumor growth and immune infiltration. Anti-PD-L1 plus a complement inhibitor showed synergistic anti-tumor activity in PIGR-overexpressing tumors. Thus, acidic stress engages a pathway that allows cancer cells to evade necroptosis and promote tumor plasticity, providing potential avenues for therapeutic intervention targeting pH-dependent cell-death pathways.

</details>


### [21] [Combined administration of interleukin-2 and 18 with anti-PD-L1 antibody induces CCL5-positive CD8 T cells to suppress liver tumors.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42022218/)
*Masamichi Kimura,Kenzaburo Yamaji,Kenichi Harada,Hideya Kawaji,Jun Imamura,Haruki Okamura,Yoshimasa Tanaka,Michinori Kohara,Kiminori Kimura*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究证实，重组白细胞介素-2 (rIL-2)、重组白细胞介素-18 (rIL-18) 与抗PD-L1抗体 (αPD-L1 Ab) 的三联疗法能显著缩小肝细胞癌 (HCC) 肿瘤。其机制是通过诱导肿瘤内CD8+ T细胞分泌CCL5，促进CD8+ T细胞向肝脏募集和浸润，从而增强抗肿瘤效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管免疫检查点抑制剂在癌症免疫治疗中取得进展，但在肝细胞癌 (HCC) 等实体瘤中疗效有限，主要原因是效应细胞（如细胞毒性CD8+ T细胞）向肿瘤组织的浸润不足。因此，亟需探索能够改善T细胞浸润的新型免疫治疗策略。

Method: 使用自发性老年相关肝癌模型—— multidrug resistance gene 2 (Mdr2) 缺陷小鼠。实验组分别接受 rIL-2、rIL-18 和 αPD-L1 Ab 的单药、双药或三联治疗。通过计算机断层扫描 (CT) 和血清甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 水平评估抗肿瘤效果。利用空间基因表达谱分析分子机制，并通过 CD8 中和抗体及 CCL5 中和抗体进行功能验证。

Result: 1. 仅 rIL-2 + rIL-18 + αPD-L1 Ab 三联治疗组观察到显著的肿瘤缩小，双药组合无效。
2. 联合使用抗CD8中和抗体可消除抗肿瘤效果，证明该疗效依赖于CD8+ T细胞。
3. 空间基因表达谱显示，治疗后肿瘤内CD8+效应T细胞表达并分泌CCL5，促进CD8+ T细胞向肝脏募集。
4. 预处理CCL5中和抗体抑制了CD8+细胞向肿瘤的浸润，并消除了抗肿瘤效应。

Conclusion: rIL-2、rIL-18 与 αPD-L1 Ab 的三联疗法通过促进CD8+ T细胞在肝脏中的浸润和维持，展现出作为肝细胞癌新型免疫疗法的巨大潜力。其关键机制在于诱导肿瘤内CD8+ T细胞分泌CCL5，进而增强T细胞的募集和抗肿瘤功效。

Abstract: Remarkable progress has been made in cancer immunotherapy, partly because of the development of immune checkpoint inhibitors. However, their efficacy varies across cancer types, with limited response observed in solid tumors, such as hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). The primary cause of this low efficacy is insufficient infiltration of effector cells, such as cytotoxic CD8+ T cells, into the tumor tissue. This study investigated whether recombinant interleukin (rIL)-2, rIL-18, and antiprogrammed cell death-ligand 1 (αPD-L1) antibody (Ab) could serve as novel immunotherapies for HCC. Multidrug resistance gene 2-deficient mice, a spontaneously occurring liver cancer model associated with aging, were administered rIL-2, rIL-18, and αPD-L1Ab. Antitumor effects were evaluated using computed tomography and serum alpha-fetoprotein levels. Significant tumor shrinkage was observed in the rIL-2+rIL-18+αPD-L1Ab group, but not in the rIL-2+αPD-L1Ab or rIL-18+αPD-L1Ab groups. Concurrent administration of αCD8-neutralizing antibody abolished the antitumor effect, indicating CD8+ T-cell dependency. Spatial gene expression profiling revealed that intratumoral CD8+ effector T cells express and secrete CCL5 after treatment, promoting CD8+ T-cell mobilization to the liver and enhancing antitumor efficacy. Pretreatment with a CCL5 neutralizing antibody suppressed CD8+ cell infiltration into the tumor, eliminating the antitumor effect. The triple combination therapy used in this study promotes the infiltration and maintenance of CD8+ T cells in the liver, suggesting a promising new immunotherapy for HCC.

</details>


### [22] [Navigating controversies in stage III NSCLC: a multidisciplinary case discussion on evolving treatment paradigms.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42000138/)
*Ashley Horne,Igor Gomez-Randulfe,Amy Ford,Richard Milton,Petra Jankowska,Emma O'Dowd,Alastair Greystoke,Corinne Faivre-Finn*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本文通过一例PD-L1高表达（100%）的cT3N2aM0 III期非小细胞肺癌病例，展示了新辅助化疗联合免疫治疗（卡铂+紫杉醇+纳武利尤单抗）如何实现肿瘤降期，将手术方案从高风险的全肺切除改为创伤较小的右上肺叶切除，并达到病理完全缓解。文章强调了多学科团队（MDT）在应对治疗范式转变中的关键作用，并指出当前在可操作性定义、治疗选择及生物标志物缺乏方面仍存在争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新辅助和围手术期化疗-免疫疗法及靶向治疗的进展，III期非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）的管理日益复杂。这种演变给多学科团队（MDT）在患者选择、治疗顺序、手术规划以及可操作性和可切除性定义方面带来了新挑战。本文旨在通过具体病例探讨这些临床困境及最佳实践。

Method: 采用病例报告结合专家讨论的方法。首先呈现一名69岁男性单站N2腺癌患者的诊疗过程，包括接受3周期新辅助治疗后影像学评估及手术方案调整。随后，基于2025年8月的学术网络研讨会，汇总呼吸内科、胸外科、内科及放射/临床肿瘤学专家关于该病例的关键讨论点，涵盖可切除性定义、系统治疗选择、手术风险及术后放疗角色等议题。

Result: 患者在接受新辅助治疗后，轴向肿瘤尺寸缩小60%，实现部分缓解。这一降期效果使得手术计划从原本因肿瘤靠近右主支气管而需进行的全肺切除，转变为范围更小的右上肺叶切除。最终病理检查显示患者达到病理完全反应（ypT0ypN0），显著降低了围手术期风险并保留了肺功能。

Conclusion: 新辅助和围手术期化疗-免疫疗法为减少手术范围和改善肿瘤学结果提供了机会，但该策略伴随风险且缺乏验证性的决策指导生物标志物。对于不可切除或无法手术的适合患者，同步放化疗（cCRT）后接度伐利尤单抗仍是标准治疗。未来进展依赖于临床试验、生物标志物开发以及真实世界数据和国家级审计的收集。MDT在解决当前争议和优化个体化治疗中起至关重要的作用。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: The management of stage III non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) has become increasingly complex, driven by advances in neoadjuvant and perioperative chemo-immunotherapy as well as targeted therapies. These evolving treatment paradigms have introduced new challenges for multidisciplinary teams (MDTs), regarding patient selection, treatment sequencing, surgical planning and definitions of operability and resectability.

CASE PRESENTATION: We present a case of a 69-year-old male with cT3N2aM0 (single-station N2) adenocarcinoma NSCLC with a programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) tumour proportion score of 100%. Following discussion in MDT, he received 3 cycles of neoadjuvant carboplatin, paclitaxel, and nivolumab. The initial surgical plan was for a pneumonectomy due to tumour proximity to the right main bronchus, a procedure associated with perioperative risk and long-term functional compromise. Restaging computed tomography (CT) scan demonstrated a partial response, with a 60% reduction in axial tumour dimensions. This downstaging facilitated a change in surgical plan from pneumonectomy to the less extensive right upper lobectomy, resulting in a pathological complete response (ypT0ypN0).

DISCUSSION: The case was discussed during an academic webinar in August 2025. Expert faculty from respiratory medicine, thoracic surgery and medical and radiation/clinical oncology highlighted key discussion points and challenges. This included: 1) evolving definitions of operability and resectability, 2) perioperative systemic therapy selection, 3) the risk of not proceeding to surgery, and 4) the role of postoperative radiotherapy (PORT) and salvage therapy in case of progression.

CONCLUSION: The case underscores current controversies in the management of stage III NSCLC and the critical role of the MDT. While neoadjuvant and perioperative chemo-immunotherapy offers an opportunity for less extensive surgical resection and improved oncological outcomes, this strategy is not without risks and validated biomarkers to guide decision making are lacking. Concurrent chemoradiotherapy (cCRT) followed by durvalumab remains the standard for fit patients with unresectable or inoperable stage III disease. Future progress depends on clinical trials, biomarker development, and real-world data collection and national audits.

</details>


### [23] [Phenethyl Isothiocyanate Suppresses Hepatocellular Carcinoma Progression and Immune Evasion via ZNF652 Regulation of PD-L1.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41983254/)
*Dongxue Ren,Jinyi Wu,Jiayu Zhu,Chunfeng Xie,Caiyun Zhong,Hui Cao,Hongyu Han,Jianyun Zhu*

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究发现植物化学物PEITC通过抑制转录因子ZNF652，下调PD-L1表达，从而增强肝细胞癌（HCC）对T细胞介导的细胞毒性的敏感性，并提升抗PD-L1免疫疗法的疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对肝细胞癌（HCC）中免疫检查点抑制剂（特别是PD-1/PD-L1阻断疗法）临床疗效有限、仅对部分患者有效的问题，寻求能够增敏免疫治疗的新策略及潜在药物。

Method: 1. 体外实验：评估PEITC对HCC细胞增殖、迁移及凋亡的影响；检测PEITC对PD-L1表达的调控作用及对T细胞介导细胞毒性的影响。
2. 体内实验：验证PEITC对HCC肿瘤发生的抑制作用。
3. 联合用药实验：评估PEITC与抗PD-L1抗体联合使用的治疗效果。
4. 机制研究：结合生物信息学分析与实验验证，鉴定ZNF652为PD-L1的上游转录调控因子；通过ZNF652过表达和敲低实验，验证其在PEITC调控PD-L1及免疫逃逸中的具体机制（包括ChIP等验证ZNF652与PD-L1启动子的结合）。

Result: 1. PEITC在体外抑制HCC细胞增殖和迁移，促进凋亡；在体内显著抑制肿瘤发生。
2. PEITC以浓度依赖方式下调PD-L1表达，增强HCC细胞对T细胞介导细胞毒性的敏感性。
3. PEITC与抗PD-L1抗体联用可显著提高免疫治疗疗效。
4. ZNF652在HCC中上调且与PD-L1表达正相关；ZNF652作为转录因子直接结合PD-L1启动子激活其转录。
5. ZNF652过表达可部分逆转PEITC对PD-L1的抑制及对T细胞毒性的增强作用，而敲低ZNF652则增强这些效应。

Conclusion: PEITC通过调节转录因子ZNF652来下调PD-L1表达，从而抑制HCC进展及免疫逃逸。这一机制揭示了PEITC作为辅助药物增强HCC免疫治疗效果的潜力，为克服当前免疫检查点抑制剂疗效局限提供了新的理论依据和治疗策略。

Abstract: The therapeutic efficacy of immune checkpoint inhibitors in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains limited, with programmed death receptor-1 (PD-1)/programmed cell death 1 ligand 1 (PD-L1) blockade effective in only some patients. Here, we report that phenethyl isothiocyanate (PEITC), a bioactive phytochemical, demonstrated promising anticancer potential in HCC by inhibiting cell proliferation and migration, while promoting apoptosis in vitro. We also observed that PEITC significantly suppressed tumorigenesis of HCC in vivo. Importantly, PEITC downregulated PD-L1 expression in a concentration-dependent manner and enhanced the sensitivity of HCC cells to T cell-mediated cytotoxicity. When combined with anti-PD-L1 antibody, PEITC improved the efficacy of immunotherapy. Additionally, bioinformatics and experimental validation revealed that zinc finger protein 652 (ZNF652), a transcription factor, was upregulated and positively correlated with PD-L1 expression in HCC. Mechanistically, ZNF652 bound to the promoter of PD-L1 to activate its transcription. ZNF652 overexpression partially reversed the suppressive effects of PEITC on PD-L1 expression and T cell cytotoxicity, while ZNF652 knockdown enhanced these effects in vitro. These findings indicated that PEITC suppresses HCC progression and immune evasion by downregulating PD-L1 via modulation of ZNF652, providing a basis for its potential use in HCC immunotherapy.

</details>


### [24] [Nerves Stimulate Cross-talk Between Gastric Cancer and Group 3 Innate Lymphoid Cells to Enhance Immunosuppression.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41534088/)
*Fangli Liao,Yanran Tong,Hua Sun,Sen Chen,Siyang Wen,Yan-E Du,Linshan Jiang,Tong Huang,Manran Liu,Weixian Chen,Liping Yang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究揭示迷走神经通过释放乙酰胆碱，诱导胃癌细胞分泌LysoPS，进而激活ILC3产生IL22，最终通过UPR途径上调PD-L1表达，促进免疫逃逸；靶向该轴可增强抗PD-L1免疫疗法疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免疫抑制性肿瘤微环境（TME）导致癌细胞逃避免疫治疗。神经网络是TME的重要组成部分，癌细胞、神经元和免疫细胞间的相互作用介导了免疫抑制。理解这种细胞间串扰机制有助于开发调节免疫的策略，以提高免疫治疗的效力。

Method: 研究聚焦于胃癌模型，分析迷走神经浸润对胃癌细胞与3型固有淋巴样细胞（ILC3）之间串扰的影响。具体机制涉及检测乙酰胆碱（ACh）对脂质酶ABHD16A表达的调控、LysoPS的分泌、ILC3通过GPR34-AKT-STAT3轴产生IL22，以及IL22触发未折叠蛋白反应（UPR）从而上调PD-L1的过程。同时评估了靶向ACh或阻断该串扰对抗PD-L1免疫疗法疗效的影响，并分析了胃癌患者血清中LysoPS和IL22的水平。

Result: 1. 迷走神经释放的ACh上调胃癌细胞中ABHD16A的表达，促进代谢物LysoPS的产生和分泌。
2. LysoPS促进TME中ILC3的增殖和活化，通过GPR34-AKT-STAT3轴产生细胞因子IL22。
3. IL22触发胃癌细胞的UPR，导致PD-L1表达增加，增强免疫耐药性。
4. 靶向ACh或胃癌细胞与ILC3之间的串扰显著提高了抗PD-L1免疫治疗的疗效。
5. 胃癌患者（尤其是伴有神经周围浸润者）血清中LysoPS和IL22水平升高。

Conclusion: 迷走神经衍生的乙酰胆碱调节胃癌细胞与ILC3之间的串扰，上调PD-L1并促进免疫抑制。这一发现揭示了胃癌检测和治疗的潜在诊断标志物（LysoPS, IL22）及治疗策略（靶向该神经-免疫轴），具有重要的临床意义。

Abstract: UNLABELLED: The immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME) enables cancer cells to evade clinical immunotherapies. Neural networks are vital components of the TME, and interactions among cancer cells, neuronal cells, and immune cells mediate immunosuppression. Hence, understanding the mechanisms of intercellular cross-talk could inform immunomodulatory approaches to enhance immunotherapy efficacy. In this study, we found that the vagus nerve regulated the cross-talk between gastric cancer cells and group 3 innate lymphoid cells (ILC3), boosting immune resistance in gastric cancer by enhancing programmed cell death ligand 1 (PD-L1) expression. Specifically, the infiltrated vagus nerve released acetylcholine (ACh) that elevated the expression of lipase ABHD16A in gastric cancer cells, facilitating the production and secretion of the metabolite lysophosphatidylserine (LysoPS) into the TME. LysoPS facilitated the proliferation and activation of ILC3s in the TME, resulting in the production of the cytokine IL22 via the GPR34-AKT-STAT3 axis. In turn, IL22 triggered the unfolded protein response (UPR) in gastric cancer cells, which led to an increase in PD-L1 expression that enhanced immune resistance. Importantly, targeting ACh or the cross-talk between gastric cancer cells and ILC3s significantly enhanced the efficacy of anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy. Serum levels of LysoPS and IL22 were elevated in patients with gastric cancer, particularly those with perineural invasion. Collectively, these findings provide valuable insights into the cross-talk among gastric cancer cells, nerve cells, and ILC3s that regulate immunosuppression and response to anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy, emphasizing the potential clinical significance of this axis for detecting and treating gastric cancer.

SIGNIFICANCE: Vagus nerve-derived acetylcholine regulates cross-talk between gastric cancer cells and innate lymphoid cells to upregulate PD-L1 and promote immunosuppression, revealing potential therapeutic and diagnostic strategies for gastric cancer patients.

</details>


### [25] [Multi-Omics Analysis Reveals m7G Methylation-Related Genes May Be Involved in TGF-β Signaling-Mediated Anti-PD-L1 Response in Bladder Cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42016541/)
*Hai-Qi Liang,Yu-Jian Li,Jia-Yin Yu,Qi-Zhou Mo,Qiu-Ju Wei,Yu-Qi Liang,Fa-Ye Wei,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Jia-Wen Zhao,Yu-Xian He,Xin-Dong Feng,Bin-Tong Yin,Xin-Wen Liang,Ren-Jun Nong,Yao-Yu Wang,Min Qin,Ji-Wen Cheng*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过多组学分析发现，膀胱癌中m7G甲基化相关基因NUDT10的高表达与免疫抑制微环境及抗PD-L1治疗耐药密切相关。NUDT10通过调控TGF-β信号通路激活因子LRRC32，促进癌细胞增殖和迁移，导致不良预后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免疫治疗耐药是膀胱癌治疗面临的主要挑战，目前N7-甲基鸟苷（m7G）甲基化在这一过程中的具体作用机制尚不明确。本研究旨在利用多组学方法探究m7G甲基化相关基因在膀胱癌免疫治疗耐药中的潜在角色。

Method: 研究整合了生物信息学分析与体外实验验证。利用TCGA、GEO和IMvigor210等公共数据库中的批量、单细胞及空间转录组数据，通过机器学习模型识别分子簇并筛选特征基因。进行基因本体论富集分析，并通过siRNA转染敲低膀胱癌细胞系中的特征基因表达。采用qRT-PCR、Western blot和免疫组化检测基因表达，利用CCK-8和伤口愈合实验评估特征基因对癌细胞增殖和迁移能力的影响。

Result: 鉴定出两个膀胱癌分子簇，其中C1簇表现为预后较差、m7GRGs高表达及免疫抑制微环境。NUDT10被确定为区分分子簇的关键预后基因。差异甲基化基因富集于TGF-β信号通路，且与抗PD-L1耐药相关。单细胞和空间转录组分析显示NUDT10与TGF-β激活因子LRRC32共表达。临床数据集证实NUDT10和LRRC32的高表达与抗PD-L1治疗反应不佳有关。体外实验表明，敲低NUDT10可抑制LRRC32表达，并降低癌细胞的增殖和迁移能力。

Conclusion: NUDT10是基于m7GRGs定义的膀胱癌分子簇中与不良预后相关的关键基因。NUDT10的表达可能通过调控TGF-β信号通路，导致晚期或转移性膀胱癌患者对抗PD-L1治疗产生耐药反应。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Immunotherapy resistance is a significant challenge in bladder cancer treatment. The role of N7-methylguanosine (m7G) methylation in this process remains unclear. This study aims to investigate the potential role of m7G methylation-related genes (m7GRGs) in immunotherapy resistance using a multi-omics approach.

METHODS: This study integrated bioinformatics analysis with in vitro experimental validation. We obtained bulk, single-cell, and spatial transcriptomics data from multiple bladder cancer cohorts in public databases, including TCGA, GEO and IMvigor210. Machine learning models were performed to identify molecular clusters and screen signature genes. Gene ontology analysis was used for gene function enrichment. siRNA transfection knocked down the expression of characteristic genes in bladder cancer cell lines to validate their roles in signaling regulation. Gene expression was assessed using qRT-PCR, Western blot, and immunohistochemistry. Additionally, CCK-8 and wound healing assays were performed to evaluate the effects of characteristic genes on the proliferation and migration of bladder cancer cells.

RESULTS: Two bladder cancer molecular clusters were identified, with the C1 cluster exhibiting worse prognosis, higher m7GRGs expression, and an immunosuppressive microenvironment. NUDT10 was a key prognostic gene distinguishing molecular clusters. A subset of differentially methylated genes correlated with anti-PD-L1 resistance were enriched in the TGF-β signaling pathway. Single-cell and spatial transcriptomics revealed that NUDT10 was co-expressed with TGF-β activator LRRC32. IMvigor210 and GSE176307 datasets found that expression of NUDT10 and LRRC32 was associated with poor response to anti-PD-L1 therapy. Knockdown of NUDT10 in bladder cancer cells inhibited the expression of LRRC32 and reduced the proliferation and migration abilities of the cancer cells.

CONCLUSION: This study identifies NUDT10 as a key gene associated with poor prognosis based on m7GRGs-defined molecular clusters in bladder cancer. NUDT10 expression may regulate TGF-β signaling and is associated with poor response to anti-PD-L1 therapy in advanced or metastatic bladder cancer.

</details>

### [27] [Cancer-Associated Fibroblasts Promote Tumor Immunosuppression in Hepatocellular Carcinoma via the NNMT-ANGPTL4 Axis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41980209/)
*Shounan Lu,Shanjia Ke,Hongjun Yu,Zhanzhi Meng,Miaoyu Bai,Yanan Xu,Hui Zhu,Jinwen Yang,Baolin Qian,Bing Yin,Chaoqun Wang,Zhigang Feng,Zhongyu Li,Yongzhi Zhou,Zihao Li,Xinglong Li,Yongliang Hua,Yao Fu,Wei Tang,Yaohua Wu,Yong Ma*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了肝癌中癌症相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）内的NNMT通过ANGPTL4介导的代谢-表观遗传级联反应，促进肿瘤免疫逃逸。靶向该轴可恢复CD8+ T细胞活性并增强抗PD-L1疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）在肝细胞癌（HCC）中驱动免疫抑制，但其具体的代谢调控机制尚不明确。研究旨在探究烟酰胺N-甲基转移酶（NNMT）在CAFs中的作用及其对肿瘤微环境的影响。

Method: 结合西方印迹、免疫荧光染色确认CAFs中NNMT的高表达；整合单细胞RNA测序（GSE149614）、患者来源类器官（PDOs）、成纤维细胞特异性NNMT敲除小鼠模型及代谢组学分析；利用PDX模型评估治疗潜力。

Result: CAFs中的NNMT结合EZH2并阻碍其核转位，降低ANGPTL4启动子处的H3K27me3富集，从而增加ANGPTL4分泌。分泌的ANGPTL4与HCC细胞中的GLUT1结合，激活有氧糖酵解并提高组蛋白H3K18la水平，进而上调PD-L1表达以促进免疫逃逸，同时促进血管生成。靶向NNMT-ANGPTL4轴可恢复CD8+ T细胞活性，并与抗PD-L1疗法产生协同作用。

Conclusion: 鉴定了CAFs中由NNMT-ANGPTL4驱动的代谢-表观遗传级联反应，该机制诱导PD-L1介导的免疫逃逸。靶向该通路为克服HCC患者免疫治疗耐药性提供了新的治疗策略。

Abstract: BACKGROUND & AIMS: Cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) drive immunosuppression in hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). However, their metabolic regulation remains poorly defined. We investigated the role of nicotinamide N-methyltransferase (NNMT) in CAFs.

APPROACH & RESULTS: High NNMT expression in CAF tissues was confirmed by western blotting and immunofluorescence staining. Primary CAFs from HCC patients, single-cell RNA-seq (GSE149614), patient-derived organoids (PDOs), and fibroblast-specific NNMT-knockout mice were integrated by metabolomic analyses. NNMT in CAFs binds EZH2 and impedes its nuclear translocation, thereby reducing H3K27me3 enrichment at the promoter of angiopoietin-like 4 (ANGPTL4) to increase ANGPTL4 secretion. Secreted ANGPTL4 engages GLUT1 in HCC cells, activating aerobic glycolysis and increasing histone H3K18la levels. This epigenetic reprogramming transcriptionally upregulates PD-L1 expression, thereby facilitating tumor immune evasion. Additionally, CAF-derived ANGPTL4 promotes angiogenesis in HCC. Therapeutically, targeting the NNMT-ANGPTL4 axis restored CD8+ T-cell activity and synergized with anti-PD-L1 therapy in both patient-derived xenografts (PDXs) and fibroblast-specific NNMT-knockout murine models.

CONCLUSION: We identified an NNMT-ANGPTL4-driven metabolic-epigenetic cascade in CAFs that induces PD-L1-mediated immune evasion, providing a therapeutic strategy to overcome resistance to immunotherapy in patients with HCC.

</details>


### [28] [Comparative pharmacovigilance signals for PD-1, PD-L1, CTLA-4, and LAG-3 immune checkpoint inhibitor-associated hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis in the FAERS database.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41973466/)
*Connor Frey*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模药物警戒分析证实，噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增生症（HLH）是免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）的显著类别毒性。所有主要ICI类别均与HLH相关，其中PD-L1抑制剂（特别是阿替利珠单抗）、CTLA-4抑制剂（伊匹木单抗）及PD-1抑制剂（帕博利珠单抗）显示出最强的安全信号，临床需对接受免疫治疗出现高炎症症状的患者保持高度警惕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLH是一种暴发性、高炎症综合征，日益被识别为罕见但具毁灭性的ICI毒性。由于缺乏强有力的流行病学数据，亟需通过大规模分析来表征不同ICI类别中HLH的报告模式，以填补这一知识空白。

Method: 利用FDA不良事件报告系统（FAERS），检索2003年第四季度至2025年第三季度期间与FDA批准的PD-1、PD-L1、CTLA-4和LAG-3抑制剂相关的HLH病例。采用不成比例分析（Disproportionality analysis）量化相对于完整数据库的安全信号。

Result: 所有主要ICI类别均与HLH相关。在PD-1抑制剂中，帕博利珠单抗病例数最多（n=120; ROR 9.51），cemiplimab的点估计值最高（ROR 10.56）。在PD-L1类中，阿替利珠单抗信号最强（n=76; ROR 14.48）。CTLA-4抑制剂伊匹木单抗显示显著不成比例（n=54; ROR 12.77），LAG-3抑制剂relatlimab也显示出可比信号（ROR 12.64）。

Conclusion: 分析确认HLH是免疫检查点阻断治疗的显著类别毒性，帕博利珠单抗、阿替利珠单抗和伊匹木单抗观察到强烈的安全信号。研究强调在免疫治疗期间，对于出现高炎症症状的患者，必须加强临床警惕并进行快速的HLH诊断评估。

Abstract: BackgroundHemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis (HLH) is a fulminant, hyperinflammatory syndrome increasingly recognized as a rare but devastating immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICI) toxicity. Given the lack of robust epidemiological data, a large-scale pharmacovigilance analysis was performed to characterize HLH reporting patterns across distinct ICI classes.MethodsThe FDA Adverse Events Reporting System (FAERS) was queried to identify HLH cases associated with FDA-approved PD-1, PD-L1, CTLA-4, and LAG-3 inhibitors from Q4 2003 through Q3 2025. Disproportionality analysis was conducted to quantify safety signals relative to the full database.ResultsAll major ICI classes were associated with HLH, with PD-L1 inhibitors (particularly atezolizumab) and CTLA-4 inhibitors showing the strongest signals; no single agent class appeared to be spared. Among PD-1 inhibitors, pembrolizumab exhibited the highest burden (n = 120; ROR 9.51, 95% CI 7.93-11.40), while cemiplimab demonstrated a high point estimate (ROR 10.56). In the PD-L1 class, atezolizumab showed the strongest signal among widely used agents (n = 76; ROR 14.48, 95% CI 11.54-18.18). The CTLA-4 inhibitor ipilimumab yielded substantial disproportionality (n = 54; ROR 12.77, 95% CI 9.76-16.70), and the LAG-3 inhibitor relatlimab showed a comparable signal (ROR 12.64) despite limited case numbers.ConclusionsThis analysis confirms HLH as a significant class-wide toxicity of immune checkpoint blockade, with robust safety signals observed for pembrolizumab, atezolizumab, and ipilimumab. These findings underscore the critical need for heightened clinical vigilance and rapid diagnostic evaluation for HLH in patients presenting with hyperinflammatory symptoms during immunotherapy.

</details>

*Camilla O Hoff,Simon Heeke,Yoshitsugu Mitani,Luana G de Sousa,Juliana M Siqueira,Kaiyi Li,Flavia Bonini,Diana Bell,Mario L Marques-Piubelli,Shiaw-Yih Lin,Jennifer A Wargo,Adel K El-Naggar,Daniel J McGrail,Renata Ferrarotto*

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究分析了阿昔替尼联合阿维鲁单抗治疗复发/转移性腺样囊性癌（ACC）的II期试验数据，发现ACC分为两种亚型（侵袭性ACC-I和惰性ACC-II），其无进展生存期（PFS）差异显著。研究鉴定出一个167基因免疫富集特征以及特定微生物（肠内大肠杆菌、肠道双歧杆菌和阿克曼氏菌）的存在可预测免疫联合治疗的临床获益，为ACC的精准治疗提供了生物标志物框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管阿昔替尼联合阿维鲁单抗已被NCCN指南推荐为ACC的首个免疫治疗方案，但尚缺乏预测该疗法临床获益的生物标志物。本研究旨在通过多组学分析，识别能够预测ACC患者对阿昔替尼联合阿维鲁单抗治疗反应的生物标志物，以实现患者分层和精准医疗。

Method: 纳入28名ACC患者，收集治疗前肿瘤样本进行全外显子组测序、转录组谱分析、成像质谱流式细胞术检测，并对肿瘤、漱口水和粪便样本进行16s rRNA基因测序以表征微生物组。使用Cox比例风险模型评估各因素与无进展生存期（PFS）的关联，并将ACC亚型（ACC-I vs. ACC-II）作为协变量纳入分析。

Result: 1. ACC分为两种蛋白基因组学截然不同的亚型：NOTCH1激活的侵袭性ACC-I和较惰性的ACC-II。ACC-I的中位PFS为1.8个月，而ACC-II为11.4个月（HR 0.14, P<0.0001）。
2. 临床获益与PD-L1表达、肿瘤突变负荷或复发性基因突变无关。
3. 较好的PFS与肿瘤内大肠杆菌以及肠道双歧杆菌和阿克曼氏菌的存在相关。
4. 鉴定出一个167基因的免疫富集特征，可预测阿昔替尼联合阿维鲁单抗的PFS获益；该特征也能预测伊匹木单抗联合纳武利尤单抗的获益，但不能预测瑞戈非尼单药治疗的获益，显示其对免疫联合疗法的特异性。

Conclusion: 阿昔替尼联合阿维鲁单抗的临床结果在ACC不同亚型间存在显著差异。鉴定的167基因免疫相关特征可预测ACC患者对免疫联合治疗的临床获益。这些发现为这种罕见恶性肿瘤的未来生物标志物驱动试验设计和患者分层策略提供了重要框架。

Abstract: PURPOSE: The phase II axitinib plus avelumab trial in recurrent/metastatic adenoid cystic carcinoma (ACC) demonstrated favorable response rates and progression-free survival (PFS), leading to its inclusion in NCCN guidelines as the first immunotherapy-based option for ACC. We sought to identify biomarkers predictive of clinical benefit from axitinib plus avelumab.

EXPERIMENTAL METHODS: Twenty-eight patients with ACC enrolled. Pre-treatment tumors underwent whole exome sequencing, transcriptome profiling, imaging mass cytometry, and tumor, oral rinse and stool microbiome characterization using 16s rRNA gene sequencing. Associations with PFS were assessed using Cox proportional hazards model, incorporating ACC subtype (ACC-I vs. ACC-II) as a covariate.

RESULTS: ACC comprises two proteogenomically distinct subtypes: aggressive, NOTCH1-activated ACC-I and more indolent ACC-II. Here, median PFS was 1.8 months for ACC-I versus 11.4 months for ACC-II (HR, 0.14; P<0.0001), representing the first demonstration of subtype-specific clinical trial outcomes in ACC. Clinical benefit was not associated with PD-L1 expression, tumor mutational burden, or recurrent genomic mutations. Instead, improved PFS correlated with the presence of intratumoral Escherichia and gut Bifidobacterium and Akkermansia. Transcriptomic profiling identified a 167-gene immune-enriched signature predictive of PFS benefit with axitinib plus avelumab. This signature also predicted benefit from ipilimumab plus nivolumab, but not from regorafenib monotherapy, suggesting its specificity for immunotherapy-based combinations.

CONCLUSIONS: Clinical trial outcomes with axitinib plus avelumab differ significantly by ACC subtype. Furthermore, the identified 167-gene immune-related signature predicts clinical benefit to immunotherapy-based combinations in ACC. These findings provide a framework for future biomarker-driven trial design and patient stratification strategies for this rare malignancy.

</details>

*Li Luo,Ziqin Liu,Zimin Song,Dong Zhang,Cong Liang,Fei Fang,Kai Lei,Lina Wang,Weikang Chen,Shunli Shen,Ming Kuang,Xiaoxing Li,Jun Yu,Shiyan Wang,Lixia Xu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知m6A阅读蛋白YTHDF1在肝内胆管癌（ICC）发生中起关键作用，但其在肿瘤免疫微环境中的具体功能及机制尚不明确。

Method: 结合人类ICC样本的RNA测序分析、多种ICC小鼠模型（体内实验）、人类ICC组织芯片免疫组化验证以及分子机制研究（探究YTHDF1与FOSL2 mRNA的结合及下游CXCL6/CXCR2通路）。

Result: 1. YTHDF1表达与免疫评分呈显著负相关。
2. YTHDF1过表达促进髓源性抑制细胞（MDSCs）募集，抑制细胞毒性CD8+ T细胞反应。
3. 机制上，YTHDF1结合FOSL2 mRNA的m6A位点促进其翻译，进而上调转录因子FOSL2驱动的趋化因子CXCL6表达。
4. CXCL6通过受体CXCR2招募并激活MDSCs，导致CD8+ T细胞功能障碍。
5. 靶向YTHDF1联合阻断下游趋化因子通路可增强抗PD-L1治疗的预临床疗效。

Conclusion: YTHDF1通过FOSL2/CXCL6轴介导免疫抑制微环境，促进ICC进展；靶向YTHDF1及其下游通路是改善ICC免疫治疗效果的潜在策略。

Abstract: The N6-methyladenosine (m6A) reader YTH N6-methyladenosine RNA binding protein 1 (YTHDF1) plays a critical role in the tumorigenesis of intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but its function in the tumor immune microenvironment remains unclear. RNA sequencing analysis of human ICC samples revealed that, among m6A-related regulators, YTHDF1 exhibited the most significant negative correlation with immune score. In multiple ICC mouse models, Ythdf1 overexpression enhanced the recruitment of myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs) and suppressed cytotoxic CD8+ T cell responses, promoting ICC progression. Immunostaining of human ICC tissue microarray verified that high YTHDF1 protein expression was significantly associated with increased accumulation of MDSCs and decreased infiltration of CD8+ T cells. Mechanistically, YTHDF1 bound to the m6A site of FOSL2 mRNA and promoted the translation of FOSL2, a transcription factor driving cytokine CXCL6 expression. Consequently, elevated CXCL6 recruited and activated MDSCs by binding to its receptor CXCR2, leading to the dysfunction of CD8+ T cells in ICCs. In addition, targeting YTHDF1 alongside blockade of its downstream chemokine pathway enhanced the efficacy of anti-PD-L1 treatment in preclinical ICC mouse models, serving a promising strategy for improving immunotherapy efficacy in ICC.

</details>


### [32] [Co-delivering macrophage engager mRNA and PD-L1 antibody via tumor-responsive nanoparticles for glioblastoma immunotherapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41965349/)
*Haoge Zhang,Jia Miao,Lin Gao,Xuhong Yang,Zhengcheng Yun,Lei Dong,Wanqing Cheng,Yuqi Wang,Hui Yang,Ying Zhou,Yini Zhu,Jinbing Xie*
