<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双特异性T细胞衔接器在治疗胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）等实体瘤时面临的系统性毒性、T细胞浸润差及药物渗透受限导致疗效有限的问题。

Method: 开发PL@mBiME脂质纳米颗粒（LNP）平台，递送编码BiME的mRNA。BiME同时靶向胶质瘤细胞上的ErbB2和M2巨噬细胞上的CD206。LNP具备pH响应电荷反转以改善肿瘤积累和溶酶体逃逸，并通过谷胱甘肽触发释放表面偶联的PD-L1抗体。

Result: 在多种GBM模型中，该方法重编程巨噬细胞向促炎M1表型转化，增强吞噬作用和抗原呈递，协同激活先天和适应性免疫，诱导肿瘤消退，延长生存期，并产生持久的免疫记忆。

Conclusion: PL@mBiME平台通过靶向递送和免疫微环境调控，有效克服了GBM治疗瓶颈，在无显著毒性的情况下实现了强大的抗肿瘤效果和长期免疫保护。

Abstract: Bispecific immune cell engagers, particularly bispecific T-cell engagers, show limited efficacy in solid tumors such as glioblastoma (GBM) due to systemic toxicities, poor T cell infiltration, and restricted drug penetration. We develop PL@mBiME, a multifunctional lipid nanoparticle (LNP) platform that enables brain tumor-targeted delivery and sustained in vivo expression of mRNA encoding a bispecific macrophage engager (BiME). The BiME simultaneously targets ErbB2 on glioma cells and CD206 on M2 macrophages, reprogramming macrophages toward pro-inflammatory M1 phenotype while promoting macrophage-tumor cell bridging, enhancing tumor cell phagocytosis and antigen presentation. PL@mBiME incorporates pH-responsive charge reversal to improve tumor accumulation and lysosomal escape as well as glutathione-triggered release of surface-conjugated PD-L1 antibody to amplify anti-tumor immunity. Across multiple GBM models, this coordinated activation of innate and adaptive immunity induces tumor regression, prolongs survival, and generates durable immune memory without significant toxicity.

</details>


### [33] [Dual Pathway Inhibition in Patients With mRCC: A Case Series From Kuwait Highlighting the Role of Avelumab and Axitinib.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41969645/)

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过科威特癌症控制中心（KCCC）的4例晚期转移性肾细胞癌（mRCC）患者病例系列，证实了阿维鲁单抗（Avelumab）联合阿昔替尼（Axitinib）在真实世界临床实践中的有效性和耐受性。尽管患者具有高转移负荷和合并症，所有患者均表现出良好的放射学和临床反应，且无3-4级毒性，结果与JAVELIN Renal 101试验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转移性肾细胞癌（mRCC）具有侵袭性强和早期转移潜能的特点。虽然JAVELIN Renal 101等关键试验已证明阿维鲁单抗联合阿昔替尼的疗效，但需要进一步报告在真实世界环境中，特别是针对具有高转移负荷、合并症或既往治疗失败的患者群体的临床结果，以验证该联合疗法在实际临床应用中的价值。

Method: 采用病例系列研究方法，回顾性分析科威特癌症控制中心（KCCC）收治的4例晚期mRCC且伴有广泛转移的患者。通过多个时间点的PET-CT和CT扫描等影像学手段，评估患者的诊断影像、治疗方案、疾病进展、治疗反应、疾病控制情况及治疗耐受性。

Result: 所有4名患者尽管存在高转移负荷和合并症，均显示出有利的放射学和临床反应，表现为多个转移灶的疾病消退或病情稳定。治疗总体耐受性良好，未记录到3级或4级毒性反应。

Conclusion: 阿维鲁单抗联合阿昔替尼作为转移性肾细胞癌的一线治疗选项，在真实世界中展现出良好的疗效和耐受性，即使对于高转移负荷或伴有合并症的患者也是如此。研究结果支持双重免疫和血管生成通路抑制在晚期RCC治疗中的价值，并与JAVELIN Renal 101试验发现一致。未来需进一步研究生物标志物和患者选择策略以优化不同人群的临床结局。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Renal cell carcinoma (RCC) accounts for approximately 3% of adult cancers and is characterized by aggressive behavior and early metastatic potential. The therapeutic paradigm of metastatic RCC (mRCC) has evolved from tyrosine kinase inhibitors (TKIs) alone to immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICIs) and ICI-TKI combinations. Avelumab, an anti-PD-L1 antibody, in combination with axitinib, a VEGFR-targeted TKI, has demonstrated efficacy in pivotal trials such as JAVELIN Renal 101.

OBJECTIVE: The objective of this study is to report the clinical outcomes of four patients with advanced mRCC and extensive metastases treated with avelumab with axitinib at the Kuwait Cancer Control Center (KCCC).

METHODS: This case series describes diagnostic imaging, treatment regimens, and disease progression in four patients. Imaging modalities included PET-CT and CT scans, performed at multiple intervals to assess therapeutic response, disease control, and treatment tolerability.

RESULTS: All patients demonstrated favorable radiological and clinical responses despite high metastatic burden and the presence of comorbidities, including disease regression or stable disease across multiple metastatic sites. Treatment was generally well tolerated, with no Grade III or IV toxicities recorded.

CONCLUSION: This case series involving patients treated at KCCC demonstrates the real-world efficacy and tolerability of avelumab plus axitinib as a first-line option for metastatic renal cell carcinoma, even in patients with high metastatic burden, comorbidities, or prior treatment failure. Outcomes observed are consistent with JAVELIN Renal 101 trial findings, reinforcing the therapeutic value of dual immune and angiogenic pathway inhibition in advanced RCC care. Further investigation into biomarkers and patient selection may help optimize clinical outcomes across diverse populations.

</details>

*Kelli L Adeleye,Damilola Oluwalana,Raisa Krutilina,Yang Xie,Satyanarayana Pochampally,Lily Waltz,Gustavo A Miranda-Carboni,Duane D Miller,Tiffany N Seagroves,Wei Li*

Method: 1. 利用体内模型评估SP-1-39在转移性TNBC中的抗转移活性；2. 在PD-L1响应的结肠癌模型中，评估SP-1-39与抗PD-L1免疫检查点抑制剂联合使用的治疗潜力；3. 通过体外实验观察SP-1-39对PD-L1表达的调控作用。

Conclusion: SP-1-39是一种极具前景的治疗剂，既可作为单药用于治疗TNBC，也可与免疫疗法联合应用以增强疗效，为包括TNBC在内的多种实体瘤提供了新的治疗策略。

Abstract: Triple-negative breast cancer (TNBC) accounts for approximately 15% of all breast cancer cases and is defined by the absence of estrogen receptor, progesterone receptor, and human epidermal growth factor receptor 2 (HER2) expression. Approximately 40% of patients with TNBC develop metastatic disease, reducing median survival to 13.3 months. Paclitaxel remains a standard chemotherapeutic option; however, its efficacy is often limited by ATP-driven efflux pumps and βIII-tubulin overexpression. These limitations underscore the need for agents that can overcome resistance and suppress metastasis. Colchicine-binding site inhibitors (CBSIs) target the colchicine-binding site on tubulin to induce G2/M arrest and apoptosis and can bypass taxane resistance. SP-1-39, a novel CBSI, exhibits potent anti-cancer activity in a paclitaxel-resistant prostate cancer in vivo model, confirming its capacity to overcome chemoresistance. We also discovered that SP-1-39 upregulates PD-L1 expression in vitro, suggesting synergy with immune checkpoint blockade. This study evaluates the anti-metastatic activity of SP-1-39 in metastatic TNBC and the therapeutic potential of combining SP-1-39 with anti-PD-L1 immune checkpoint inhibition in a PD-L1-responsive colon cancer model. Our findings provide preclinical evidence supporting SP-1-39 as a promising therapeutic agent for TNBC and other solid tumors, either as a single agent or in combination with immunotherapy.

</details>

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: ASCO 2025展示了多项改变胃肠道癌症管理的III期临床试验结果：CheckMate 577显示辅助纳武利尤单抗仅在PD-L1阳性食管/GEJ癌中可能有OS获益；MATTERHORN证实围手术期度伐利尤单抗联合FLOT显著改善胃/GEJ癌的EFS和OS；ATOMIC支持dMMR III期结肠癌的辅助阿替利珠单抗联合化疗；BREAKWATER确立恩考芬尼三联疗法为BRAF V600E突变mCRC的一线标准；DESTINY-Gastric04确认曲妥珠单抗德鲁替康为HER2阳性胃癌二线优越疗法；DYNAMIC-III证明ctDNA指导的降阶梯治疗可减少毒性。这些进展强调了全面生物标志物检测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在总结和评估ASCO 2025会议上发布的具有潜在影响力的胃肠道癌症管理III期临床试验数据，包括食管/胃食管结合部（GEJ）癌、胃癌、结肠癌和结直肠癌的最新治疗进展，并整合ESMO 2025的DYNAMIC-III试验结果，以突出生物标志物驱动的治疗策略和当前存在的证据缺口。

Method: 综述和分析多项关键III期临床试验的结果：
1. CheckMate 577：评估辅助纳武利尤单抗在食管/GEJ癌中的成熟总生存期（OS）数据。
2. MATTERHORN：评估围手术期度伐利尤单抗联合FLOT方案在胃/GEJ癌中的无事件生存期（EFS）和OS。
3. ATOMIC：评估辅助阿替利珠单抗联合化疗在III期dMMR结肠癌中的疗效。
4. BREAKWATER：评估恩考芬尼基于的三联疗法作为BRAF V600E突变转移性结直肠癌（mCRC）一线治疗的地位。
5. DESTINY-Gastric04：评估曲妥珠单抗德鲁替康作为HER2阳性胃癌二线治疗的优越性。
6. DYNAMIC-III（ESMO 2025）：评估ctDNA指导的降阶梯治疗在减少毒性方面的效果。

Result: 1. CheckMate 577：意向治疗人群未显示统计学显著的OS获益，获益似乎局限于PD-L1阳性肿瘤。
2. MATTERHORN：围手术期度伐利尤单抗联合FLOT显著改善胃/GEJ癌的EFS和OS。
3. ATOMIC：支持辅助阿替利珠单抗联合化疗用于III期dMMR结肠癌，但化疗的必要性和新辅助免疫治疗的作用仍存在疑问。
4. BREAKWATER：确立恩考芬尼基于的三联疗法为BRAF V600E突变mCRC的一线标准治疗。
5. DESTINY-Gastric04：确认曲妥珠单抗德鲁替康是HER2阳性胃癌二线治疗的优越选择，强调需要重新活检。
6. DYNAMIC-III：ctDNA指导的降阶梯治疗减少了毒性，且疗效接近标准护理。

Conclusion: ASCO 2025展示的进展标志着胃肠道癌症治疗向更精准的生物标志物驱动模式转变。这些结果确立了特定分子亚群（如BRAF V600E、HER2阳性、dMMR）的新标准治疗方案，并强调了全面生物标志物检测的临床必要性。同时，研究也揭示了持续存在的证据缺口，如免疫治疗中化疗的最佳角色以及ctDNA在临床决策中的进一步优化应用。

Abstract: The ASCO 2025 meeting featured several phase III trials with the potential to impact gastrointestinal cancer management. CheckMate 577 reported mature overall survival (OS) data for adjuvant nivolumab in esophageal/GEJ cancer; however, OS benefit was not statistically significant in the intent-to-treat population and appeared restricted to PD-L1-positive tumors. MATTERHORN demonstrated significant improvements in event-free survival and OS with perioperative durvalumab plus FLOT in gastric/GEJ cancer. In stage III dMMR colon cancer, ATOMIC supported adjuvant atezolizumab plus chemotherapy, though questions persist about chemotherapy necessity and neoadjuvant immunotherapy. For advanced disease, BREAKWATER established encorafenib-based triplet therapy as first-line standard for BRAF V600E-mutant metastatic colorectal cancer, while DESTINY-Gastric04 confirmed trastuzumab deruxtecan as superior second-line therapy for HER2-positive gastric cancer, mandating re-biopsy. Given the great interest in circulating tumor DNA, we integrate the ESMO 2025 DYNAMIC-III trial, demonstrating ctDNA-guided de-escalation reduces toxicity with outcomes approaching standard care. These advances mandate comprehensive biomarker testing while highlighting persistent evidence gaps.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决免疫检查点阻断（ICB）治疗胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）时面临的两大主要障碍：血脑屏障（BBB）的限制以及与胶质瘤干细胞（GSCs）相关的免疫抑制肿瘤微环境。

Method: 设计并合成多模态两性离子胶束（MCB-PD@S）：将抗PD-L1抗体通过pH敏感的可断裂连接子偶联至超小两性离子胶束（MCB），并包裹萨利诺霉素（SAL）。该载体利用BGT-1介导的主动转运穿越BBB，通过囊泡运输途径在GBM血管中特异性积聚，并利用两性离子表面通过单羧酸转运蛋白（MCTs）增强对GSC生态位的渗透。

Result: MCB-PD@S能有效清除GSCs，诱导强烈的免疫原性细胞死亡、树突状细胞成熟及T细胞激活。在酸性肿瘤微环境中释放结构完整的抗PD-L1抗体，与SAL效应产生协同作用，激发强劲且持久的抗肿瘤免疫反应，有效抑制GBM复发。

Conclusion: 该研究通过同时克服生物递送屏障和免疫逃逸机制，确立了脑肿瘤治疗的新范式，为术后GBM管理提供了一种具有临床转化潜力的解决方案。

Abstract: The efficacy of immune checkpoint blockade (ICB) against glioblastoma (GBM) is significantly constrained by the blood-brain barrier (BBB) and the immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment associated with glioma stem cells (GSCs). To address these challenges, anti-programmed death-ligand 1 antibodies (anti-PD-L1) are conjugated to ultra-small zwitterionic micelles (MCB) through pH-sensitive traceless linkers, followed by encapsulation of salinomycin (SAL) to construct multimodal zwitterionic micelles (MCB-PD@S). The resulting micelles efficiently cross the BBB via BGT-1-mediated active transport, exploit vesicular trafficking pathways in GBM vasculature for tumor-specific accumulation, and utilize their zwitterionic surface to enhance penetration into GSC niches through monocarboxylate transporters (MCTs). SAL-induced eradication of GSCs robustly stimulates immunogenic cell death, dendritic cell maturation, and subsequent T-cell activation. This effect is synergistically amplified by the release of structurally intact anti-PD-L1 within the acidic tumor microenvironment, collectively fostering potent and sustained antitumor immunity that effectively suppresses GBM recurrence. This work establishes a new paradigm in brain tumor therapy by simultaneously overcoming biological delivery barriers and immune evasion, offering a clinically translatable solution for post-resection GBM management.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝细胞癌（HCC）由于致密的间质结构和具有免疫排斥表型的免疫抑制肿瘤微环境，对免疫检查点阻断疗法表现出显著的耐药性，亟需新的治疗策略来克服这一挑战。

Method: 开发了一种包含全氟碳核心、嵌入血卟啉单甲醚的脂质双层壳以及PD-1过表达细胞膜外涂层的仿生纳米气泡（NB@CM-H-PD-1）。该平台执行同步级联反应：膜展示PD-1介导肿瘤特异性靶向并提供检查点抑制；声敏剂激活产生活性氧（ROS）触发免疫原性细胞死亡；超声靶向纳米气泡破坏通过空化诱导的基质破坏增强瘤内渗透。

Result: 体外实验显示NB@CM-H-PD-1具有良好的生物相容性及超声触发的ROS产生和细胞毒性。在皮下和转移性HCC模型中，该方法实现了近乎完全的肿瘤清除且无全身毒性。免疫学分析表明，该策略诱导了强烈的免疫原性细胞死亡、树突状细胞成熟、T细胞浸润增加及IFN-γ分泌，重编程肿瘤微环境并启动系统性抗肿瘤免疫。

Conclusion: 该仿生平台作为一种有效的机械-化学-免疫协同驱动器，能够敏化难治性肝细胞癌对免疫疗法的响应，为克服HCC免疫治疗耐药性提供了新策略。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma exhibits profound resistance to immune checkpoint blockade due to its dense stromal architecture and immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment with an immune-excluded phenotype. To overcome this therapeutic challenge, we developed PD-1-displaying biomimetic nanobubbles (NB@CM-H-PD-1) that orchestrate a trimodal strategy combining PD-L1-targeted checkpoint blockade, sonodynamic therapy, and acoustic cavitation-mediated stromal remodeling to transform immunologically "cold" HCC into an immune-responsive state. These engineered nanobubbles comprise a perfluorocarbon core, a lipid-bilayer shell embedded with hematoporphyrin monomethyl ether, and an outer coating of cell membranes derived from PD-1-overexpressing cells. The platform executes a synchronized cascade: membrane-displayed PD-1 mediates tumor-specific targeting while providing checkpoint inhibition; sonosensitizer activation generates reactive oxygen species (ROS) triggering immunogenic cell death; and ultrasound-targeted nanobubble destruction enhances intratumoral penetration through cavitation-induced stromal disruption. In vitro characterization demonstrated that NB@CM-H-PD-1 maintained excellent biocompatibility under physiological conditions while exhibiting potent ultrasound-triggered ROS production and cytotoxic activity. In both subcutaneous and metastatic HCC models, this approach achieved near-complete tumor eradication without systemic toxicity. Comprehensive immunological analyses revealed robust immunogenic cell death induction, dendritic cells (DCs) maturation, enhanced T-cell infiltration with increased IFN-γ secretion, and tumor microenvironment reprogramming that initiated systemic antitumor immunity controlling both primary and metastatic lesions. Thus, our findings establish this biomimetic platform as an effective mechano-chemical-immune synergistic actuator for sensitizing refractory HCC to immunotherapy.

</details>


### [38] [Deep tumor access via tumor-specific mannose unmasking: Smart heptamannosylated β-cyclodextrin nanoassembly.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41967444/)

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种名为“纳米搭车者”（nano-hitchhikers）的肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM）靶向纳米载体，通过酸性肿瘤微环境触发甘露糖暴露，利用TAM的穿透能力将阿霉素深层递送至肿瘤内部，并结合抗PD-L1肽实现化疗-免疫协同治疗，显著抑制肿瘤生长并重塑免疫微环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统药物难以穿透致密肿瘤间质到达深部肿瘤细胞的难题。利用肿瘤驻留免疫细胞（如TAMs）固有的肿瘤穿透能力，作为主动运输外渗治疗剂的载体，以消除深部肿瘤细胞。

Method: 构建由负载阿霉素（Dox）的七甘露糖基化β-环糊精组成的纳米载体，并通过苯硼酸封端的抗PD-L1肽进行交联（直径约100 nm）。利用甘露糖上的邻二醇与苯硼酸形成硼酸酯键，使载体在全身循环中保持惰性；在酸性肿瘤微环境中，硼酸酯水解暴露甘露糖，从而激活TAM靶向及“搭车”机制。

Result: 1. 静脉注射24小时后实现深部肿瘤渗透。
2. Dox释放引发细胞毒性及免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD）。
3. 与原位释放的抗PD-L1肽协同，在4T1模型中抗肿瘤效率达43.4%。
4. 重塑免疫抑制微环境：CD8+ T细胞浸润增加3倍，树突状细胞成熟度显著提升至46.7 ± 0.76%。
5. 在4T1及富含间质的Pan02模型中均显示出显著的肿瘤生长抑制作用。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种原位可激活的系统，通过利用内源性免疫细胞（TAMs）作为运输工具，实现了深部肿瘤的精准清除及化疗-免疫联合治疗的高效协同，为克服肿瘤间质屏障提供了新策略。

Abstract: Tumor-resident immune cells, such as tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs), possess inherent tumor-penetrating capabilities and hold significant potential for actively transporting extravasated therapeutics through dense tumor stroma to eliminate deep-seated tumor cells. Inspired by this mechanism, we engineered TAM-hitchhiking nanocarriers ("nano-hitchhikers") for deep-tumor drug delivery. The nano-hitchhikers comprise heptamannosylated β-cyclodextrin loaded with doxorubicin (Dox), crosslinked via phenylboronic acid-terminated anti-PD-L1 peptides with a diameter of ∼100 nm. Boronate ester formation with vicinal diols on mannose-a TAM-targeting ligand-rendered the nano-hitchhikers inert during systemic circulation. Upon accumulation in the acidic tumor microenvironment, acid-triggered hydrolysis of boronate esters exposed mannose, enabling active TAM hitchhiking. Subsequently, TAM-mediated transport delivered Dox into deep tumor regions in a time-dependent manner, reaching a deep-penetration at 24 h post-i.v. injection. Subsequently, the Dox released from TAMs elicited potent cytotoxicity and robust immunogenic cell death. When synergized with the in situ released anti-PD-L1 peptides, the anti-tumor efficacy reached 43.4% in the 4T1 model. Additionally, this deep-delivery strategy successfully reprogrammed the immunosuppressive microenvironment, evidenced by a 3-fold increase in CD8+ T cell infiltration and markedly enhanced dendritic cell maturation (46.7 ± 0.76%). Ultimately, this chemo-immunotherapy achieved significant tumor growth inhibition in the 4T1 model, alongside robust therapeutic efficacy in the stroma-rich Pan02 model. Our work presents an in situ activable system leveraging endogenous immune cells for precision eradication of deep tumors.

</details>


### [39] [Oncolytic adenovirus in combination with PD-L1-targeted radioimmunotherapy exerts synergistic antitumor effect against pancreatic cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41956542/)
*A-Rum Yoon,Seungyoun Kim,Ji Yi,Javeria Zaheer,Hyeongi Kim,Je Hyeon Seo,Jinwoo Hong,Jeongwon Lee,Hyeju Jeong,Joycie Shanmugiah,Ju Hee Kim,In-Wook Kim,Jin Su Kim,Chae-Ok Yun*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种共表达IL-12、GM-CSF和松弛素的溶瘤腺病毒（HY-oAd），并与靶向PD-L1的放射性免疫疗法（177Lu-aPD-L1）联用。HY-oAd通过降解细胞外基质和上调PD-L1表达，显著增加了药物在胰腺肿瘤中的蓄积，协同激活树突状细胞及T细胞浸润，从而在临床前模型中展现出针对难治性胰腺癌的强效抗肿瘤作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚无美国FDA批准的用于胰腺癌治疗的放射性免疫疗法（RIT）。胰腺癌高度致密的间质（desmoplastic）和免疫荒漠（immune-desert）表型严重阻碍了常规放化疗及免疫疗法（如免疫检查点抑制剂和CAR-T细胞）的疗效，亟需新的治疗策略来克服这些障碍。

Method: 研究构建了一种共表达白细胞介素-12（IL-12）、粒细胞-巨噬细胞集落刺激因子（GM-CSF）和松弛素（relaxin）的溶瘤腺病毒（HY-oAd）。将其与靶向PD-L1的RIT药物（镥-177标记的阿替利珠单抗，177Lu-aPD-L1）联合使用。通过皮下和原位胰腺肿瘤模型，评估该联合疗法（HY-oAd + aPD-L1 或 HY-oAd + 177Lu-aPD-L1）相对于单药治疗的抗肿瘤效果及机制。

Result: HY-oAd处理提高了肿瘤组织中的PD-L1表达水平并促进细胞外基质降解，从而增加了aPD-L1或64Cu-aPD-L1在肿瘤组织中的蓄积。在皮下和原位胰腺肿瘤模型中，HY-oAd与aPD-L1或177Lu-aPD-L1的联合治疗比各自单药治疗产生了更强大的抗肿瘤效应。这种协同效应归因于联合治疗显著促进了树突状细胞以及CD4+和CD8+ T细胞在肿瘤内部的浸润和激活。

Conclusion: HY-oAd能够通过多重机制增强177Lu-aPD-L1在肿瘤内的蓄积，引发协同的抗肿瘤免疫反应，有效对抗具有致密间质和低免疫原性特征的胰腺肿瘤，为胰腺癌的治疗提供了潜在的新策略。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: To date, no radioimmunotherapy (RIT) regimen has been approved by US Food and Drug Administration for the treatment of pancreatic cancers. Highly desmoplastic and immune-desert phenotypes of pancreatic cancer remain two major hurdles that attenuate the efficacy of conventional treatment (radiotherapy and chemotherapy) and immunotherapeutic (immune checkpoint inhibitors and chimeric antigen receptor T cells).

METHOD: To overcome these hurdles, an oncolytic adenovirus (oAd) co-expressing interleukin-12, granulocyte macrophage colony-stimulating factor, and relaxin (HY-oAd) was investigated in combination with programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1)-targeted RIT (lutetium-177-labeled atezolizumab (177Lu-aPD-L1)).

RESULTS: HY-oAd treatment was shown to elevate PD-L1 expression level and promoted degradation of extracellular matrix of pancreatic tumors, resulting in increased aPD-L1 or 64Cu-aPD-L1 accumulation in tumor tissues. HY-oAd in combination with either aPD-L1 or 177Lu-aPD-L1 (HY-oAd+aPD-L1 or HY-oAd+177Lu-aPD-L1, respectively) elicited more potent antitumor effect against the pancreatic tumors than respective monotherapy in both subcutaneous and orthotopic pancreatic tumor models. The potent antitumor effect of HY-oAd+aPD-L1 combination therapy was due to superior intratumoral infiltration and activation of dendritic cells and CD4+ or CD8+ T cells over the respective monotherapy.

CONCLUSION: Collectively, our findings demonstrate that HY-oAd can enhance intratumoral accumulation of 177Lu-aPD-L1 in a multifaceted manner to elicit synergistic antitumor immune response against desmoplastic and poorly immunogenic pancreatic tumors.

</details>

*Yu Sato,Takahiro Kodama,Kazuki Maesaka,Machiko Kai,Kazuhiro Murai,Yuki Tahata,Yoshinobu Saito,Tasuku Nakabori,Kazuyoshi Ohkawa,Satoshi Tanaka,Ryotaro Sakamori,Masanori Miyazaki,Kunimaro Furuta,Hisashi Ishida,Kengo Matsumoto,Seiichi Tawara,Takayuki Yakushijin,Yasutoshi Nozaki,Atsushi Hosui,Akira Nishio,Nobuyuki Tatsumi,Naruyasu Kakita,Changhoon Yoo,Moto Fukai,Akinobu Taketomi,Hayato Hikita,Tomohide Tatsumi,Tetsuo Takehara*

TL;DR: 本研究通过血浆蛋白质组学筛选发现，碳酸酐酶9（CA9）是预测肝细胞癌（HCC）患者对阿替利珠单抗联合贝伐珠单抗（Atez/Bev）治疗耐药及预后不良的独立生物标志物。机制上，肿瘤源性CA9导致免疫抑制微环境（CD8+ T细胞浸润减少、M2巨噬细胞富集），而联合CA9抑制剂可逆转耐药并恢复疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿替利珠单抗联合贝伐珠单抗（Atez/Bev）已成为不可切除肝细胞癌（HCC）的一线疗法，但目前缺乏可靠的生物标志物来预测患者的治疗反应和预后，临床亟需识别能够预测疗效及耐药的分子指标。

Method: 1. **发现与验证队列**：利用Olink邻近延伸分析法对78名接受Atez/Bev治疗的HCC患者进行高通量血浆蛋白质组学筛选，并在89名患者的独立队列中进行验证。
2. **多组学分析**：通过转录组学和单细胞分析检查候选分子碳酸酐酶9（CA9）在肿瘤组织中的表达特征。
3. **功能研究**：构建Car9过表达的肝癌细胞同系小鼠模型，评估抗PD-L1/抗VEGF抗体联合或不联合CA9抑制剂的治疗效果及免疫微环境变化。

Result: 1. **临床关联**：高血浆CA9水平与客观缓解率、疾病控制率、无进展生存期（PFS）和总生存期（OS）显著负相关，是多变量分析中预测治疗耐药和不良预后的独立因子。
2. **来源与相关性**：肿瘤内CA9表达局限于恶性细胞，且与循环CA9水平强相关。
3. **机制阐释**：体内实验显示，Car9过表达导致对Atez/Bev耐药，特征为CD8+ T细胞浸润减少、细胞毒性基因表达受抑、M2样巨噬细胞富集及促血管生成信号增强。
4. **干预效果**：添加CA9抑制剂可逆转耐药性，恢复Atez/Bev的抗肿瘤功效。

Conclusion: 肿瘤衍生的循环CA9不仅是HCC患者对Atez/Bev治疗耐药和预后不良的预测性生物标志物，也是功能性的耐药驱动因子。靶向抑制CA9有望增强Atez/Bev的治疗反应，为克服免疫治疗耐药提供新策略。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Atezolizumab plus bevacizumab (Atez/Bev) has become the first-line therapy for unresectable hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). However, reliable biomarkers to predict therapeutic response remain lacking.

METHODS: We conducted a high-throughput plasma proteomic screen using the Olink proximity extension assay to identify predictive biomarkers in 78 patients with unresectable HCC treated with Atez/Bev. Validation was performed in an independent cohort of 89 patients. Tumorous expression of the lead candidate, carbonic anhydrase 9 (CA9), was examined using transcriptomic and single-cell analyses. Functional studies were performed in a syngeneic mouse model using Car9-overexpressing hepatoma cells, treated with anti-programmed death-ligand 1/vascular endothelial growth factor antibodies, with or without a CA9 inhibitor.

RESULTS: High plasma CA9 levels were significantly associated with poor objective response rate, disease control rate, progression-free survival, and overall survival in both discovery and validation cohorts. Multivariable analysis confirmed CA9 as an independent predictor of treatment resistance and poor outcomes. Tumorous CA9 expression correlated strongly with circulating CA9 and was restricted to malignant cells. In vivo, tumors overexpressing Car9 showed resistance to Atez/Bev therapy, characterized by reduced CD8+T cell infiltration, suppressed cytotoxic gene expression, enrichment of M2-like macrophages, and pro-angiogenic signaling. The addition of a CA9 inhibitor reversed resistance and restored antitumor efficacy of Atez/Bev in this model.

CONCLUSIONS: Tumor-derived circulating CA9 is a predictive biomarker and functional driver of resistance to Atez/Bev therapy in HCC. Targeting CA9 may enhance therapeutic response.

</details>

Method: 这是一项回顾性研究，记录了359名NSCLC患者的人口统计学、临床和治疗数据，包括组织病理学特征、疾病分期及转移部位。研究分析了中位生存率、1/3/5年生存率、协变量风险比以及总体生存率的独立预测因子，并对比了不同治疗方案（如三联疗法、化疗联合放疗、单纯化疗及单一治疗）的效果。

Result: 1. 不同治疗方案的中位生存期存在统计学显著差异（p = 0.002）。

3. 三联疗法（铂类双药化疗+mAb）的中位生存期（20.7个月）显著高于化疗联合放疗（13.6个月）、单纯铂类双药化疗（9.1个月）或单一治疗（12.9个月）（p = 0.01）。
4. 三联疗法的1年、3年和5年生存率均优于其他组别。
5. 总体生存率的显著预测因子包括：癌症转移（p = 0.0001）、诊断时的疾病分期（p = 0.001）、患者年龄（p = 0.017）和肺切除手术执行情况（p = 0.021）。有转移的患者中位生存期显著低于无转移患者。

Conclusion: 在铂类双药化疗中加入单克隆抗体（如抗PD-1/PD-L1、表皮生长因子受体抑制剂或血管内皮生长因子抑制剂）可显著改善非小细胞肺癌患者的总体生存期和生存率。

Abstract: Lung cancer treatment presents a major challenge globally. The advent of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) has ushered in a new era in the treatment of non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). This study aimed to investigate the impact of the addition of mAbs to platinum-based doublet therapy on overall survival in NSCLC patients and the difference in median survival among patients treated with different treatment protocols. In this study, demographic, clinical, and therapeutic data from 359 NSCLC patients were recorded, and the histopathological and disease stages of the patients, along with the site of metastasis, were documented. The median survival rates, 1-, 3-, and 5-year survival rates, covariate hazard ratios, and independent predictors of overall survival were analyzed. Statistically significant differences were observed in the median survival of patients treated with different treatment protocols (p = 0.002). Comparing therapies, patients treated with carboplatin/paclitaxel+anti-PD-1/anti-PD-L1 mAb or cisplatin/vinorelbine+anti-PD-1/anti-PD-L1 mAb had highest median survival (median survival months ± SE) (49.4 months ± 9.15 and 34.9 months ± 8.61 respectively) with lowest hazard ratio (HR = 0.032; 95% confidence interval (CI) [0.003-0.310], p = 0.003 and HR = 0.048; 95% CI [0.005-0.465], p = 0.0083 respectively). Patients treated with triple therapy [platinum-based doublet chemotherapy combined with mAb drugs had significantly (p = 0.01) greater median survival (20.7 months ± 3.11; HR = 0.593; 95% CI [0.443-0.794], p = 0.00046) compared to patients treated with platinum-based doublet chemotherapy with concurrent radiation (13.6 months ± 3.8; HR = 0.742 95% CI, [0.531-1.035], p = 0.07884] or patients treated with platinum-based doublet chemotherapy (9.1 months ± 0.68; HR = 1) or single treatment (12.9 months ±3.81; HR = 0.927; 95% CI [0.629-1.365], p = 0.69970) and better 1-, 3-, and 5-year survival rates (95% CI) [66.20% (0.554-0.77), 26.40% (0.162-0.366), and16.25% (0.065-0.261) respectively] than patients treated with platinum-based doublet chemotherapy with concurrent radiation [54% (0.403-0.677), 22% (0.104-0.336), and 4% (0.001-0.079) respectively] or without concurrent radiation [39.10% (0.324-0.458), 12.80% (0.081-0.175) and 3.57% (0.012-0.381) respectively] or single treatment [51.50% (0.407-0.623), 12.12% (0.0193-0.223), and 10.12% (0.003-0.199) respectively]. The statistically significant predictors for overall survival were cancer metastasis (p = 0.0001), disease stage at diagnosis (p = 0.001), patient age (p = 0.017), and performance of lung resection surgery (p = 0.021). 64.1% of the patients had metastasis, and they had significantly lower median survival than patients without metastasis (p = 0.0001). Multi-organ metastasis was the most common type of metastasis (20.9%). In conclusion, the addition of mAbs such as anti-programmed cell death protein 1/programmed death-ligand 1 or epidermal growth factor receptor inhibitors or vascular endothelial growth factor inhibitors to platinum-doublet chemotherapy markedly improved the overall survival and survival rates of NSCLC patients.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在制定符合哥伦比亚国情的、基于证据的共识建议，以指导围手术期及不可切除III期非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）的系统性治疗，同时充分考虑当地的检测能力和药物可及性限制。

Method: 由三个哥伦比亚专业学会发起，采用改良德尔菲法。多学科专家小组在2024年9月完成两轮匿名评分及结构化辩论。基于2010-2024年的系统文献检索，形成了涵盖诊断评估、新辅助/辅助治疗及不可切除III期管理的34条草案陈述。

Result: 34条陈述中有33条（97.1%）达成共识。主要推荐包括：多学科肿瘤委员会审查及治疗前分子谱分析；对于无敏感EGFR/ALK突变的可切除II-III期疾病，首选新辅助化疗-免疫治疗；对于不可切除III期，PD-L1阳性肿瘤推荐放化疗后度伐利尤单抗巩固（PD-L1 TPS <1%需个体化）；EGFR突变不可切除III期不推荐度伐利尤单抗，而倾向于奥希替尼巩固。唯一未达成共识的是关于EGFR/ALK阴性II-IIIA期患者铂类化疗后辅助阿替利珠单抗的使用（因当时未获INVIMA批准）。

Conclusion: 该德尔菲共识为哥伦比亚围手术期及不可切除III期NSCLC的管理提供了一个实用的国家级框架，平衡了国际证据与当地实际医疗条件。

Abstract: OBJECTIVES: To develop Colombian, evidence-informed consensus recommendations for perioperative systemic therapy and unresectable stage III non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), considering local testing and access constraints.

METHODS: Three Colombian professional societies conducted a modified Delphi process. A multidisciplinary panel completed two anonymous rating rounds in September 2024 and a structured debate. A systematic literature search (2010-2024) informed 34 draft statements spanning diagnostic workup, neoadjuvant and adjuvant therapy, and unresectable stage III management.

RESULTS: Consensus was reached for 33/34 statements (97.1%). The panel endorsed multidisciplinary tumor board review and pretreatment molecular profiling. For resectable stage II-III disease without sensitizing EGFR/ALK alterations, neoadjuvant chemo-immunotherapy was preferred. For unresectable stage III, chemoradiotherapy followed by durvalumab consolidation was endorsed for PD-L1-positive tumors; use in PD-L1 tumor proportion score < 1% should be individualized. In EGFR-mutated unresectable stage III disease, durvalumab was not endorsed; osimertinib consolidation was favored. The only non-consensus item was adjuvant atezolizumab for PD-L1 ≥1% after platinum chemotherapy in EGFR/ALK-negative stage II-IIIA disease (AJCC 7th), lacking INVIMA approval at the time.

CONCLUSION: This Delphi consensus provides a practical national framework for perioperative and unresectable stage III NSCLC management in Colombia.

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 结合临床样本分析与KPC小鼠模型，利用基因敲除（HAS2 KO）、分子生物学手段（检测泛素化修饰、信号通路激活）及体内实验，探究HAS2对PD-L1稳定性的调控机制及其对肿瘤微环境和免疫应答的影响。

Result: 1. HAS2在胰腺癌中显著上调，与不良预后、CD4⁺/CD8⁺ T细胞浸润减少及生存期缩短相关。
2. 机制上，HAS2激活AKT信号通路，下调E3泛素连接酶March4的表达，从而减少PD-L1的K6/K63位点泛素化降解，增强PD-L1稳定性。
3. 体内实验显示，HAS2敲除可降低PD-L1水平，增加March4表达，促进T细胞浸润，减少胶原沉积，并延缓癌症进展。
4. 抑制HAS2、使用抗PD-L1抗体或过表达March4均能抑制肿瘤生长并提高生存率。

Conclusion: HAS2通过HAS2-AKT-March4-PD-L1轴调控PD-L1稳定性，介导胰腺癌免疫逃逸。靶向该轴线（如抑制HAS2）联合免疫检查点阻断疗法，为克服胰腺癌免疫治疗耐药提供了新策略。

Abstract: Pancreatic cancer is an aggressive malignancy with poor prognosis and limited treatment options. Recent advances in immunotherapy have shown potential for improving outcomes; however, therapeutic resistance remains a challenge. We demonstrate that HAS2 is significantly upregulated in pancreatic cancer, and is associated with poor prognosis, reduced infiltration of CD4⁺ and CD8⁺ T cells, and shorter survival. Mechanistically, HAS2 stabilizes PD-L1, a key immune checkpoint molecule, by modulating K6- and K63-linked ubiquitination, enhancing PD-L1 stability and suppressing immune responses. Our study further reveals that HAS2 activates the AKT signaling pathway to downregulate March4 expression, a critical E3 ubiquitin ligase that negatively regulates PD-L1 stability, thereby promoting PD-L1 stabilization. In vivo, HAS2 knockout in KPC mice reduces PD-L1 levels, increases March4 expression, enhances T cell infiltration, and delays cancer progression. Reduced collagen deposition in HAS2⁺/⁻ tumors further underscores its role in tumor microenvironment remodeling. Targeting the HAS2-March4-PD-L1 axis through HAS2 inhibition, anti-PD-L1 therapy, or March4 overexpression suppresses tumor growth and improves survival.

</details>


### [44] [Clinical relevance of AI-based PD-L1 scoring in non-small cell lung cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42022313/)
*Mateusz Maniewski,Jędrzej Borowczak,Justyna Durślewicz,Marek Zdrenka,Adam Kowalewski,Łukasz Szylberg*

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究评估了uPath VENTANA PD-L1 (SP263) 算法与专家病理学家在非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）PD-L1评分中的一致性。结果显示两者在二分类（≥1%）和三分类中具有高度一致性（κ > 0.89），但在中间范围（1-49%）存在差异，导致6%的病例分类改变。结论认为AI目前最适合作为辅助工具，特别是用于验证临界病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人工智能（AI）的算法在组织病理学癌症诊断中的应用日益增加，旨在提高生物标志物评估的可重复性和效率。尽管AI有望标准化免疫组化评估并减少观察者间差异，但其作为专家评估替代方案的临床可靠性仍需严格验证。特别是在PD-L1解释中的微小差异可能改变患者分层和治疗结果，尤其是在边界表达范围内，因此需要评估AI算法与专家评估的一致性。

Method: 研究队列包含333例使用抗PD-L1 (SP263) 抗体染色的非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）病例。数字切片由一名认证病理学家和uPath算法独立评估。分析采用肿瘤比例评分（TPS），分为三类：<1%、1-49% 和 ≥50%。通过计算整体一致性及加权Kappa值来评估算法与病理学家评估结果的一致性，并分析不同TPS组别的差异。

Result: uPath算法与病理学家在PD-L1状态（截断值≥1%）上的总体一致性为95.2%（κ = 0.89），三分类一致性为94.5%（加权κ = 0.93）。uPath的中位PD-L1表达评分比病理学家高1.2个百分点（pp）。差异随TPS组别变化：<1%组平均差异0.2 pp，1-49%组为4.4 pp，≥50%组为1.95 pp。6%（20/333）的病例发生类别变更，可能影响免疫治疗资格。

Conclusion: AI辅助评估与专家病理学家显示出高度一致性，特别是在二分类中。然而，由于中间范围存在差异，该算法目前最佳定位是作为支持性工具，用于验证边界病例，而非完全替代专家评估。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-based algorithms are increasingly implemented in histopathological cancer diagnostics to enhance reproducibility and efficiency in biomarker assessment. Although AI-driven image analysis shows promise in standardizing immunohistochemical evaluation and reducing inter-observer variability, its clinical reliability as a substitute for expert assessment requires rigorous validation. Minor discrepancies in Programmed Death Ligand 1 (PD-L1) interpretation can alter patient stratification and treatment outcomes, especially in borderline expression ranges. This study evaluated the concordance between PD-L1 expression scores assessed by an expert pathologist and the uPath VENTANA PD-L1 (SP263) Assay Algorithm. The cohort included 333 non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) cases stained with the anti-PD-L1 (SP263) antibody. Digital slides were independently assessed by a board-certified pathologist and the uPath algorithm. Analysis used the Tumor Proportion Score (TPS), stratified into three categories: <1%, 1-49%, and ≥50%. The overall concordance of PD-L1 status (cutoff ≥1%) between the uPath algorithm and the pathologist was 95.2% (κ = 0.89), while the concordance for the three-tier categorization was 94.5% (weighted κ = 0.93). The median PD-L1 expression score from uPath was 1.2 percentage points (pp) higher than the pathologist's assessment. Discrepancies varied by TPS group, with mean differences of 0.2 pp, 4.4 pp, and 1.95 pp for TPS <1%, 1-49%, and ≥50%, respectively. Category changes occurred in 6% (20/333) of cases, potentially altering immunotherapy eligibility. AI-assisted assessment demonstrates high concordance with the expert pathologist, particularly in binary classification. However, due to discrepancies in the intermediate range, the algorithm is currently best positioned as a supportive tool for verifying borderline cases.

</details>


### [45] [Efficient Method for High-Throughput Screening of Compound Libraries Targeting Human PD-L1.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41970812/)
*Mohammad Reza Zamani,Kateřina Čermáková,Martin Hadzima,Pavel Šácha,Jan Konvalinka*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为DIANA的高通量筛选平台，用于发现针对PD-1/PD-L1相互作用的小分子抑制剂。该平台结合竞争性结合与qPCR检测，具有高灵敏度、宽动态范围和良好的鲁棒性。尽管在初步筛选中未确认有效命中化合物，但DIANA被证明是加速下一代PD-1/PD-L1抑制剂发现的有力工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对PD-1/PD-L1免疫检查点的单克隆抗体虽然临床成功，但存在生产成本高、肿瘤渗透性差及潜在免疫原性等局限。因此，亟需开发能够识别和表征低分子量抑制剂的高效筛选方法，以克服现有疗法的不足。

Method: 开发了DNA连接抑制剂抗体测定法（DIANA），整合竞争性结合与qPCR检测技术，实现单孔测定解离常数（Kd）及快速筛选数千种化合物。使用三种FDA批准的单抗（atezolizumab, avelumab, durvalumab）、宏环肽WL12及天然PD-1受体进行验证，并应用于两个化合物库（5,280种内部化合物和1,298种商业拟肽库）的筛选。

Result: DIANA展现出超过4个数量级的宽动态范围，优异的鲁棒性（Z'-factor = 0.94）及对DMSO的高耐受性（高达10%）。验证实验获得的Kd值与文献一致。在化合物库筛选中，虽检测到极弱的初始命中，但在后续手动实验及正交细胞基测定中均未得到确认。

Conclusion: 尽管本次筛选未发现确证的有效小分子抑制剂，但DIANA凭借其高灵敏度、可扩展性及微量样品需求，确立了其作为加速下一代PD-1/PD-L1抑制剂发现、克服传统筛选方法局限性的强大工具地位。

Abstract: The PD-1/PD-L1 immune checkpoint is a pivotal target for cancer immunotherapy. Monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) targeting the PD-1/PD-L1 interaction have achieved clinical success but face limitations, including high production costs, suboptimal tumor penetration, and potential immunogenicity. To address these challenges, we present the DNA-linked Inhibitor Antibody Assay (DIANA)a robust, high-throughput screening platform optimized for identifying and characterizing low-molecular-weight inhibitors of human PD-L1. DIANA integrates competitive binding with qPCR detection, enabling single-well determination of dissociation constants (K d) and rapid screening of thousands of compounds. The assay was validated using three FDA-approved mAbs (atezolizumab, avelumab, and durvalumab), the PD-L1-binding macrocyclic peptide WL12, and the native PD-1 receptor, yielding K d values consistent with the literature. DIANA demonstrated a broad dynamic range spanning more than 4 orders of magnitude, excellent robustness (Z'-factor = 0.94), and high tolerance to DMSO (up to 10%). We applied DIANA to screen two libraries: a 5,280-compound in-house library (pooled format) and a 1,298-compound commercial peptidomimetic library (individual format). While very weak initial hits were detected, none were confirmed in follow-up manual (non-HTS) experiments or in an orthogonal cell-based assay. Nonetheless, DIANA's sensitivity, scalability, and minimal sample requirements establish it as a powerful tool for accelerating the discovery of next-generation PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitors and overcoming key limitations of conventional screening methods.

2. UTMC联合抗PD-L1治疗显著优化了肿瘤免疫微环境：增加CD8+ T细胞浸润，降低T细胞耗竭，减少调节性T细胞（Treg）浸润，促进巨噬细胞从M2型向M1型极化，并提高促炎细胞因子水平。

Conclusion: UTMC是一种有前景的辅助治疗策略，能够通过缓解缺氧和改善免疫微环境来增强抗PD-L1免疫疗法在TNBC中的疗效，支持进一步开展基于UTMC的联合策略的转化研究。

Abstract: Hypoxia critically restricts the effectiveness of immunotherapy in triple-negative breast cancer (TNBC). Comprehensive bioinformatics analyses demonstrated that highly hypoxic TNBC tumors exhibited elevated T cell exhaustion, increased immune checkpoint molecule expression, and diminished responsiveness to immune checkpoint blockade (ICB). Consequently, strategies aimed at alleviating tumor hypoxia may effectively augment ICB therapy. Although ultrasound-targeted microbubble cavitation (UTMC) has been shown to reduce tumor hypoxia, the precise molecular mechanisms remain unclear. Here, we provided evidence that UTMC activated endothelial nitric oxide synthase (eNOS) through G protein-coupled signaling, resembling pathways induced by fluid shear stress. UTMC-induced eNOS activation was largely Ca²⁺-dependent and resulted in increased nitric oxide production. Enhanced nitric oxide generation was associated with improved tumor perfusion and reduced hypoxia. Combining UTMC with anti-PD-L1 therapy markedly improved the tumor immune microenvironment, characterized by increased CD8+ T cell infiltration, reduced T cell exhaustion, diminished regulatory T cell infiltration, increased macrophage polarization from an M2 to M1 phenotype, and elevated production of pro-inflammatory cytokines. Collectively, our findings identified UTMC as a promising adjunctive therapeutic approach to mitigate hypoxia and enhance the efficacy of anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy in TNBC. These results support further translational evaluation of UTMC-based combination strategies in hypoxic TNBC.

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期mRNA测序分析提示泛素醌氧化还原酶亚基A10（NDUFA10）可能是环状RNA circRAPGEF5在肺腺癌（LUAD）进展中的关键下游效应因子，但其具体作用机制及临床意义尚不明确。因此，本研究旨在深入探讨NDUFA10在LUAD细胞中的表达模式、临床相关性及其对细胞增殖和转移的功能影响。

2. **实验验证**：通过逆转录定量PCR（RT-qPCR）在LUAD细胞系中验证表达水平。
3. **功能实验**：在体外细胞水平上，通过敲低NDUFA10，检测其对LUAD细胞增殖、克隆形成、迁移和侵袭能力的影响。

Result: 1. **表达与临床特征**：NDUFA10在泛癌分析及LUAD中显著上调；其高表达与转移（M分期）、患者性别、晚期N分期（N2/N3）、高病理分期（III/IV）及高龄显著相关。
2. **预后与治疗反应**：NDUFA10高表达与总生存期（OS）和无进展生存期（FP）呈负相关，并与抗PD-1/PD-L1抗体疗法的疗效有关联。
3. **通路富集**：GSEA显示NDUFA10相关基因显著富集于WNT、Notch信号通路以及调节上皮-间质转化（EMT）的TGFβ通路。
4. **功能验证**：体外敲低NDUFA10显著抑制了LUAD细胞的增殖、克隆形成、迁移和侵袭能力。

Conclusion: 沉默NDUFA10能够有效抑制LUAD细胞的增殖、迁移和侵袭。研究结果表明，circRAPGEF5可能通过调控NDUFA10来促进LUAD的进展。NDUFA10的高表达预示不良诊断和预后，潜在机制涉及WNT/Notch/TGFβ通路或ROS/能量代谢途径，为LUAD的治疗提供了新的潜在靶点。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: mRNA sequencing analysis suggested that ubiquinone oxidoreductase subunit A10 (NDUFA10) may function as a key downstream effector of circRAPGEF5 in lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD) progression; however, the underlying mechanism remains unclear. This study aimed to explore the expression and clinical significance of NDUFA10 in LUAD cells and its role in cell proliferation and metastasis.

METHODS: Variations in NDUFA10 mRNA and protein expression between LUAD and adjacent normal tissues were assessed using the Cancer Genome Atlas Program (TCGA) and Tumor Immune Estimation Resource (TIMER) databases. These findings were subsequently validated in LUAD cell lines via Reverse Transcription Quantitative PCR (RT-qPCR). Further analyses included clinical correlation, Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC), survival analysis, and Gene Set Enrichment Analysis (GSEA) of NDUFA10. The functional role of NDUFA10 was investigated in vitro at the cellular level.

RESULTS: NDUFA10 mRNA expression was significantly upregulated in pan-cancer analyses and specifically in LUAD. Its expression levels correlated with clinical parameters, including metastasis (M stage) and patient gender. Elevated NDUFA10 expression demonstrated a negative correlation with overall survival (OS) and first progression (FP), and was associated with the best efficacy of anti-PD-1/PD-L1 antibody therapies. GSEA indicated significant enrichment of NDUFA10-related genes in pathways involving WNT and Notch signaling, as well as the TGFβ pathway regulating the epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT). In vitro, knockdown of NDUFA10 significantly suppressed LUAD cell proliferation, colony formation, migration, and invasion compared to control cells.

DISCUSSION: This study focuses on the role of NDUFA10 in lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD). Through database screening and experimental verification, it was found that NDUFA10 is upregulated in LUAD. Its high expression is associated with advanced N stage (N2/N3), high pathological stage (III/IV), poor therapeutic effect and advanced age, suggesting its potential as a biomarker for poor diagnosis and prognosis. Further analysis indicates that NDUFA10 is associated with immune infiltration and involves pathways such as neuroactive ligand-receptor interaction and chemokine signaling. In vitro experiments have confirmed that knocking down NDUFA10 can inhibit the proliferation and metastasis of lung adenocarcinoma cells. This study provides a preliminary basis for the function and mechanism of NDUFA10 in LUAD. In the future, we will further explore how it affects tumor progression through the WNT/Notch/TGFβ pathway or ROS/ energy metabolism.

CONCLUSION: Silencing NDUFA10 suppresses LUAD cell proliferation, migration, and invasion. The study suggested that circRAPGEF5 regulated NDUFA10 to facilitate the progression of LUAD.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症相关成纤维细胞（CAFs）在胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）微环境中起关键作用，促进肿瘤进展和治疗抵抗，但目前对PDAC中CAF膜蛋白的表达图谱定义尚不明确，缺乏基于CAF膜蛋白的特异性预后标志物。

Method: 整合scRNA-seq（33例，87,949个细胞）、空间转录组（2例，7,011个斑点）和bulk RNA-seq（7例，642个样本）数据，系统鉴定PDAC特异性CAF膜基因。利用LASSO-Cox模型构建预后签名PaFMS，并通过多队列验证、功能富集、免疫浸润分析、药物敏感性分析及免疫治疗响应评估进行综合验证。

Result: 鉴定出与EMT和血管生成紧密相关的PDAC富集myoCAF-c1簇，从中定义33个候选CAF膜基因，其蛋白相互作用主要涉及细胞外基质组织和胶原重塑。构建了11基因预后签名PaFMS，在六个独立队列中稳健分层患者，高预测准确性。高风险组表现为增殖信号激活、免疫抑制及T/B细胞浸润减少。PaFMS与33种抗癌药物反应相关，并预测低风险组从抗PD-L1免疫治疗中获益更多。验证了PLAU、TMEM158和TRIM59等基因的表达特异性。

Conclusion: myoCAF-c1亚群通过促进血管生成推动肿瘤进展；建立的PaFMS是一个基于CAF膜基因的稳健预后模型，具有用于PDAC精准预后和治疗决策（包括化疗和免疫治疗）的潜在临床价值。

Abstract: Cancer-associated fibroblasts (CAFs) are central to the pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma (PDAC) microenvironment, promoting tumor progression and therapeutic resistance. However, the expression landscape of CAF membrane proteins in PDAC remains poorly defined. We integrated scRNA-seq (n = 33; 87,949 cells), spatial transcriptomics (n = 2; 7,011 spots), and bulk RNA-seq (n = 7; 642 samples) to systematically identify PDAC-specific CAF membrane genes. A LASSO-based Cox model was developed to construct a prognostic signature, PaFMS, and evaluated through multi-cohort validation. Functional enrichment, immune infiltration, drug sensitivity, and immunotherapy response analyses were further conducted. Validation was performed using multiple database-driven analyses. We identified a PDAC-enriched myoCAF-c1 cluster closely associated with epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT) and angiogenesis. From this cluster, 33 candidate CAF membrane genes were defined, whose protein-protein interactions were predominantly linked to extracellular matrix organization and collagen remodeling, and spatially colocalized with myoCAF-c1 and EMT regions. An 11-gene prognostic signature, PaFMS that robustly stratified patients across six independent cohorts, achieving high predictive accuracy for overall survival. High-risk patients exhibited proliferative signaling activation, immune suppression, and reduced T/B-cell infiltration. PaFMS was associated with responses to 33 anticancer agents and predicted enhanced benefit from anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy in the low-risk group. Multi-cohort validation confirmed the expression specificity of PaFMS genes, including PLAU, TMEM158, and TRIM59. Together, these findings reveal that myoCAF-c1 promotes angiogenesis and tumor progression, and establish PaFMS as a robust CAF membrane-based prognostic model in PDAC with potential utility for precision prognosis and therapeutic decision-making. KEY MESSAGES: Integrated single-cell, spatial, and bulk RNA-seq analyses identified PDAC-specific CAF membrane genes. Discovered a PDAC-enriched myoCAF-c1 subtype linked to EMT and angiogenesis. Developed an 11-gene CAF membrane-based prognostic model (PaFMS) validated across six cohorts. PaFMS predicts patient survival, drug sensitivity, and immunotherapy response in PDAC.

</details>


### [49] [From clusters to clinic: An 8-gene signature combined with mucinous component stratifies benefit of anti-CTLA-4 addition to anti-PD-1 in dMMR/MSI-H metastatic colorectal cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41950572/)
*Margherita Ambrosini,Claire Gallois,Annalice Gandini,Aurélie Lecanu,Emily Alouani,Filippo Pietrantonio,Francesca Bergamo,Chiara Cremolini,Thibault Mazard,David Tougeron,Rosine Guimbaud,Sara Lonardi,Janick Selves,Sophie Mouillet-Richard,Jean-Francois Emile,Julien Taieb,Pierre Laurent-Puig*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发并验证了一种结合8基因特征（k-TSP分类器）与粘液性表型的复合生物标志物，用于预测dMMR/MSI-H转移性结直肠癌患者对抗CTLA-4联合抗PD-1免疫治疗的获益情况。结果显示，被归类为Cluster A且伴有粘液性成分的患者从联合治疗中显著受益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）是dMMR/MSI-H转移性结直肠癌（mCRC）的标准治疗，但仍有15-50%的患者出现原发性或继发性耐药。此前研究发现转录组Cluster A（“StromalHIGH - ProliferationLOW”）与较短的无进展生存期（PFS）相关。因此，亟需一种能够精准预测哪些患者能从抗CTLA-4联合抗PD-1治疗中获益的生物标志物，以优化治疗方案选择。

Method: 研究利用两个独立队列（队列1：103例；队列2：87例）中接受抗PD-1±抗LAG-3或抗PD-1+抗CTLA-4治疗的dMMR/MSI-H mCRC患者的治疗前3'RNA测序数据。在队列1中训练基于排名的k-Top Scoring Pairs (k-TSP) 分类器，以区分Cluster A与Cluster B/C，并在队列2中进行验证。研究定义了一个复合分层标准，结合k-TSP分类结果（A vs B/C）与粘液性成分（存在 vs 缺失），分析其与治疗效果的交互作用。

Result: Cluster A富集了粘液性肿瘤且在两个队列中均显示PFS较短。k-TSP分类器筛选出4个预测Cluster A的基因对（YPEL2>H2AZ1, H6PD>COX7A2, STAT5B>SNRPE, ZHX3>TRAPPC4），在队列2中的平衡准确率为68.1%。在合并人群（163例）中，复合分层与治疗交互作用显著改善了模型拟合度。特别是对于“k-TSP Cluster A + 粘液性”亚组（占15.3%），联合治疗相比单药治疗显著延长了PFS（调整后的24个月PFS为72.2% vs 13.8%，HR 0.10，p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 由8个基因组成的签名结合粘液性成分，能够识别出从抗PD-1联合抗CTLA-4治疗中优先获益的特定患者亚群。该发现具有重要的临床指导意义，建议进行前瞻性验证以指导dMMR/MSI-H mCRC患者的 regimen 选择。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Around 15-50% of patients with dMMR/MSI-H metastatic colorectal cancer (mCRC) experience primary and secondary resistance to immune checkpoint inhibitors (ICI). We previously described three transcriptomic clusters (A, B, C) linked to progression-free survival (PFS), with cluster A ("StromalHIGH - ProliferationLOW") associated with the shortest PFS. Here we combine gene signature and mucinous phenotype to predict the benefit from anti-CTLA-4 plus anti-PD-1.

METHODS: We used 3'RNA-sequencing data from pre-treatment samples of two independent cohorts of patients with dMMR/MSI-H mCRC treated with anti-PD-1 ±anti-LAG-3 ("Mono") or anti-PD-1 +anti-CTLA-4 ("Combo"). A rank-based k-Top Scoring Pairs (k-TSP) classifier was trained in Cohort 1 (103 patients) to predict assignment to Cluster A versus B/C and validated in Cohort 2 (87 patients). Patients receiving an anti-PD-L1 and with ECOG ≥ 2 were excluded. We defined a composite stratum crossing Clusterk-TSP (A vs B/C) with mucinous component (present vs absent).

RESULTS: Cluster A was enriched for mucinous tumors and had shorter PFS in both cohorts. The k-TSP selected four gene pairs predictive of Cluster A (YPEL2 >H2AZ1, H6PD>COX7A2, STAT5B>SNRPE, ZHX3 >TRAPPC4) and was validated in Cohort 2 with 68.1% balanced accuracy. In the pooled population (163 patients), the composite stratum × treatment interaction improved model fit (likelihood-ratio p ≈ 0.04). Patients with Clusterk-TSP A + mucinous (25/163, 15.3%) derived advantage from Combo versus Mono: adjusted 24-month PFS 72.2% vs 13.8% (HR 0.10, 95% CI 0.02-0.39; p < 0.001).

CONCLUSIONS: An 8-Gene signature combined with mucinous component identifies a subgroup that preferentially benefits from anti-PD-1 +CTLA-4, warranting prospective validation to guide regimen selection in dMMR/MSI-H mCRC.

</details>


### [50] [Durvalumab plus tremelimumab for the treatment of patients with progressive, refractory advanced thyroid carcinoma: the phase II GETNE-DUTHY trial.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41935063/)
*Jaume Capdevila,Jorge Hernando,Javier Molina-Cerillo,Maria Plana Serrahima,Miren Taberna Sanz,Beatriz Castelo,Cristina Álvarez-Escolá,Alberto Carmona-Bayonas,Inmaculada Ballester Navarro,Lara Iglesias,Mateo Bover,Alejandro Garcia-Alvarez,Javier Lavernia,Ricardo Yayá Tur,Sofia Ruiz,Neus Baste,Isabel Lorenzo-Lorenzo,Alfredo Sanchez-Hernandez,Enrique Sanz-Garcia,Gloria Marquina,Paolo Nuciforo,Belén Elguero,Enrique Grande,Teresa Alonso-Gordoa*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: GETNE-DUTHY II期临床试验评估了Durvalumab联合Tremelimumab在晚期甲状腺癌中的疗效。结果显示，该双免疫疗法在分化型（DTC）和髓样（MTC）甲状腺癌中未达到主要终点，但在未分化甲状腺癌（ATC）患者中达到了6个月总生存率的主要终点，客观缓解率达33%，且安全性良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管单药PD-1阻断剂在晚期甲状腺癌中显示出一定的疗效，但研究旨在探索通过Durvalumab（抗PD-L1）和Tremelimumab（抗CTLA-4）进行双重免疫检查点抑制，是否能比单药治疗进一步改善晚期甲状腺癌患者的临床预后。

Method: 这是一项II期、单臂、多队列临床试验（GETNE-DUTHY）。研究招募了三个平行队列：队列1为分化型甲状腺癌（DTC，n=37），队列2为髓样甲状腺癌（MTC，n=30），队列3为未分化甲状腺癌（ATC，n=12）。队列1和2纳入既往标准全身治疗后进展的患者，队列3不限既往治疗史。主要终点为队列1和2的6个月无进展生存率（PFSR）以及队列3的6个月总生存率（OSR）。次要终点包括客观缓解率（ORR）、PFS、OS及安全性。

Result: 队列1（DTC）和队列2（MTC）的6个月PFSR分别为32.4%和40.8%；队列3（ATC）的6个月OSR为65.6%。各队列的ORR分别为8%、10%和33%。研究未观察到额外的安全信号。

Conclusion: Durvalumab联合Tremelimumab治疗未分化甲状腺癌（ATC）达到了本研究的主要终点，显示出令人鼓舞的生存结果和较高的客观缓解率，表明该双免疫疗法在ATC中具有潜在临床价值，而在DTC和MTC中的疗效相对有限。

Abstract: Single-agent PD-1 blockade demonstrated promising efficacy in advanced thyroid cancer. The phase II, single-arm, multi-cohort GETNE-DUTHY trial (clinicaltrials.gov NCT03753919, EudraCT 2018-001066-42) aimed to determine whether dual anti-PD-L1/CTLA-4 inhibition using durvalumab and tremelimumab can improve the clinical outcomes in advanced thyroid cancer. Three parallel cohorts including patients with differentiated thyroid carcinoma (Cohort 1, n = 37), medullary thyroid carcinoma (Cohort 2, n = 30), and anaplastic thyroid carcinoma (Cohort 3, n = 12) were recruited. Cohort 1 and 2 included patients following progression to previous standard systemic therapy and in Cohort 3 were recruited regardless of previous therapy. The primary endpoint was 6-month progression-free survival rate for Cohort 1 and 2 and 6-month overall survival rate for Cohort 3. Secondary endpoints included objective response rate, progression-free survival, overall survival, and safety. The 6-month progression-free survival rates were 32.4% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 20.4-51.6) (Cohort 1) and 40.8% (95% CI: 26.3-63.6) (Cohort 2); 6-month overall survival rate was 65.6% (95% CI: 43.2-99.8) (Cohort 3). The objective response rates were 8%, 10%, and 33% for Cohort 1, 2, and 3, respectively. No additional safety signals observed. Durvalumab plus tremelimumab treatment in patients with anaplastic thyroid carcinoma met the primary endpoint of this study, showing encouraging survival outcomes.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 进行了一项单中心、回顾性真实世界分析，纳入2024年5月至2025年1月期间开始接受一线免疫检查点抑制剂单药治疗（帕博利珠单抗或阿替利珠单抗）的125名高PD-L1表达转移性NSCLC患者。使用Kaplan-Meier法估算无进展生存期（PFS），并通过log-rank检验进行比较；应用单变量和多变量Cox比例风险回归模型识别与PFS相关的临床因素。

Result: 帕博利珠单抗组（n=73）的中位PFS在数值上长于阿替利珠单抗组（n=52）（9.93个月 vs 5.33个月），但差异无统计学意义（HR 1.14, 95% CI 0.74-1.74; p = 0.559）。多变量分析显示，较差的体能状态（ECOG PS 2）与PFS缩短独立相关（HR 3.22），而极高的PD-L1表达（TPS 90-100%）与PFS改善独立相关（HR 0.57）。

Conclusion: 在该真实世界队列中，帕博利珠单抗与阿替利珠单抗相比，PFS在数值上更长，但无统计学显著差异。治疗效果主要受基线临床因素驱动，尤其是体能状态和PD-L1表达程度。需要更大样本量和更长随访时间的研究来明确潜在差异并评估长期结果。

Abstract: Background: Immune checkpoint inhibitor monotherapy targeting the PD-1/PD-L1 axis is a standard first-line treatment option for patients with metastatic non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) and high PD-L1 expression. However, comparative real-world data evaluating PD-1 versus PD-L1 inhibitor monotherapy in this population remain limited. Methods: We conducted a retrospective, single-center real-world analysis of patients with metastatic NSCLC and high PD-L1 expression who initiated first-line immune checkpoint inhibitor monotherapy between May 2024 and January 2025. Patients were treated with either pembrolizumab (PD-1 inhibitor) or atezolizumab (PD-L1 inhibitor) as monotherapy. Progression-free survival (PFS) was estimated using the Kaplan-Meier method and compared using the log-rank test. Univariable and multivariable Cox proportional hazards regression models were applied to identify clinical factors associated with PFS. Results: A total of 125 patients were included, of whom 73 received pembrolizumab and 52 received atezolizumab. The median PFS was numerically longer in patients treated with pembrolizumab compared with atezolizumab (9.93 vs. 5.33 months), although this difference did not reach statistical significance (HR 1.14, 95% CI 0.74-1.74; p = 0.559). Poor performance status (ECOG PS 2) was independently associated with inferior PFS (HR 3.22, 95% CI 2.02-5.12), whereas very high PD-L1 expression (TPS 90-100%) was independently associated with improved PFS (HR 0.57, 95% CI 0.35-0.93). Conclusions: In this real-world cohort, pembrolizumab was associated with numerically longer PFS than atezolizumab; however, this difference was not statistically significant. Outcomes were primarily driven by baseline clinical factors, particularly performance status and the degree of PD-L1 expression. Larger cohorts with longer follow-up are warranted to clarify potential differences and assess long-term outcomes.

### [52] [GLT8D2-mediated PD-L1 N-glycosylation promotes tumor immune evasion in ovarian cancer peritoneal metastasis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41936856/)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌（OC）腹膜转移常见且预后不良，蛋白质异常糖基化与免疫抑制及肿瘤转移密切相关，但关于卵巢癌腹膜转移中糖基转移酶相关生物标志物的报道较少，亟需探索其机制及临床价值。

Method: 结合单细胞RNA测序技术与临床样本分析，鉴定GLT8D2的表达特征及其与免疫通路的相关性；通过分子机制实验验证GLT8D2与PD-L1的相互作用及特定位点（Asn192）的N-糖基化修饰；利用体内实验评估GLT8D2敲低与抗PD-L1疗法的协同效应；鉴定HIF-1α为GLT8D2的直接转录激活因子。

Result: 1. GLT8D2在转移性卵巢癌组织中显著上调，且与免疫通路呈负相关。
2. GLT8D2介导PD-L1在Asn192位点的N-糖基化，增强PD-L1稳定性。
3. PD-L1糖基化促进肿瘤细胞迁移和侵袭，导致CD8+ T细胞浸润减少及T细胞耗竭标志物（LAG3, PD-1）上调。
4. GLT8D2敲低在体内与抗PD-L1疗法协同减少肿瘤转移。
5. HIF-1α直接转录激活GLT8D2。

Conclusion: GLT8D2是卵巢癌转移和免疫逃逸的关键调节因子，通过HIF-1α/GLT8D2/PD-L1轴发挥作用。靶向GLT8D2可能成为增强免疫治疗效果、改善卵巢癌患者预后的新策略。

Abstract: Peritoneal metastasis is the most common form of metastasis in ovarian cancer (OC), resulting in a poor prognosis. Abnormal modification of proteins by glycosyltransferases is closely related to immunosuppression and tumor metastasis. However, there were few reports on glycosyltransferase related biomarkers of peritoneal metastasis in OC. This study investigates the role of glycosyltransferase GLT8D2 in promoting peritoneal metastasis and tumor immune evasion through PD-L1 N-glycosylation in OC. Using single-cell RNA sequencing and clinical samples, we found that GLT8D2 is significantly upregulated in metastatic OC tissues and negatively correlated with immune pathways. Mechanistically, GLT8D2 interacts with PD-L1, mediates its N-glycosylation at the Asn192 site, thereby enhancing PD-L1 stability. The N-glycosylation of PD-L1 facilitated tumor cell migration and invasion, accompanied by decreased CD8+ T cell infiltration and upregulated expression of T cell exhaustion markers including LAG3 and PD-1. Furthermore, GLT8D2 knockdown synergized with anti-PD-L1 therapy to reduce tumor metastasis in vivo. Hypoxia-inducible factor HIF-1α was identified as a direct transcriptional activator of GLT8D2. Our findings highlight GLT8D2 as a key regulator of metastasis and immune evasion in OC, offering a potential strategy to improve prognosis.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共价蛋白药物虽具治疗潜力，但受限于靶点结合速度慢以及缺乏高通量筛选平台。实现快速共价结合需要对亲和力、稳定性和弹头几何结构进行协调优化，这是一个内在的多维挑战，亟需有效的技术手段来解决。

Method: 开发了一个耦合化学选择性修饰的酵母展示平台。该系统允许在不增加内在弹头反应性的情况下，对快速作用的共价蛋白进行定向选择和工程化改造。

Result: 1. 工程化了一种共价PD-L1拮抗纳米抗体，表现出快速的交联动力学（kobs = 0.18 min-1, t1/2 = 3.8 min），且肿瘤抑制效果优于envafolimab和atezolizumab。
2. 工程化了快速作用的共价IL-18（kobs = 0.54 min-1, t1/2 = 1.3 min）。
3. 开发了针对SARS-CoV-2受体结合域（RBD）的共价迷你蛋白。
4. 证明了该平台在不同蛋白质模态中的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该酵母展示平台有效克服了共价蛋白药物开发的瓶颈，能够高效筛选出具有快速结合动力学的共价蛋白，并在多种治疗靶点（包括癌症免疫检查点、细胞因子及病毒抗原）中展示了优异的性能和通用性。

Abstract: Covalent protein drugs offer therapeutic potential but are limited by slow target engagement and the absence of high-throughput selection platforms. Rapid covalent binding requires coordinated optimization of affinity, stability, and warhead geometry-an intrinsically multidimensional challenge. We develop a yeast display platform coupled with chemoselective modification that enables selection of fast-acting covalent proteins without increasing intrinsic warhead reactivity. Using this system, we engineered a covalent programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) antagonistic nanobody with rapid crosslinking kinetics (kobs = 0.18 min-1, t1/2 = 3.8 min) and improved tumor suppression compared with envafolimab and atezolizumab. Similarly, we engineered a fast-acting covalent interleukin-18 (IL-18) (kobs = 0.54 min-1, t1/2 = 1.3 min) and a covalent miniprotein targeting the receptor binding domain (RBD) of SARS-CoV-2, demonstrating applicability across protein modalities.

</details>

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: CLINATEZO研究在真实世界环境中验证了阿替利珠单抗联合铂类化疗治疗广泛期小细胞肺癌（ES-SCLC）的疗效，结果与关键临床试验一致。长期缓解（rwPFS > 12个月）与既往吸烟史和良好体能状态相关，而长期生存（OS > 24个月）则与年轻年龄、初诊时局限期及既往铂类化疗史相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广泛期小细胞肺癌（ES-SCLC）具有高度侵袭性且预后较差，标准一线治疗为铂类-依托泊苷化疗联合抗PD-L1免疫检查点抑制剂。尽管关键临床试验已确立该方案的有效性，但仍需在真实世界环境中验证其可重复性，并探索预测长期响应和长期生存的特定因素，以优化患者管理。

Method: 这是一项名为CLINATEZO (IFCT-1905) 的全国性、非干预性、回顾性研究。研究对象为2019年5月至2020年3月期间参与法国早期访问计划、接受阿替利珠单抗联合铂类化疗的连续ES-SCLC患者。研究评估了长期响应（定义为真实世界无进展生存期 [rwPFS] > 12个月）和长期生存（定义为总生存期 [OS] > 24个月）的预测因子。

Result: 共纳入来自65个中心的517名患者，中位随访时间为53.8个月。中位rwPFS为5.2个月，12个月和48个月rwPFS率分别为14.6%和6.8%。中位OS为11.3个月，24个月和48个月OS率分别为21.1%和11.4%。12.6%的患者实现长期响应，其基线特征包括既往吸烟者（相较于当前吸烟者）和ECOG体能状态0-1分。20.5%的患者实现长期生存，预测因子包括较年轻年龄、初诊时为局限期SCLC以及既往接受过铂类化疗。长期响应者的中位OS显著长于非长期响应者（53.7个月 vs 9.7个月）。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Extensive-stage SCLC (ES-SCLC) is a highly aggressive type of lung cancer with a high risk of early progression and limited survival. Standard of care for first-line treatment is platinum-etoposide chemotherapy plus anti-PD-L1 immune checkpoint inhibitors.

METHODS: Intergroupe Francophone de Cancérologie Thoracique-1905 CLINATEZO is a nationwide, non-interventional, retrospective study of consecutive patients with ES-SCLC who received atezolizumab plus platinum-based chemotherapy as part of the French Early Access Program that ran from May 2019 to March 2020. In this analysis, predictors of long-term response (defined by a real-world progression-free survival [rwPFS] > 12 mo) and long-term survival (defined by an overall survival [OS] > 24 mo) were assessed.

RESULTS: A total of 517 patients were enrolled from 65 centers. After a median follow-up of 53.8 months, median, 12-month, and 48-month rwPFS rates were 5.2 (95% confidence interval [CI]: 5.0-5.4) months, 14.6% (95% CI: 11.6-17.9), and 6.8% (95% CI: 4.7-9.4), respectively. Median, 24-month, and 48-month OS rates were 11.3 (95% CI: 10.1-12.4) months, 21.1% (95% CI: 17.7-24.8), and 11.4% (95% CI: 8.8-14.4), respectively.Long-term response was observed in 12.6% patients. Median OS was 9.7 months in patients with rwPFS less than or equal to 12 months and 53.7 months in patients with rwPFS more than 12 months. Baseline characteristics associated with rwPFS more than 12 months were being a former smoker (versus current smoker, p = 0.003) and having an Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group performance status of 0 to 1 (versus 2, p = 0.048).Long-term survival was observed in 20.5% patients. Median OS was 8.8 months in patients with OS less than or equal to 24 months and 52.9 months in patients with OS more than 24 months. Predictors of long-term survival were younger age (p = 0.030), limited stage at initial diagnosis of SCLC (p = 0.022), and previous line of platinum-based chemotherapy (p = 0.01).

CONCLUSIONS: CLINATEZO demonstrates the reproducibility of the key survival outcomes of landmark trials, in a real-life setting, for patients with ES-SCLC. Only low smoking history and performance status were associated with long-term response in our cohort.

</details>


### [55] [Real-World Evidence of Atezolizumab Efficacy as Part of First-Line Treatment for Extensive-Stage SCLC in Bulgaria.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41976352/)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IMpower133试验确立了阿替利珠单抗作为广泛期小细胞肺癌（ES-SCLC）一线标准治疗的一部分，但积累真实世界证据（RWE）对于验证临床试验结果、反映常规临床实践中的疗效差异以及进一步改善患者预后至关重要。

Result: 共纳入302名患者。真实世界研究中的中位无进展生存期（PFS）为7.6个月，高于IMpower133试验的5.2个月，且6个月后的PFS率更高。客观缓解率（ORR）为38.7%，低于试验组的65.4%，研究者认为这主要源于真实世界文档记录的实践差异。

Conclusion: 关于PFS和治疗反应的真实世界证据总体上与IMpower133试验报告的阿替利珠单抗加入ES-SCLC一线治疗的疗效一致，同时也反映了常规临床实践中可能出现的差异。

Abstract: Background/Objectives: Small cell lung cancer (SCLC) is a highly metastatic and lethal malignancy. Doublet chemotherapy consisting of a platinum compound and etoposide had remained the standard first-line regimen for decades, particularly for the majority of patients, who are diagnosed with extensive-stage disease. Over the past 5 years, results from the IMpower133 trial led to the approval of anti-PD-L1 agent atezolizumab as part of the first-line regimen for extensive-stage SCLC. The accumulation of real-world evidence on atezolizumab efficacy in this context is essential for corroborating trial results and further improving patient outcomes. Herein, we present real-world evidence from a Bulgarian cohort receiving atezolizumab as part of first-line extensive-stage SCLC treatment, comparing these data to the results of IMpower133. Methods: Data were extracted from the electronic health records of patients receiving atezolizumab plus chemotherapy as first-line treatment for extensive-stage SCLC in the period between 1 January 2022 and 31 December 2024. Analysis was conducted via the Danny Platform, an analytic platform for the integration of real-world evidence using embedded machine learning and LLM algorithms. Patients matching IMpower133 inclusion criteria were included in a group for comparison with trial results. Results: A total of 302 patients were included in the analysis. The real-world progression-free survival (PFS) in our study surpassed that in IMpower133 (7.6 versus 5.2 months), in addition to higher PFS rates beyond six months. A lower Objective Response Rate (ORR) (38.7% versus 65.4%) was noted in our real-world cohort, which we attribute to real-world documentation practices. Conclusions: Real-world evidence regarding PFS and treatment response generally align with the efficacy of adding atezolizumab to first-line extensive-stage SCLC treatment reported in the IMpower133 trial, while reflecting differences that may arise in routine clinical practice.

### [56] [Durvalumab combined with weekly carboplatin/paclitaxel as first-line treatment of patients with recurrent and/or metastatic HNSCC not eligible for cisplatin-based chemotherapies: results of the multicenter prospective study FRAIL-IMMUNE.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41962312/)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前R/M HNSCC的标准治疗（帕博利珠单抗联合铂类-5-FU或单药）存在显著毒性，特别是基于顺铂的化疗，导致虚弱患者无法耐受。此外，欧洲仅限PD-L1阳性患者使用，而美国无此限制。因此，亟需为不适合顺铂治疗的虚弱患者探索一种有效且耐受性良好的一线替代治疗方案。

Method: 这是一项前瞻性、多中心、单臂二期研究。研究对象为不适合标准顺铂化疗的R/M HNSCC患者，分为两个队列：队列A（ECOG PS 0-1）和队列B（ECOG PS 2，虚弱）。治疗方案为度伐利尤单抗（1500 mg，每4周一次，最长12个月）联合每周卡铂（AUC2）和紫杉醇（80 mg/m²，第1、8、15天），共4个周期。主要终点为12个月总生存率（OS），采用Fleming A'Hern设计设定无效边界和目标疗效阈值。

Result: 队列A纳入64名ECOG PS 1患者，其中40名在12个月时存活（12个月OS率为62.5%），超过预设目标。队列B纳入40名ECOG PS 2患者（39名可评估），其中20名在12个月时存活（12个月OS率为51.3%），同样达到主要终点。研究成功拒绝了无效假设。

Conclusion: 度伐利尤单抗联合每周卡铂/紫杉醇方案在ECOG PS 0-1及2的虚弱R/M HNSCC患者中显示出令人鼓舞的疗效和耐受性。结果支持在对比性三期临床试验中进一步调查该组合作为一线治疗的潜力。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Pembrolizumab, combined with platinum-5-FU or as a monotherapy, is the standard treatment of patients with recurrent and/or metastatic (R/M) head and neck squamous-cell carcinoma (HNSCC). This treatment is used exclusively in programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1)-positive patients in Europe, whereas it is used in the USA regardless of PD-L1 status. However, substantial toxicities, notably those related to cisplatin-based chemotherapy, preclude the use of combination therapy in fragile patients. We investigated the efficacy and tolerance of first-line durvalumab combined with weekly carboplatin/paclitaxel in R/M HNSCC patients who were ineligible for cisplatin.

PATIENTS AND METHODS: This prospective multicenter single-arm phase II study assessed tolerance in R/M HNSCC patients with an Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group performance status (ECOG-PS) of 0-1 (run-in phase study feasibility, then cohort A) and ECOG-PS 2 (cohort B), frail, not eligible for standard cisplatin-based chemotherapy. In the first-line, patients received four 28-day chemotherapy cycles (carboplatin AUC2; paclitaxel 80 mg/m2 day 1, 8, and 15) combined with durvalumab 1500 mg every 4 weeks for 12 months maximum. The primary endpoint was 12-month overall survival (OS) rate based on a Fleming A'Hern design: cohort A, P0 inefficacy boundary 47%, P1 target efficacy 65%, at least 38 successes among 64 patients assessable were required for further investigations; cohort B: P0 15%; P1 35%, at least 10 successes needed among 38 patients to reject the null hypothesis.

RESULTS: From 16 May 2019 to 4 March 2021, 64 patients with an ECOG-PS of 1 (cohort A) from 13 French authorized centers (hospital, cancer centers) were included and received study treatments. Cohort B enrolled 40 patients with an ECOG-PS of 2 from 25 Aug 2021 and 9 March 2023 and 39 were assessable. With 40 patients with an ECOG-PS of 0-1 [62.5%, unilateral 95% confidence interval (CI) 51.5%-∞], and 20 patients with an ECOG-PS of 2 (51.3%, unilateral 95% CI 37.1%-∞) alive at 12 months, the study met its primary endpoints.

CONCLUSIONS: The promising results in frail patients with an ECOG-PS of 0-1 and 2 encourages further investigation of durvalumab with weekly carboplatin/paclitaxel in first-line R/M HNSCC patients in a comparative phase III clinical trial.

</details>


### [57] [PVT1-104aa derived from the 8q24 gene desert promotes colorectal cancer tumorigenesis.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41952439/)
*Maoguang Ma,Mingdian Wang,Yufei Yang,Dakui Luo,Weixing Dai,Yiwei Li,Sanjun Cai,Shaobo Mo,Qingguo Li,Xinxiang Li*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究发现环状RNA circPVT1编码的新蛋白PVT1-104aa在结直肠癌（CRC）中高表达并预示不良预后。该蛋白通过抑制c-Myc的泛素化降解来稳定c-Myc，进而上调PD-L1表达，促进肿瘤进展。联合抑制PVT1-104aa与抗PD-L1疗法可显著抑制肿瘤生长并增强T细胞浸润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期结直肠癌的治疗效果仍然不理想，化疗仍是主要手段。鉴于环状RNA（circRNAs）可翻译为调节肿瘤恶性进展的多肽或蛋白，具有成为治疗靶点的潜力，因此亟需探索其在CRC中的具体致病机制及临床转化价值。

Method: 1. **鉴定与表达分析**：鉴定由circPVT1反向剪接生成的新蛋白PVT1-104aa，并在CRC临床样本和细胞系中检测其表达及临床意义。
2. **功能评估**：通过CCK-8、克隆形成、Transwell、伤口愈合实验及异基因移植瘤模型，评估其对增殖、转移和肿瘤生长的影响。
3. **机制研究**：利用免疫共沉淀、泛素化分析及蛋白半衰期测定，探究PVT1-104aa与c-Myc及FBW7的相互作用；通过启动子报告基因、ChIP-qPCR和免疫组化分析PVT1-104aa、c-Myc与PD-L1的关系。
4. **疗效验证**：在体内模型中测试联合抑制PVT1-104aa与抗PD-L1疗法的治疗效果。

Result: 1. **临床相关性**：PVT1-104aa在CRC中显著过表达，且与患者不良预后相关。
2. **生物学功能**：PVT1-104aa通过促进增殖和转移驱动肿瘤进展。
3. **分子机制**：PVT1-104aa增强c-Myc在Ser62位的磷酸化，破坏c-Myc与FBW7的相互作用，从而抑制c-Myc的泛素介导降解，稳定c-Myc水平。此外，PVT1-104aa通过c-Myc调控PD-L1的表达。
4. **治疗效应**：联合抗PVT1-104aa与抗PD-L1疗法在异基因移植瘤模型中抑制了CRC生长，并增加了CD4+和CD8+ T细胞的浸润。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了源自PVT1的分子物种PVT1-104aa的致病作用，证明其通过稳定c-Myc并上调PD-L1促进CRC进展。靶向PVT1-104aa通路，特别是与免疫检查点阻断联合使用，为结直肠癌治疗提供了一种有前景的治疗策略。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Current therapeutic outcomes for advanced colorectal cancer (CRC) remain suboptimal, and chemotherapy-based regimens continue to be the mainstay of treatment. Circular RNAs (circRNAs) can serve as templates for translating short peptides or proteins, and the resulting products actively regulate malignant tumour progression, making them attractive therapeutic targets.

METHODS: We identified the novel protein PVT1-104aa translated from circPVT1, which is generated by back-splicing of the non-coding PVT1 gene. Its expression and clinical significance were examined in CRC clinical specimens and cell lines. Proliferation, metastasis, and tumour growth were assessed by CCK-8, colony formation, transwell, wound healing, and xenograft syngeneic tumour models. Mechanistic studies were performed by immunoprecipitation, ubiquitination assays, and protein half-life analysis. The relationship between PVT1-104aa, c-Myc, and PD-L1 was evaluated by promoter reporter assays, ChIP-qPCR, and immunohistochemistry. The therapeutic efficacy of combining PVT1-104aa inhibition with anti-PD-L1 therapy was tested in vivo.

RESULTS: PVT1-104aa was significantly overexpressed in CRC and correlated with poor patient prognosis. Functionally, it drove tumour progression by promoting proliferation and metastasis. Mechanistically, PVT1-104aa enhanced c-Myc phosphorylation at Ser62, disrupted the c-Myc-FBW7 interaction, and thereby inhibited ubiquitin-mediated degradation of c-Myc, as shown by accelerated c-Myc turnover upon PVT1-104aa knockdown. In addition, PVT1-104aa regulated PD-L1 expression through c-Myc. Combining anti-PVT1-104aa with anti-PD-L1 therapy suppressed CRC growth and increased CD4+ and CD8+ T cell infiltration in xenograft syngeneic tumour models and CRC tissues.

CONCLUSIONS: Our results uncover a pathogenic role of the PVT1-originated molecular species PVT1-104aa and suggest that targeting this pathway represents a promising therapeutic strategy for CRC treatment.

KEY POINTS: Circ-PVT1 is upregulated in CRC patients and encoded a novel protein: PVT1-104aa. PVT1-104aa promotes CRC progression and predicts worse prognosis. PVT1-104aa enhance Myc expression through inhibition of Myc ubiquitination. PVT1-104aa regulate PD-L1 expression of CRC cells and modulate T cells infiltration in vivo.

</details>


### [58] [Self-reinforcing nanoplatform uniting pemetrexed, magnetic hyperthermia and PD-L1 blockade reshapes the tumor microenvironment and elicits combined antitumor immunity in lung adenocarcinoma.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41948465/)
*Zhengjun Guo,Xulin Xie,Wenxiu Li,Shouhui Yi,Emmanuel Koomson,Renardi Gunawan,Zhihang Zhou,Md Kowsar Alam,Li Zhou,Tongxu Si,Zesheng Wang,Xiaoyu Zhou,Yuan Peng,Xiaoyue Zhang,Zhenzhou Yang,Mengsu Yang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于介孔Fe3O4核和二氧化硅壳的多功能纳米平台（PD-L1&Pem@msNPs），共负载培美曲塞和抗PD-L1抗体。该平台通过交变磁场触发热疗和控制药物释放，实现化疗-免疫-磁热联合治疗，有效重塑肺癌免疫抑制微环境，逆转T细胞耗竭，显著抑制肿瘤生长并延长生存期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因，但免疫检查点阻断疗法常受限于免疫抑制性的肿瘤微环境（TME），导致疗效有限。因此，亟需开发能够克服TME抑制、增强免疫治疗效果的新型联合治疗策略。

Method: 构建一种具有介孔Fe3O4核和二氧化硅壳结构的新型多功能纳米平台（PD-L1&Pem@msNPs），共负载化疗药物培美曲塞和抗PD-L1抗体。利用交变磁场（AMF）触发磁热效应并控制药物释放，实现靶向递送和单系统化疗-免疫-磁热联合治疗。通过体外实验验证其理化性质、磁热性能、载药/结合能力及释放行为、细胞摄取和细胞毒性；在皮下和原位肺癌模型中评估体内疗效；结合转录组谱分析和免疫表型分析探究其作用机制。

Result: 1. 体外实验证实了纳米平台的优良理化性质、磁热性能及可控释放能力。
2. 在皮下和原位肺癌模型中，PD-L1&Pem@msNPs相比对照组实现了更优越的肿瘤抑制效果并延长了生存期。
3. 机制研究表明，该治疗显著重塑了TME，增加了瘤内T细胞浸润，并协调改变了巨噬细胞状态。
4. 纵向流式细胞术显示，T细胞状态从耗竭中间型（Tex-int）向祖细胞样（Tex-prog）表型转变，表明T细胞功能得到恢复。

Conclusion: PD-L1&Pem@msNPs提供了一个可外部激活的模块化平台，能够有效重塑肺癌的免疫抑制微环境，恢复T细胞功能，从而显著提高PD-L1阻断疗法的治疗效果，为肺癌联合治疗提供了新策略。

Abstract: Lung cancer remains the leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, and immune checkpoint blockade is often limited by an immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME). To address these challenges, we developed a novel multifunctional nanoplatform, based on a mesoporous Fe3O4 core and silica shell, co-loading pemetrexed and an anti-PD-L1 antibody (PD-L1&Pem@msNPs). The construct enables checkpoint-targeted delivery, alternating magnetic field (AMF)-triggered hyperthermia and controlled drug release, enabling a single-system chemo-immuno-magnetothermal regimen. We comprehensively investigated the physicochemical properties, magnetothermal performance, loading/conjugation, and release behavior, and validated cellular uptake and cytotoxicity in vitro. In subcutaneous and orthotopic lung tumor models, PD-L1&Pem@msNPs achieved superior tumor suppression and extended survival compared with control formulations. Mechanistically, transcriptomic profiling together with immunophenotyping demonstrated marked TME remodeling, with increased intratumoral T-cell representation accompanied by coordinated rewiring of macrophages status. Notably, longitudinal flow cytometry revealed a shift in T-cell states from an exhausted-intermediate (Tex-int) toward a progenitor-like (Tex-prog) phenotype, consistent with restoration of T-cell functionality under treatment. Collectively, PD-L1&Pem@msNPs provides an externally activatable, modular platform to remodel the TME and improve the therapeutic impact of PD-L1 blockade in lung cancer.

</details>


### [59] [Surgical resection of an adrenocorticotropic hormone-producing pulmonary typical carcinoid with mediastinal lymph node metastasis and high programmed death-ligand 1 expression.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41929548/)
*Masakazu Matsuda,Fumihiko Kinoshita,Yukina Takeichi,Koichi Igata,Naoya Iwamoto,Taichi Matsubara,Kazuki Takada,Mikiko Hashisako,Shinichi Aishima,Yoshinao Oda,Tomoyoshi Takenaka,Tomoharu Yoshizumi*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本文报告了一例伴有高PD-L1表达和异位库欣综合征的典型肺类癌病例。患者在接受手术及含阿替利珠单抗的辅助治疗后，18个月无复发。尽管典型类癌通常PD-L1低表达，但高表达者预后较差，免疫检查点抑制剂在此类患者中的疗效仍需更多研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型肺类癌中程序性死亡配体1（PD-L1）阳性率通常较低，但PD-L1表达与淋巴结转移频率增加及预后不良相关。虽然免疫检查点抑制剂已用于PD-L1阳性的非小细胞肺癌辅助治疗，但其在肺类癌中的疗效尚不明确，亟需通过病例研究探索其潜在价值。

Method: 报告一例47岁女性患者的临床病例。患者因满月脸就诊，确诊为促肾上腺皮质激素（ACTH）依赖性库欣综合征。影像学发现左肺下叶结节及左肺门淋巴结肿大，疑似异位库欣综合征。患者接受机器人辅助胸腔镜左肺下叶切除术及ND2a-2淋巴结清扫术。术后病理确诊为典型类癌（pT1bN2M0, pStage IIIA），且PD-L1高表达。基于淋巴结受累及PD-L1表达情况，患者接受顺铂和长春瑞滨四个疗程化疗，随后使用阿替利珠单抗进行治疗。

Result: 术后患者ACTH水平恢复正常。随访18个月，患者存活且无复发。病理证实为高PD-L1表达的典型类癌，且伴有纵隔淋巴结转移。

Conclusion: PD-L1阳性的典型类癌与更高的淋巴结转移频率及更差的预后相关。本病例提示免疫检查点抑制剂（如阿替利珠单抗）可能在治疗此类患者中有效，但仍需进一步的病例调查和研究以评估其在典型类癌中的确切疗效。

Abstract: The programmed death-ligand 1 positivity rate in typical carcinoid is generally low; however, cases with programmed death-ligand 1 expression are more frequently associated with lymph node metastasis and have a poorer prognosis. While immune checkpoint inhibitors were incorporated into adjuvant chemotherapy regimens for programmed death-ligand 1-positive non-small cell lung cancer, their efficacy in pulmonary carcinoids remains unclear. A 47-year-old woman presented to a local clinic with moon face. Laboratory testing revealed elevated levels of adrenocorticotropic hormone and cortisol and adrenocorticotropic hormone-dependent Cushing's syndrome was suspected. For further evaluation, the patient was referred to our hospital. Computed tomography revealed a nodule in the left lower lobe of the lung and enlargement of the left hilar lymph node, suggesting ectopic Cushing's syndrome. Left lung cancer (cT1bN1M0, cStage IIB) was suspected, and the patient underwent robot-assisted thoracoscopic left lower lobectomy with ND2a-2 lymph node dissection for diagnostic and therapeutic purposes. Pathological examination confirmed a diagnosis of typical carcinoid (pT1bN2M0, pStage IIIA) with high programmed death-ligand 1 expression. Considering the presence of mediastinal lymph node involvement and programmed death-ligand 1 expression, the patient received four courses of cisplatin and vinorelbine, followed by treatment with atezolizumab. Postoperatively, her adrenocorticotropic hormone levels normalized, and the patient has been alive 18 months postoperatively without recurrence. Programmed death-ligand 1-positive typical carcinoids are associated with a higher frequency of lymph node metastasis and poorer prognosis. Additional case investigations are required to assess the efficacy of immune checkpoint inhibitors in typical carcinoid.

</details>


### [60] [Intraperitoneal CF33-hNIS combined with PD-L1 blockade eradicates gastric cancer peritoneal metastases and prevents recurrence via durable T cell memory.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41922086/)
*Annie Yang,Zhifang Zhang,Anthony K Park,Shyambabu Chaurasiya,Sang-In Kim,Jianming Lu,Yoya Vashi,Jennifer Cillis,Hannah Valencia,Courtney Chen,Supriya Deshpande,Yuman Fong,Yanghee Woo*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究在免疫健全的小鼠模型中评估了腹膜内注射CF33-hNIS溶瘤病毒联合抗PD-L1免疫检查点阻断疗法治疗胃癌腹膜转移（GCPM）的效果。结果显示，高剂量CF33-hNIS联合腹膜内抗PD-L1治疗能显著延长生存期，实现75%的完全肿瘤消退，并诱导持久的记忆T细胞免疫反应，为克服GCPM免疫耐药性提供了有力的临床前依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃癌腹膜转移（PM）预后极差且治疗选择有限。现有的全身性治疗效果不佳，亟需开发针对腹膜微环境的新型靶向疗法，以克服免疫抑制并提高疗效。

Method: 1. **体外实验**：评估CF33-hNIS在人和小鼠胃癌细胞系中的复制能力及细胞毒性。
2. **体内实验**：使用同基因小鼠GCPM模型（ACKPY3944细胞），测试单独腹膜内（IP）注射CF33-hNIS，以及其与静脉或腹膜内注射抗PD-L1联合使用的疗效。
3. **免疫分析**：通过流式细胞术和免疫组化分析腹腔及肿瘤微环境中CD3+ T细胞亚群的浸润情况。
4. **再挑战实验**：对完全消退（CTR）的小鼠重新接种肿瘤细胞，以评估记忆T细胞介导的长期免疫保护。

Result: 1. CF33-hNIS能有效感染并杀伤胃癌细胞。
2. 单独使用IP CF33-hNIS可显著延长生存期，并增加腹腔和实体瘤内CD3+及CD8+ T细胞的浸润。
3. 最佳疗效见于高剂量CF33-hNIS（10^8 PFU）联合腹膜内抗PD-L1（Combo 2方案），完全肿瘤消退率达75%。
4. 完全消退的小鼠能抵抗肿瘤再挑战，其腹腔和脾脏中效应性和中央记忆T细胞群体显著增加。

Conclusion: 高剂量腹膜内注射CF33-hNIS联合抗PD-L1是一种简化的治疗策略，能有效克服胃癌腹膜转移的免疫耐药性，诱导强大的抗肿瘤免疫和记忆反应，具有明确的临床转化潜力。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Peritoneal metastasis (PM) from gastric cancer (GC) is associated with poor prognosis and limited treatment options. We investigated a novel peritoneal-targeted therapy using CF33-human sodium iodide symporter (hNIS), a chimeric orthopoxvirus, combined with anti-PD-L1 immune checkpoint blockade in an immunocompetent mouse model of GCPM.

METHODS: We evaluated replication and cytotoxicity of CF33-hNIS in human and murine GC cell lines. We assessed a syngeneic mouse model of PM using the transgenic mouse GC ACKPY3944 cells to test the efficacy of intraperitoneal (IP) CF33-hNIS alone and combined with intravenous or IP anti-PD-L1. Next, we performed flow cytometry and immunohistochemistry to analyze immune cell populations of CD3+ T cell subsets in the peritoneal cavity and tumor microenvironment. Mice that showed complete tumor regression (CTR) were rechallenged with ACKPY3944 cells to assess memory T cell responses.

RESULTS: CF33-hNIS efficiently infected and killed GC cells. In vivo, IP CF33-hNIS alone significantly prolonged survival and increased infiltration of CD3+ and CD8+ T cells within the peritoneal cavity and solid tumor. The most pronounced therapeutic effect was observed with a single high-dose of CF33-hNIS (108 plaque-forming units) combined with IP anti-PD-L1 (Combo 2) with 75% CTR. Notably, mice with CTR rejected tumor rechallenge, exhibiting significantly elevated effector and central memory T cell populations in the peritoneal cavity and spleen. IP CF33-hNIS demonstrates robust anti-tumor efficacy against GCPM, particularly when combined with IP anti-PD-L1 in the high-dose Combo 2 regimen.

CONCLUSIONS: These findings support a simplified, high-dose IP treatment strategy to overcome immune resistance in GCPM and provide a strong rationale for future clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [61] [UCP2 in Glioblastoma Cells Regulates Mitochondrial Metabolic Reprogramming to Mediate Immunophenotypic Repolarization of Tumor-Associated Macrophages and Immunotherapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41918143/)
*Hailong Du,Xiaoliang Wang,Jianping Sun,Xiaosong Liu,Lei Zhao,Haoming Guo,Jianliang Wu,Jiankai Yang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究证实胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）中UCP2的高表达通过调节线粒体代谢稳态，促进M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs）浸润。敲低UCP2可抑制GBM细胞恶性行为，诱导代谢重编程，促使TAMs向M1型极化，并增强抗PD-L1免疫治疗的疗效，表明UCP2是潜在的联合治疗靶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究解偶联蛋白2（UCP2）是否通过调节GBM细胞自身的线粒体代谢稳态来调控TAMs的免疫表型，进而影响抗肿瘤治疗反应，以解决GBM免疫微环境抑制及治疗耐药的问题。

Method: 1. 临床与数据库分析：利用公共数据库、免疫组化、RT-qPCR和Western blot评估临床GBM样本及细胞系中的UCP2水平。
2. 体外功能实验：构建UCP2特异性敲低细胞模型，检测细胞增殖、迁移、侵袭及凋亡。
3. 代谢评估：采用ATP测定、Seahorse分析、JC-1染色、H2DCFDA及MitoSOX染色评估线粒体代谢功能及ROS水平。
4. 共培养体系：建立GBM-THP-1共培养系统，评估UCP2敲低对巨噬细胞极化的影响。
5. 体内实验：建立皮下肿瘤模型，评估UCP2沉默联合抗PD-L1治疗的协同效应。

Result: 1. UCP2在GBM组织中高表达，且伴随M2型TAMs浸润增加。
2. 敲低UCP2抑制GBM细胞增殖与侵袭，促进凋亡；通过抑制线粒体能量代谢、降低膜电位及积累ROS诱导代谢重编程。
3. UCP2敲低的GBM细胞与共培养巨噬细胞相互作用，促进其向M1型极化。
4. UCP2敲低联合抗PD-L1抗体显著抑制GBM生长，并增加M1型TAMs浸润。

Conclusion: GBM细胞中UCP2的敲低通过调节肿瘤细胞线粒体代谢重塑肿瘤微环境，影响其与TAMs的相互作用，促进M1型复极化，从而增强抗肿瘤治疗效果，具有作为潜在治疗靶点的价值。

Abstract: This paper sought to determine whether uncoupling protein 2 (UCP2) regulates the immune phenotype of TAMs by modulating its own mitochondrial metabolic homeostasis in glioblastoma (GBM) cells, thereby influencing anti-tumor treatment response.
