This study evaluated UCP2 levels in clinical GBM samples and cell lines based on public databases, immunohistochemistry, RT-qPCR, and Western blot. A cell model with specific knockdown of UCP2 was constructed, and cell proliferation, migration and invasion, and apoptosis were detected. ATP measurement, Seahorse analysis, JC-1 staining, H2DCFDA, and MitoSOX staining were employed to assess mitochondrial metabolic function and ROS levels in GBM cells. A GBM-THP-1 co-culture system was established to evaluate the impact of UCP2 knockdown in GBM cells on macrophage polarization. A subcutaneous tumor model was established to evaluate the synergistic effect of UCP2 silencing + anti-PD-L1 therapy. UCP2 was upregulated in GBM tissues and accompanied by increased infiltration of M2-type TAMs. Specific knockdown of UCP2 in GBM cells inhibited cell proliferation and invasion, promoted apoptosis, and induced metabolic reprogramming by inhibiting mitochondrial energy metabolism, reducing mitochondrial membrane potential, and ROS accumulation. Co-culture with GBM cells with UCP2 knockdown promoted macrophage polarization toward the M1 type. UCP2 knockdown + anti-PD-L1 antibody inhibited GBM growth and increased the infiltration of M1-type TAMs. Knockdown of UCP2 in GBM cells reshapes the tumor microenvironment by regulating the tumor cell mitochondrial metabolism, thereby influencing their interaction with TAMs. This promotes M1-type repolarization and enhances the efficacy of anti-tumor treatment, making it a potential therapeutic target.

</details>


### [62] [PAK4 functions as an immune suppressor by reprogramming the phosphatidylcholine metabolism of CD8 + T cells within the glioblastoma tumor microenvironment.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41872692/)
*Lin Yao,Heyang Gao,Zuopeng Su,Guozheng Zhao,Liyan Tang,Shuo Feng,Yuting Ma,Xiao Zhang,Ming Feng,Jialiang Qian,Yanyan Li,Ting Sun,Jiangang Liu,Hao Wang,Youxin Zhou*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究发现PAK4在胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）中高表达，通过TGF-β1/PI3K/AKT/NF-κB轴上调CD8+ T细胞中的PLA2G4A，导致磷脂酰胆碱耗竭和线粒体功能障碍，进而引起T细胞衰竭。抑制PAK4联合抗PD-L1治疗可恢复T细胞毒性并抑制肿瘤生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）具有高度免疫抑制性的肿瘤微环境（TME），导致现有免疫疗法仅对少数患者有效。亟需阐明GBM免疫逃逸的新机制并开发有效的联合治疗策略。

Method: 1. 鉴定PAK4为GBM中的关键免疫抑制基因；
2. 机制研究：分析PAK4介导的TGF-β1释放及其对CD8+ T细胞中PI3K/AKT/NF-κB信号通路和PLA2G4A表达的调控；
3. 功能验证：评估PLA2G4A激活导致的磷脂酰胆碱（PC）耗竭、线粒体/溶酶体损伤及自噬流抑制对T细胞衰竭的影响；
4. 治疗评估：测试PLA2G4A抑制剂、PAK4抑制剂单独及联合抗PD-L1抗体对T细胞功能和肿瘤生长的影响。

Result: 1. PAK4促进GBM细胞释放TGF-β1，激活CD8+ T细胞中的PI3K/AKT/NF-κB通路，上调PLA2G4A；
2. PLA2G4A导致CD8+ T细胞内PC耗竭，损害线粒体和溶酶体功能，抑制自噬流，最终导致T细胞功能衰竭；
3. PLA2G4A抑制剂可减轻T细胞衰竭并增强其细胞毒性；
4. PAK4抑制剂与抗PD-L1联合治疗显著增强CD8+ T细胞毒性并抑制肿瘤生长。

Conclusion: 靶向PAK4可通过阻断PLA2G4A介导的代谢和功能障碍来逆转CD8+ T细胞衰竭，联合免疫检查点阻断疗法为GBM免疫治疗提供了潜在的新策略。

Abstract: Glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) is a highly aggressive primary brain tumor that represents a significant therapeutic challenge because of its immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME). GBM employs multiple sophisticated mechanisms for immune evasion, including proinflammatory cytokine secretion and immune cell effector function impairment. Due to these complex immune evasion strategies, immunotherapies are effective in only a minority of GBM patients. Herein, we identified P21-activated kinase 4 (PAK4) as a critical immunosuppressive gene that is highly expressed in GBM and actively promotes tumor progression. Mechanistically, PAK4 mediates transforming growth factor-beta 1 (TGF-β1) release from GBM cells, triggering PI3K/AKT/NF-κB signalling pathway activation in CD8 + T cells, which consequently upregulates phospholipase A2 group IVA (PLA2G4A) expression. PLA2G4A activation triggers phosphatidylcholine (PC) depletion in CD8 + T cells, damages mitochondrial and lysosomal functions, inducing subsequent mitophagic flux suppression, which culminates in the functional exhaustion of CD8 + T cells. Furthermore, PLA2G4A inhibitor treatment effectively reduces CD8 + T cell exhaustion while enhancing T cell cytotoxic capacity. Finally, combined PAK4 inhibitor and anti-PD-L1 therapy increases the CD8 + T cell cytotoxic function and suppresses tumor growth. Overall, our study results suggest that targeting PAK4 could be a potential strategy for GBM immunotherapy.

</details>


### [63] [Photosynthetic Nanobacteria Drive Metabolic-Immune Synergy for Hypoxia-Resistant Cancer Therapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41848509/)
*Mixiao Tan,Guoliang Cao,Ruihao Qian,Wenying Qu,Yue Yin,Chaofan Zhang,Shih-Hsin Ho,Haitao Ran,Hai Wang,Heling Feng*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种源自微囊藻的多功能光合纳米平台（PnanoCB），结合模拟禁食饮食（FMD），通过原位产氧缓解肿瘤缺氧，增强光动力治疗（PDT）疗效，并激活STING通路诱导系统性抗肿瘤免疫，有效抑制乳腺癌生长及复发且无明显毒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光动力疗法（PDT）是一种有前景的癌症治疗手段，但其疗效常受限于肿瘤微环境的缺氧状态以及有限的免疫激活能力，导致治疗效率不高且易复发。

Method: 1. 构建源自魏氏微囊藻（Microcystis wesenbergii）的原生质体衍生囊泡，保留其光合作用能力和叶绿素。
2. 通过pH敏感的低插入肽（pHLIP）连接酶，功能化修饰MMP-2可切割的抗PD-L1肽，实现肿瘤靶向免疫检查点阻断。
3. 将PnanoCB与模拟禁食饮食（FMD）联合应用，以改善肿瘤积聚和治疗效果。
4. 在4T1乳腺癌模型中进行红光照射治疗，并评估肿瘤抑制、复发预防及生物安全性。

Result: 1. PnanoCB在红光照射下能高效原位产生氧气，克服缺氧并显著放大PDT诱导的活性氧（ROS）产生。
2. PnanoCB联合FMD进一步提高了肿瘤积聚和治疗效能。
3. 该组合疗法在4T1乳腺癌模型中实现了最强的肿瘤抑制效果，并在再挑战模型中有效防止了肿瘤复发。
4. 主要器官未检测到明显毒性。
5. 机制上，PnanoCB缓解了缺氧，促进了免疫原性细胞死亡，并通过激活STING通路触发树突状细胞（DC）成熟。

Conclusion: PnanoCB策略成功整合了光合产氧、光动力免疫疗法和代谢干预，能够有效重塑肿瘤微环境，缓解缺氧，诱导强烈的系统性抗肿瘤免疫反应，为克服PDT局限性和提高癌症治疗效果提供了新思路。

Abstract: Photodynamic therapy (PDT) is a promising cancer treatment, yet its efficacy is often compromised by tumor hypoxia and limited immune activation. Here, we developed a multifunctional photosynthetic nanoplatform (PnanoCB) derived from Microcystis wesenbergii to alleviate hypoxia, enhance reactive oxygen species (ROS)-mediated tumor cell killing, and activate antitumor immunity. The cyanobacteria were restructured into protoplast-derived vesicles, retaining photosynthetic capacity and chlorophyll, and functionalized with a Matrix metalloproteinase-2 (MMP-2)-cleavable anti-PD-L1 peptide via a pH-sensitive pH low insertion peptide (pHLIP) linker for tumor-targeted immune checkpoint blockade. Upon red light irradiation, PnanoCB efficiently generated oxygen in situ, overcoming hypoxia and significantly amplifying PDT-induced ROS production. Combining PnanoCB with a fasting-mimicking diet (FMD) further improved tumor accumulation and therapeutic efficacy. The combination of PnanoCB, light irradiation, and FMD achieved the most potent tumor inhibition in a 4T1 breast cancer model and effectively prevented tumor recurrence in a rechallenge model, without detectable toxicity to major organs. Overall, PnanoCB significantly alleviates hypoxia, promotes immunogenic cell death, and triggers robust dendritic cell (DC) maturation through stimulator of interferon genes (STING) pathway activation. This study demonstrates a strategy integrating photosynthetic oxygen generation, photodynamic immunotherapy, and metabolic intervention to remodel the tumor microenvironment and elicit robust systemic antitumor immunity.

</details>


### [64] [Expression of Bruton's Tyrosine Kinase Reflects Immune Cells Infiltration and Cell Proliferation in Breast Cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41822319/)
*Tamrah AlRammah,Rongrong Wu,Kohei Chida,Kei Kawashima,Kenichi Hakamada,Takashi Ishikawa,John M L Ebos,Kazuaki Takabe*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过整合分析5,240例乳腺癌样本及多个临床试验队列，发现BTK表达主要定位于髓系和B细胞，与肿瘤微环境的免疫活性及增殖信号相关，尤其在TNBC中表达最高。然而，BTK表达水平并不具备预后价值，也不能预测新辅助化疗或PD-L1基于免疫治疗的反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BTK是B细胞受体信号的关键介质，且在恶化乳腺癌预后的髓系来源抑制性细胞（MDSCs）中表达。鉴于BTK抑制剂已在血液癌症中应用并在实体瘤中进行测试，研究者旨在探究BTK表达在不同乳腺癌亚型中的临床相关性，评估其作为疾病进展预测因子或治疗反应生物标志物的潜力。

Method: 研究整合了三个大型乳腺癌队列（TCGA, METABRIC, SCAN-B，共n=5,240）、10个新辅助化疗（NAC）数据集以及I-SPY2新辅助免疫试验队列的数据。采用基因集富集分析（GSEA）和xCell去卷积技术评估BTK表达与细胞增殖、免疫浸润及肿瘤微环境（TME）组成的关联；利用单细胞测序数据（SCP1039, SCP1106）鉴定表达BTK的细胞群；并通过Kaplan-Meier和log-rank检验进行生存分析。

Result: 1. BTK在三阴性乳腺癌（TNBC）中表达最高，但在ER+/HER2-肿瘤中意外地驱动了更强的增殖基因集富集。
2. BTK表达与AJCC分期、总生存期、无病生存期或疾病特异性生存期均无相关性。
3. 高BTK肿瘤显示出显著的免疫通路富集（如IFN-γ、IL-6/JAK/STAT3、TNF-α信号），伴有较高的肿瘤浸润白细胞/淋巴细胞分数、细胞溶解活性及髓系/淋巴系细胞丰度。
4. BTK表达与新辅助化疗（NAC）反应无一致关联（仅1/10数据集显示弱关联）。
5. 尽管BTK与PD-1/PD-L1表达强相关，但不能预测I-SPY2队列中对durvalumab联合olaparib的治疗反应。
6. 单细胞分析显示BTK转录本主要定位于髓系细胞和B细胞。

Conclusion: 乳腺癌中的BTK表达反映了一种增殖性和免疫活跃的肿瘤微环境（特别是在TNBC和HER2阳性亚型中），但其缺乏预后价值，且不能作为新辅助化疗或基于PD-L1的免疫治疗反应的预测指标。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Bruton's tyrosine kinase (BTK) is a downstream mediator in B-cell receptor (BCR) signaling and is essential for B-cell differentiation and proliferation. BTK inhibitors are approved and in clinical use for hematological cancers such as lymphoma and leukemia, with testing underway in solid tumors. Because BTK is expressed in myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs) known to worsen breast cancer (BC) outcomes, we investigated the clinical relevance of BTK expression in multiple BC subtypes as a predictor of progression and/or response to treatment.

METHODS: We performed an integrative transcriptomic analysis of tumor BTK expression across three large BC cohorts (The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA), Molecular Taxonomy of Breast Cancer International Consortium (METABRIC), and Sweden Cancerome Analysis Network-Breast (SCAN-B); total n = 5,240), 10 neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NAC) datasets, and the I-SPY2 neoadjuvant immunotherapy trial cohort. Gene set enrichment analysis (GSEA) and xCell deconvolution were used to evaluate associations with cell proliferation, immune infiltration, and tumor microenvironment (TME) composition while single-cell sequencing datasets (SCP1039, SCP1106) were used to identify BTK-expressing cell populations. Survival analyses were performed using Kaplan-Meier and log-rank tests.

RESULTS: BTK levels were the highest in triple-negative BC (TNBC) among the subtypes and unexpectedly drove stronger proliferation gene set enrichment in estrogen receptor-positive (ER+)/human epidermal growth factor receptor 2-negative (HER2-) tumors (e.g., MITOTIC_SPINDLE, G2M_CHECKPOINT). However, BTK expression did not correlate with American Joint Committee on Cancer (AJCC) stage or overall, disease-free, or disease-specific survival across cohorts or molecular subtypes. Notably, BTK-high tumors showed robust enrichment of immune pathways (interferon gamma (IFN-γ) response, interleukin-6 (IL-6)/Janus kinase (JAK)/signal transducer and activator of transcription 3 (STAT3), tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α) signaling) and exhibited elevated tumor-infiltrating leukocyte and lymphocyte fractions, increased cytolytic activity, and greater abundance of myeloid and lymphoid cell populations. BTK expression was not consistently associated with NAC response as only one of 10 datasets (GSE25066) showed a weak association within ER+ and HER2-positive subtypes. Similarly, BTK levels did not predict response in I-SPY2 patients receiving durvalumab plus olaparib, despite strong correlations with programmed cell death protein 1/programmed cell death ligand 1 (PD-1/PD-L1) expression. Single-cell analysis localized BTK transcripts primarily to myeloid and B cells.

CONCLUSION: BTK expression in BC reflects a proliferative and immune-active TME, particularly in TNBC and HER2-positive subtypes, but lacks prognostic or predictive value for NAC or PD-L1-based immunotherapy response.

</details>


### [65] [The spectrum of clinical and histopathological features in patients undergoing liver biopsy for immune-checkpoint-inhibitor-associated hepatotoxicity: a 10-year multicentre experience.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41723048/)
*Choon Ean Boon,Preithy Uthamalingam,Dilini Gunawardena,Tim Mitchell,Bella Nguyen,Willem Bastian de Boer*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究回顾性分析了西澳大利亚13例免疫检查点抑制剂相关肝毒性（ICI-H）患者的肝活检样本。结果显示肝炎型损伤最常见（9/13），而纯胆汁淤积型损伤与单药帕博利珠单抗治疗相关。所有患者经类固醇治疗后均缓解，部分患者成功重新挑战ICI治疗，且未发现慢性肝病发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免疫检查点抑制剂（ICI）疗法虽革新了肿瘤学，但其引发的免疫相关不良事件（特别是ICI相关肝毒性，ICI-H）带来了显著的诊断和治疗挑战。本研究旨在深入探讨ICI-H的组织学表现及其对临床决策和随访的影响，以填补该领域在病理特征与临床结果关联方面的认知空白。

Method: 采用回顾性研究设计，筛选2015年至2024年间西澳大利亚州公立三级护理病理实验室接收的所有疑似ICI-H肝活检样本。纳入标准包括接受ICI治疗或在活检前3个月内暴露于ICI的患者。研究收集了患者的人口统计学数据、使用的ICI类型、组织学损伤模式、治疗方案及随访结果。

Result: 共纳入13名患者（年龄29-83岁），均患有转移性恶性肿瘤并在活检时接受类固醇治疗。主要发现包括：1. 最常见的损伤模式为肝炎型损伤（9例），其中5例以小叶为主；2. 3例表现为纯胆汁淤积型损伤，其中1例具有ICI相关胆管病特征，这些病例均使用单药帕博利珠单抗治疗；3. 1例为混合型肝炎和胆汁淤积型损伤。所有患者经类固醇治疗（伴或不伴停止ICI）后ICI-H均得到解决。4名患者在恢复后耐受单药ICI重新挑战，但1例在4年后重新挑战时复发。

Conclusion: 在ICI-H病例中，肝活检显示肝炎型损伤最为常见。纯胆汁淤积型损伤模式与单药PD-1抑制剂帕博利珠单抗的使用密切相关，包括ICI相关胆管病病例。在该有限队列中，未发现患者在暴露于ICI后发展为慢性肝病。研究结果强调了组织学评估在指导ICI相关肝毒性管理和重新挑战决策中的重要性。

Abstract: Immune checkpoint inhibitor (ICI) therapy has revolutionised oncology, but presents significant diagnostic and therapeutic challenges due to immune-related adverse events, notably ICI-related hepatotoxicity (ICI-H). This study aims to provide insights into the histological manifestations of ICI-H and their impact on clinical decision-making along with follow-up data where available. We retrospectively screened all liver biopsy samples received at statewide tertiary care public pathology laboratories in Western Australia between 2015 and 2024 using the laboratory information system for suspected ICI-H. Patients receiving ICI therapy and those with exposure within 3 months of biopsy were included in the study. Thirteen patients met study criteria. The age range was 29-83 years. All patients had been treated for metastatic malignancy and received steroids at the time of biopsy. ICIs administered included ipilimumab/nivolumab combination therapy (n=5), single-agent pembrolizumab (n=4), single-agent nivolumab (n=2), atezolizumab/bevacizumab combination therapy (n=1), and an experimental bifunctional protein-targeting programmed death ligand 1 (PD-L1) (n=1). The most common pattern of injury was hepatitic injury (nine cases), which was lobular predominant in five cases. These patients were treated with ipilimumab/nivolumab combination therapy or single-agent programmed cell death protein 1 (PD-1) or PD-L1 inhibitors. A pure cholestatic injury pattern was seen in three cases, of which one showed features of ICI-related cholangiopathy, all treated with single-agent pembrolizumab. One case had a mixed hepatitic and cholestatic pattern of injury (treated with atezolezumab). All patients had resolution of ICI-H on being treated with steroids and with or without cessation of ICI. Two patients completed the treatment course while receiving steroids. Four patients tolerated rechallenge with single-agent ICI after the resolution of ICI-H. One patient was rechallenged 4 years later but developed recurrent ICI-H with a similar hepatitic morphology. In conclusion, the hepatitic pattern of injury on biopsy was the most common (nine out of 13 cases), while a pure cholestatic pattern was associated with single-agent PD-1 inhibitor pembrolizumab, including one case with features consistent with ICI-related cholangiopathy. In our limited cohort, there were no patients who developed chronic liver disease after exposure to ICIs.

</details>


### [66] [PRELID1 and VDAC3 Coordinate a Senescence-Like State in Germinal Center B Cells to Promote IL-7-Driven Antitumor Immunity in Colorectal Cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41706757/)
*Yuhan Liao,Huimeng Xu,Xinghua Zhuo,Shupeng Hu,Lanhui Huang,Zhe Hao,Jianxiong Chen,Yulu Wang,Jun Zhou*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了结直肠癌中衰老样生发中心B细胞（Bgc）通过PRELID1-VDAC3-IL-7轴增强抗肿瘤免疫的机制，并证明靶向该轴可提升抗PD-L1免疫疗法的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌具有免疫抑制微环境，导致肿瘤进展和免疫治疗耐药。虽然已知肿瘤浸润B细胞亚群在调节抗肿瘤免疫中具有双重作用，但其具体的调控机制尚不清楚，亟需深入解析以改善治疗效果。

Method: 研究构建了单细胞转录组分析体系，并在B细胞缺陷小鼠中建立了Bgc过继转移模型。通过多方面的实验验证，深入探讨了生发中心B细胞（Bgc）在结直肠癌中的功能状态及其分子调控机制。

Result: 研究发现Bgc中存在一种衰老样状态，该状态由PRELID1和VDAC3通过协同调控线粒体-溶酶体相互作用所驱动。这种状态促进了白细胞介素-7（IL-7）的分泌，从而减轻CD8+ T细胞的耗竭，增强与CD8+ T细胞的功能性串扰，最终提升抗肿瘤免疫力并增加对抗PD-L1治疗的敏感性。

Conclusion: 靶向衰老样Bgc细胞中的PRELID1-VDAC3-IL-7轴与抗PD-L1免疫疗法相结合，是一种具有潜力的结直肠癌联合治疗策略，为克服免疫治疗耐药提供了新的理论依据和治疗靶点。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer (CRC) is characterized by an immune-suppressive microenvironment that facilitates to tumor progression and immunotherapy resistance. Emerging evidence indicates that tumor-infiltrating B cell subsets play dual roles in modulating antitumor immunity. However, the mechanisms underlying their regulatory functions remain poorly understood. In this study, we constructed single-cell transcriptomic analyses and a Bgc adoptive transfer model in B cell-deficient mice, performing multifaceted validation to confirm that targeting the PRELID1-VDAC3-IL-7 axis in senescence-like germinal center B cells enhances sensitivity to anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy. Mechanistically, we identify a senescence-like state in germinal center B cells (Bgc) that enhances antitumor immunity in CRC by promoting Interleukin-7 (IL-7) secretion and alleviating CD8+ T cell exhaustion. Specifically, this senescence-like state is driven by PRELID1 and VDAC3 through their cooperative regulation of mitochondria-lysosome interactions in Bgc cells, which enhances IL-7 secretion and promotes functional crosstalk with CD8+ T cells to sustain antitumor immunity. In summary, our findings support a combinatorial strategy integrating targeting of the PRELID1-VDAC3-IL-7 axis in senescence-like Bgc cells with anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy for colorectal cancer.

</details>


### [67] [Ultrasound-triggered carrier-free nanoprodrugs activate cGAS-STING pathway to enhance tumor-targeting chemo-immunotherapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41704299/)
*Xiaodan Xu,Peile Jin,Yijie Chen,Bihan Wu,Xia Fang,Yue Song,Jieli Luo,Guowei Wang,Pintong Huang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种超声触发的无载体纳米前药PBSN38-CUR，通过增强肿瘤局部DNA损伤以激活cGAS-STING通路，从而在三阴性乳腺癌模型中显著抑制肿瘤生长、防止肺转移并增强抗PD-L1免疫疗法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化疗诱导的细胞凋亡虽能释放dsDNA片段激活cGAS-STING通路引发抗肿瘤免疫反应，但受限于化疗药物的非特异性全身毒性以及dsDNA积累效率低，导致该通路的激活效果不佳。

Method: 构建一种结合声敏剂姜黄素（CUR）和ROS响应性前药PBSN38的无载体纳米前药PBSN38-CUR。利用超声（US）照射在肿瘤部位触发大量ROS生成，激活前药释放SN38造成DNA损伤，同时CUR通过钙超载加剧线粒体损伤和dsDNA释放，从而在不依赖微环境的情况下放大cGAS-STING通路信号。

Result: PBSN38-CUR具有高载药量和良好的生物相容性，对正常组织毒性极低。在超声激活下，该纳米药物在三阴性乳腺癌模型中显著抑制了肿瘤生长，有效防止了肺转移，并增强了与抗PD-L1抗体联合治疗的疗效。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种通过超声触发前药释放来放大DNA损伤进而空间控制激活cGAS-STING通路的策略，展示了其在肿瘤靶向化学-免疫治疗临床转化中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Chemotherapy-induced cell apoptosis results in nuclear damage and the subsequent release of double-stranded DNA (dsDNA) fragments, which can stimulate the cGAS-STING pathway to initiate antitumor immune responses. However, this pathway may be less effective due to nonspecific systemic toxicity caused by chemotherapeutic agents and inefficient dsDNA accumulation. This study aimed to develop an ultrasound (US)-triggered carrier-free nanoprodrug PBSN38-curcumin (PBSN38-CUR), incorporating the sonosensitizer curcumin (CUR) and reactive oxygen species (ROS)-responsive prodrug pinacol boronic ester-conjugated 7-ethyl-10-hydroxycamptothecin (PBSN38). The objective was to enhance tumor-targeted chemo-immunotherapy by amplifying in situ DNA damage signals to activate the cGAS-STING pathway in a microenvironment-independent manner. The biocompatible PBSN38-CUR was characterized by high drug-loading capacity, demonstrating negligible toxicity in normal tissues. PBSN38-CUR generated a significant number of ROS in tumor tissues under US irradiation. The toxic chemotherapeutic SN38 was subsequently activated to induce cellular DNA damage and eradicate solid tumors. Moreover, the released CUR led to increased ROS generation and mitochondrial damage through calcium overload, further promoting dsDNA release in the cytoplasm and amplifying a robust immunological effect by activating the cGAS-STING pathway. As a result, US-activated PBSN38-CUR significantly reduced tumor growth in a triple-negative breast cancer model, preventing lung metastases and enhancing the effectiveness of anti-PD-L1 antibody combination therapy. Overall, this study proposed a spatio-controlled strategy for activating the cGAS-STING pathway by amplifying DNA damage through US-triggered prodrug release, thereby highlighting the significant potential of this strategy for clinical translation in tumor-targeted chemo-immunotherapy.

</details>


### [68] [A Spatiotemporally Controlled Nanoplatform for Photothermal BRD4 Degradation Enables Synergistic Cancer Immunotherapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41662485/)
*Luyi Wang,Jiasha Wu,Rui Ji,Sufeng Qiang,Yulin Shen,Yan Zuo,Shiqin Jian,Siyao Liu,Fusheng Xu,Honggang Hu,Xiaochun Hu*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种光热裂解靶向偶联物（PTTAC）策略，利用基于Fe-卟啉MOF的多功能诊疗纳米平台（PCN-CuS-JQ/RGD），通过近红外二区激光激活实现BRD4蛋白的精准降解。该方法不仅抑制肿瘤生长，还通过诱导免疫原性细胞死亡和下调PD-L1表达，显著增强了抗PD-L1免疫检查点阻断疗法的疗效，克服了传统PROTAC技术依赖E3连接酶及缺乏时空控制导致的系统性毒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的靶向蛋白降解（TPD）技术（如PROTACs）存在对E3连接酶的依赖性以及时空控制能力的缺失，这往往导致系统性毒性。为了解决这些局限性并实现更精准、可控的蛋白降解，研究人员旨在开发一种不依赖E3连接酶且具备光激活特性的新型降解策略。

Method: 构建了一种名为PCN-CuS-JQ/RGD的多功能诊疗纳米平台。该平台以具有MRI可视性的Fe-卟啉金属有机框架（PCN(Fe)）为载体，修饰有CuS光热剂、BRD4抑制剂（JQ1）和肿瘤靶向肽（RGD）。其作用机制包括：1) RGD肽介导对整合素过表达肿瘤细胞的主动靶向；2) 细胞内化后载体分解，释放较小的CuS-JQ/RGD单元；3) 单元进入细胞核结合BRD4蛋白；4) 在1064 nm（NIR-II）激光照射下，CuS产生的局部光热效应诱导BRD4发生光热裂解降解。

Result: 该PTTAC策略实现了高效且精准的BRD4降解，有效抑制了肿瘤生长。同时，它诱导了免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD）并下调了PD-L1的表达，从而启动了强大的抗肿瘤免疫反应。在双侧荷瘤小鼠模型中，该策略与抗PD-L1免疫检查点阻断疗法表现出显著的协同效应，大幅提升了治疗效果。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一种可光激活的蛋白降解平台，具有高肿瘤选择性和强效的免疫治疗潜力。PTTAC策略为解决传统TPD技术的局限性提供了新途径，并为癌症治疗，特别是联合免疫疗法，开辟了充满希望的新方向。

Abstract: Conventional targeted protein degradation (TPD) via PROTACs is limited by E3 ligase reliance and a lack of spatiotemporal control, often causing systemic toxicity. Here, we report a photothermolysis-targeting conjugate (PTTAC) strategy for precise, light-activated degradation. We constructed a multifunctional theranostic nanoplatform (PCN-CuS-JQ/RGD) based on an MRI-visible, Fe-porphyrin MOF (PCN(Fe)) carrier, decorated with CuS photothermal agents, a BRD4 inhibitor (JQ1), and a tumor-targeting peptide (RGD). It executes a sophisticated step-wise delivery: 1) The RGD peptide facilitates active targeting to integrin-overexpressing tumor cells. 2) Upon internalization, the PCN(Fe) carrier decomposes within the cell, releasing smaller CuS-JQ/RGD units. 3) These units enter the nucleus and bind to the BRD4 protein. Under 1064 nm (NIR-II) laser irradiation, the localized photothermal effect generated by CuS induces the efficient degradation of BRD4. This PTTAC strategy not only inhibits tumor growth but also initiates a robust anti-tumor immune response by inducing immunogenic cell death (ICD) and downregulating PD-L1 expression. Crucially, in a bilateral tumor-bearing mouse model, our strategy demonstrated a powerful synergistic effect, significantly enhancing the efficacy of anti-PD-L1 immune checkpoint blockade therapy. This work presents a light-activatable degradation platform that achieves high tumor selectivity and potent immunotherapy, offering a promising new avenue for cancer treatment.

</details>


### [69] [Combination therapy for colorectal cancer with anti-PD-L1 and cancer vaccine: A multiscale mathematical model of tumor-immune interactions.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41619849/)
*Chenghang Li,Haifeng Zhang,Xiulan Lai,Jinzhi Lei*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究构建了肿瘤-免疫多尺度数学模型及基于近似贝叶斯计算的虚拟肿瘤样本框架，评估结直肠癌中抗PD-L1与癌症疫苗联合治疗的策略。研究发现，抗PD-L1治疗采用多次低剂量方案效果更佳，而癌症疫苗则适用最大剂量方案。细胞毒性T细胞（特别是其与调节性T细胞的比率）被确定为预测治疗反应的关键生物标志物，有助于患者分层和个性化治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于肿瘤-免疫系统在结直肠癌进展中的关键作用，以及前期研究显示抗PD-L1与癌症疫苗联合疗法能改善治疗反应，本研究旨在通过计算模型深入探究不同治疗策略下的肿瘤进化动力学，并解决个体间治疗反应的异质性问题，以优化临床治疗方案。

Method: 1. 开发了一个描述肿瘤、免疫细胞和细胞因子相互作用的多尺度数学模型。
2. 建立基于近似贝叶斯计算（Approximate Bayesian Computation, ABC）的计算框架，生成虚拟肿瘤样本以模拟个体间的治疗反应异质性。
3. 对比分析不同给药方案（如多次低剂量与最大剂量）在抗PD-L1治疗和癌症疫苗治疗中的疗效。

Result: 1. 在抗PD-L1治疗中，多次低剂量给药方案相比基线治疗显著降低了晚期肿瘤负荷。
2. 在癌症疫苗治疗中，最大剂量疗法在控制肿瘤生长方面表现更优。
3. 细胞毒性T细胞（Cytotoxic T cells）在治疗前后均被识别为一致的预测生物标志物。
4. 治疗前的细胞毒性T细胞与调节性T细胞（Regulatory T cells）比率是一个稳健的预测生物标志物。

Conclusion: 不同的免疫治疗策略（抗PD-L1 vs. 癌症疫苗）需要差异化的给药方案以实现最佳疗效。细胞毒性T细胞及其与调节性T细胞的比率可作为有效的预治疗预测生物标志物，具有指导患者分层和实现个性化治疗的临床潜力。

Abstract: The tumor-immune system plays a critical role in colorectal cancer progression. Recent preclinical and clinical studies showed that combination therapy with anti-PD-L1 and cancer vaccines improved treatment response. In this study, we developed a multiscale mathematical model of interactions among tumors, immune cells, and cytokines to investigate tumor evolutionary dynamics under different therapeutic strategies. Additionally, we established a computational framework based on approximate Bayesian computation to generate virtual tumor samples and capture inter-individual heterogeneity in treatment response. The results demonstrated that a multiple low-dose regimen significantly reduced advanced tumor burden compared to baseline treatment in anti-PD-L1 therapy. In contrast, the maximum dose therapy yielded superior tumor growth control in cancer vaccine therapy. Furthermore, cytotoxic T cells were identified as a consistent predictive biomarker both before and after treatment initiation. Notably, the cytotoxic T cells-to-regulatory T cells ratio specifically served as a robust pre-treatment predictive biomarker, offering potential clinical utility for patient stratification and therapy personalization.

</details>


### [70] [Patient-reported outcomes from the randomized ALICE trial evaluating the addition of atezolizumab to anthracycline-based chemotherapy in metastatic triple-negative breast cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41581361/)
*K G Svalheim,N K Andresen,C Bjerre,B Gilje,E H Jakobsen,R S Falk,B Naume,S Kaasa,J A Kyte*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: ALICE试验的患者报告结果（PROMs）显示，在转移性三阴性乳腺癌（mTNBC）治疗中，相比单纯化疗，阿替利珠单抗联合化疗不仅改善了无进展生存期（PFS），还显著提升了患者的生活质量、情绪功能和疼痛控制。基线生活质量评分较高的患者从免疫治疗中获益更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ALICE试验已证实阿替利珠单抗联合蒽环类免疫调节化疗能改善mTNBC患者（包括PD-L1阴性患者）的无进展生存期（PFS），但缺乏关于该治疗方案对患者主观感受、生活质量及症状负担影响的详细数据。本研究旨在报告该试验中的患者报告结局指标（PROMs），以全面评估治疗对患者生活质量和症状控制的影响。

Method: 这是一项随机对照试验的分析。患者被随机分配接受“化疗+阿替利珠单抗”或“化疗+安慰剂”治疗。研究使用EORTC QLQ-C15-PAL、Chalder疲劳问卷（CFQ）和疼痛数字评分量表（NRS）在基线及第9、17、25、49周收集PROMs数据。主要分析包括各时间点相对于基线的均值变化以及至恶化时间（Time-to-deterioration）的分析。

Result: 1. **短期改善**：在第9周，阿替利珠单抗联合化疗组在所有QLQ-C15-PAL领域、CFQ评分和NRS疼痛强度方面的平均变化均优于安慰剂组。
2. **至恶化时间**：阿替利珠单抗组在整体生活质量（HR 0.24）、情绪功能（HR 0.30）和疼痛（HR 0.20）方面的恶化风险显著降低。
3. **疼痛控制**：作为预设的关键症状，疼痛在阿替利珠单抗组的所有时间点均有改善。
4. **长期耐受性**：12个月时的PROMs数据显示了持续的耐受性。
5. **预测价值**：基线PROMs评分较好与改善的PFS和总生存期相关，特别是在阿替利珠单抗治疗组中。基线生活质量评分高于中位数的患者在接受阿替利珠单抗治疗时PFS显著改善（HR 0.25），而低于或等于中位数的患者未观察到获益（HR 1.02）。

Conclusion: 在mTNBC治疗中，在标准化疗基础上加入阿替利珠单抗不仅能改善无进展生存期，还能显著提升患者报告的生活质量、情绪福祉和症状控制。这些结果支持该联合方案的进一步开发，并提示基线生活质量可作为预测免疫治疗获益的有效指标。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: The ALICE trial demonstrated that adding atezolizumab to anthracycline-based immunomodulatory chemotherapy improved progression-free survival (PFS) in patients with metastatic triple-negative breast cancer (mTNBC), including those with PD-L1-negative tumors. Here, we report the patient-reported outcome measures (PROMs).

METHODS: Patients were randomized to receive chemotherapy plus atezolizumab (atezo-chemo) or chemotherapy plus placebo (placebo-chemo). PROMs were collected at baseline and weeks 9, 17, 25, and 49 using the EORTC QLQ-C15-PAL, Chalder Fatigue Questionnaire (CFQ), and Numeric Rating Scale (NRS) for pain.

RESULTS: PROMs were available from 64 of 68 patients. At week 9, mean changes from baseline favored the atezo-chemo arm across all QLQ-C15-PAL domains, CFQ scores, and NRS pain intensity. Time-to-deterioration analyses also favored atezo-chemo, with statistically significant hazard ratios for global quality of life (QoL; HR 0.24), emotional functioning (HR 0.30), and pain (HR 0.20). Pain-a pre-specified cardinal symptom-improved in the atezo-chemo group at all time points. At 12 months, PROMs indicated sustained tolerability. Better baseline PROM scores were associated with improved PFS and overall survival, especially among patients treated with atezolizumab. Patients with >median QoL score at baseline recorded improved PFS when treated with atezolizumab (HR 0.25), while patients with ≤median QoL score did not (HR 1.02).

CONCLUSIONS: Adding atezolizumab to the study-chemotherapy in mTNBC improves both PFS and patient-reported quality of life, emotional well-being and symptom control. These findings support continued development of this combination regimen and suggest that baseline quality of life may serve as a useful predictor of immunotherapy benefit.

TRIAL REGISTRATION: NCT03164993, May 24th 2017; https://clinicaltrials.gov/ct2/show/record/NCT03164993.

</details>


### [71] [Engineered outer membrane vesicles enhance solid tumour CAR-T cell therapy.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41501251/)
*Xianjun Li,Xuehan Li,Jiaqi Shi,Yingjing Li,Hanyu Zhang,Tianjun Chen,Hui Pang,Shuyuan Zhang,Shengnan Luo,Fengyi Liu,Shuang Li,Chujie Ding,Linlin Sun,Fan Xing,Tongsen Zheng*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了BROAD-CAR平台，利用工程化细菌外膜囊泡（OMVs）递送抗PD-L1抗体和靶抗原质粒，通过逆转免疫抑制和原位修饰肿瘤抗原，显著增强了CAR-T细胞对实体瘤（特别是抗原异质性肿瘤）的治疗效果，并抑制了复发与转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CAR-T细胞疗法在实体瘤治疗中面临的主要挑战，即免疫抑制性肿瘤微环境（TME）和肿瘤抗原表达的异质性，这些因素限制了CAR-T细胞的浸润、扩增及杀伤效率。

Method: 开发基于细菌外膜囊泡（OMV）的BROAD-CAR平台：1. 改造OMVs以表达高亲和力抗PD-L1抗体，用于阻断PD-1/PD-L1信号通路；2. 在OMVs中装载编码CAR靶抗原的质粒；3. 将该平台应用于乳腺癌小鼠模型，评估其体外及体内的抗肿瘤活性、对TME的改善作用以及对抗原异质性/阴性肿瘤的治疗效果。

Result: 1. BROAD-CAR成功阻断了PD-1/PD-L1通路，改善了肿瘤微环境，促进了CAR-T细胞的扩增；2. 实现了实体瘤的原位抗原修饰，使CAR-T细胞能够裂解抗原异质性及抗原阴性的肿瘤细胞；3. 在乳腺癌小鼠模型中，显著抑制了肿瘤的复发和转移；4. 证实了该方法的安全性和高效性。

Conclusion: BROAD-CAR提供了一种安全且高效的策略，通过逆转免疫抑制和克服抗原异质性，显著提升了CAR-T疗法在实体瘤治疗中的疗效和适用性，具有潜在的临床转化价值。

Abstract: Major challenges facing chimeric antigen receptor (CAR)-T cell therapy for solid tumours include the immunosuppressive tumour microenvironment and the heterogeneity of antigen expression. Bacterial outer membrane vesicles (OMVs) naturally activate the immune system and can be engineered for drug delivery. Here we develop a bacterial OMV-based immunosuppression reversal and optimized antigen decoration platform for CAR-T therapy (BROAD-CAR), in which OMVs are modified to express a high-affinity anti-PD-L1 antibody and load plasmids encoding the target antigen for CARs. By blocking the PD-1/PD-L1 signalling pathway, our tumour-targeting platform enhances the antitumour activity of CAR-T cells both in vitro and in vivo and boosts CAR-T cell expansion by ameliorating the tumour microenvironment. BROAD-CAR also facilitates the in situ antigenic modification of solid tumours and achieves CAR-mediated lysis of antigen-heterogeneous and antigen-negative tumours, inhibiting tumour recurrence and metastasis in the breast cancer mouse models. Our findings highlight a safe and efficient approach to enhance the efficacy and applicability of CAR-T therapy in solid tumours.

</details>


### [72] [Aldehyde dehydrogenase 1 A1 regulates the transcription of PD-L1 and its targeting with disulfiram together with PD-L1 immunotherapy synergistically delays cyst growth in ADPKD.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41407012/)
*Alice Shasha Cheng,Linda Xiaoyan Li,Julie Xia Zhou,Peter C Harris,James P Calvet,Xiaogang Li*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究揭示ALDH1A1在常染色体显性多囊肾病（ADPKD）中通过激活PKD相关信号通路及调控PD-L1/Ccl2转录促进囊肿生长；抑制ALDH1A1（使用双硫仑DSF）可延缓囊肿进展，且与PD-L1抗体联用具有协同治疗效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADPKD是最常见的遗传性肾脏疾病，目前缺乏有效阻止其进展的治疗方法。尽管已知ALDH1A1可通过生成9-顺式视黄酸调节PKD1基因表达，但其在ADPKD中的具体作用机制尚不明确，亟需深入探究以寻找新的治疗靶点。

Method: 利用Pkd1突变小鼠模型，通过基因敲除Aldh1A1或使用双硫仑（DSF）抑制ALDH1A1活性，单独或联合PD-L1抗体进行治疗。采用免疫组化、Western blot、qRT-PCR、流式细胞术、CUT&Tag分析及ChIP assay等技术，评估ALDH1A1对囊肿生长、免疫反应、PKD相关信号通路及靶基因转录的影响。

Result: 1. ADPKD肾脏中ALDH1A1上调，可能通过AURKA介导激活AKT、S6、STAT3和Rb等信号通路，促进上皮细胞增殖。
2. Aldh1A1敲除可延缓Pkd1突变小鼠的囊肿生长。
3. ALDH1A1作为转录因子，直接调控Pd-l1和Ccl2的转录，影响PD-L1介导的免疫监视及巨噬细胞招募。
4. DSF抑制ALDH1A1在急性和轻度Pkd1小鼠模型中均能减少囊肿生长。
5. ALDH1A1抑制剂与PD-L1抗体联用在延缓囊肿生长方面表现出协同效应。

Conclusion: ALDH1A1是ADPKD进展的关键驱动因子，双硫仑（DSF）作为一种潜在的ADPKD治疗药物，通过抑制ALDH1A1发挥作用。研究提出了化疗（DSF）与免疫疗法（PD-L1抗体）联合治疗ADPKD的新策略。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Autosomal Dominant Polycystic Kidney Disease (ADPKD) is the most common inherited kidney disease with no effective therapy to halt its progression. ALDH1A1, one of the aldehyde dehydrogenases, is the main ALDH1 isozyme known to oxidize 9-cis retinaldehyde into 9-cis retinoic acid, which can regulate the expression of the PKD1 gene. However, the role and mechanisms of ALDH1A1 in ADPKD remains elusive.

METHODS: To investigate this, Aldh1A1 was knocked out and ALDH1A1 inhibited with disulfiram (DSF) either alone or together with PD-L1 antibody in Pkd1 mutant mice. The effect of ALDH1A1 on cyst growth, immune response and PKD associated signaling pathways as well as the target genes of ALDH1A1 was determined by immunostaining, Western blot, qRT-PCR, flow cytometry, CUT-Tag analysis, and CHIP assay.

RESULTS: ALDH1A1 was upregulated in ADPKD kidneys, leading to an activation of PKD associated signaling pathways, including AKT, S6, STAT3 and Rb. This was possibly mediated by AURKA, to increase cystic kidney epithelial cell proliferation. Knockout of Aldh1A1 delayed cyst growth in Pkd1 mutant kidneys. ALDH1A1 functions as a transcription factor to target diverse genes as examined by CUT-Tag analysis. Elevated ALDH1A1 regulated the transcription of Pd-l1 and Ccl2 to affect PD-L1 mediated immune surveillance and recruitment of macrophages, respectively. Inhibiting ALDH1A1 with DSF decreased cyst growth in aggressive (Pkd1nl/nl) and mild (Pkd1RC/RC) Pkd1 mouse models. Additionally, targeting ALDH1A1 in combination with PD-L1 antibody had a synergistic effect on delaying cyst growth in Pkd1 mutant mouse kidneys.

CONCLUSIONS: Our study suggests that DSF is a promising agent for ADPKD treatment and highlights novel therapeutic strategies by the combination of chemotherapy with immunotherapy in ADPKD treatment.

</details>


### [73] [The interaction of PD-1/PD-L1 and its inhibition by anti-PD-L1 antibody at the single-molecule level.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41388338/)
*Yonghui Zhang,Diego Pilati,Mingdong Dong,Kenneth A Howard*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究利用原子力显微镜（AFM）单分子力谱和AlphaFold建模，对比分析了PD-1/PD-L1复合物与atezolizumab/PD-L1复合物的结合机制。发现PD-1/PD-L1界面因氢键作用更稳定，而atezolizumab/PD-L1界面受疏水效应影响更具灵活性，为设计更有效的检查点抑制剂提供了单分子层面的结构、力学及动力学评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-1/PD-L1检查点信号及其抑制剂的阻断效果依赖于结合界面的分子相互作用。为了深入理解这些相互作用的机制，并开发更有效的检查点抑制分子，需要从单分子水平上对蛋白质相互作用进行结构、力学和动力学层面的评估。

Method: 采用原子力显微镜（AFM）单分子力谱技术，结合AlphaFold建模预测，研究了天然状态下PD-1与PD-L1复合物以及抗PD-L1抗体atezolizumab与PD-L1复合物的完整结构。通过这种方法评估单分子大分子相互作用。

Result: AFM结果显示，由于氢键的作用，PD-1/PD-L1结合界面表现出比atezolizumab/PD-L1复合物更高的稳定性。相比之下，疏水效应增强了atezolizumab/PD-L1界面的结合灵活性。两个复合物表现出不同的键寿命，这反映了结合界面的稳定性以及与界面灵活性相关的跃迁距离。

Conclusion: 该研究开发了一种接近天然生理状态的新型平台，能够从结构、力学和动力学角度在单分子水平上评估蛋白质相互作用。这项工作不仅有助于设计更有效的检查点抑制分子，还为评估单分子大分子相互作用提供了相关的方法论支持。

Abstract: Programmed cell death-1 (PD-1)/programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) checkpoint signaling and its blockade by checkpoint inhibitors are dependent on molecular interactions at the binding interface. In this work, the two complete complex structures in the protein native state of PD-1 with PD-L1, and the anti-PD-L1 antibody atezolizumab were investigated by atomic force microscopy (AFM) single-molecule force spectroscopy and predicted by AlphaFold modeling. AFM revealed that the PD-1/PD-L1 binding interface displayed greater stability than the atezolizumab/PD-L1 complex due to hydrogen bonding, while the hydrophobic effect enhanced binding flexibility at the atezolizumab/PD-L1 interface. The two complexes exhibited different bond lifetimes reflecting binding interface stability and transition distance related to the interface flexibility. This work provides relevant methodology to evaluate single-molecule macromolecular interactions. Impact statement Our research developed a novel and close-to-native physiological platform to evaluate protein interactions from structural, mechanical, and kinetic perspectives at the single-molecule level. This could be applied in the design of more effective checkpoint inhibitory molecules and provides relevant methodologies for evaluating single-molecule macromolecular interactions.

</details>


### [74] [Relationship between PD-L1 Expression and Outcomes of Salvage Treatment Following Durvalumab Consolidation.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41238478/)
*Tawee Tanvetyanon,Dung-Tsa Chen,Jhanelle E Gray*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究分析了III期非小细胞肺癌患者在度伐利尤单抗（Durvalumab）巩固治疗后疾病进展的挽救性治疗方案。结果显示，挽救性免疫治疗（IO）仅在PD-L1 ≥ 1%的患者中显著优于非免疫治疗方案，而在PD-L1 < 1%的患者中未见获益甚至数值上更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对接受度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗后出现疾病进展的非小细胞肺癌患者，其预后较差。虽然全身治疗是主要手段，但在“后度伐利尤单抗”环境下，是否应再次使用免疫治疗（IO）尚不明确，缺乏明确的临床指导证据。

Method: 基于全国去标识化数据库，回顾性分析接受同步放化疗、度伐利尤单抗巩固治疗及后续挽救性全身治疗的III期非小细胞肺癌患者。比较挽救性免疫治疗（或含免疫方案）与非免疫方案的总生存期（OS），并评估PD-L1表达水平作为预测因子的效应修饰作用。

Result: 共纳入104名患者（49例接受挽救性IO，55例接受非IO）。总体中位OS为12.7个月（IO组13.4个月 vs 非IO组9.5个月，P=.27，无显著差异）。亚组分析显示：PD-L1 < 1%患者中，IO组生存数值上更差（HR 1.88, P=.14）；PD-L1 ≥ 1%患者中，IO组生存显著更好（HR 0.48, P=.03）。PD-L1水平对治疗方案与生存期的关联具有显著交互作用（P-interaction=.007）。此外，体能状态差和社会经济指数低是显著的不良预后因素。

Conclusion: 在后度伐利尤单抗治疗背景下，挽救性免疫治疗方案仅对PD-L1 ≥ 1%的患者显示出优于非免疫方案的生存获益，提示PD-L1表达水平是选择后续治疗方案的关键生物标志物。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Disease progression during or after consolidation treatment with durvalumab, an immune-oncology agent (IO) for non-small cell lung cancer, confers a poor prognosis. Systemic therapy is the mainstay. Currently, standard first-line systemic therapy includes IO; however, in the post-durvalumab setting, it remains unclear if IO should be used again.

METHODS: We performed an analysis of a nationwide, deidentified database of patients with stage III non-small cell lung cancer who received concurrent chemoradiation, durvalumab consolidation, and salvage systemic therapy. Overall survival, measured from salvage treatment, was compared between salvage IO or IO-based regimens (ie IO regimens) and non-IO regimens. Predictive factor of PD-L1 level was demonstrated by effect modification.

RESULTS: Analyses included 104 patients: 49 patients (47%) received salvage IO regimens and 55 (53%) received non-IO regimens. The median overall survival was 12.7 months: 13.4 months with IO regimens versus 9.5 months with non-IO regimens, P = .27. Among patients with PD-L1 < 1% (n = 41), survival was numerically worse with IO regimens than with non-IO regimens: hazard ratio (HR) 1.88 (95% CI, 0.81-4.39, P = .14). However, among those with PD-L1 ≥ 1% (n = 63), survival was better with IO regimens than non-IO regimens: HR 0.48 (95% CI, 0.25-0.93, P = .03). The association between regimens and survival differed by PD-L1 level, P-interaction =.007. Poor performance status and lower socioeconomic index were significant adverse prognostic factors.

CONCLUSION: In post-durvalumab setting, this analysis suggests that salvage IO regimens are superior to non-IO regimens, but only among patients with PD-L1 ≥ 1%.

</details>


### [75] [A Phase II Study of Durvalumab, Doxorubicin, and Ifosfamide in Recurrent and/or Metastatic Pulmonary Sarcomatoid Carcinoma (KCSG LU-19-24).](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41188111/)
*Miso Kim,Jeonghwan Youk,Tae Min Kim,Gyeong-Won Lee,Dong-Wan Kim,Bhumsuk Keam*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究评估了度伐利尤单抗联合多柔比星和异环磷酰胺治疗复发或转移性肺肉瘤样癌（PSC）的疗效与安全性。尽管因招募困难提前终止，但在20名患者中观察到了35%的客观缓解率（ORR）和9.4个月的中位总生存期（OS），表明该联合方案具有临床活性，但需更大规模研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺肉瘤样癌（PSC）是一种罕见且侵袭性强的肿瘤，预后较差。传统化疗疗效有限，而免疫检查点抑制剂和多柔比星显示出潜在疗效。因此，本研究旨在评估度伐利尤单抗、多柔比星和异环磷酰胺联合方案在复发和/或转移性PSC中的有效性和安全性，以探索更优的治疗策略。

Method: 这是一项单臂临床试验。入组患者为复发或转移性PSC患者。治疗方案为：度伐利尤单抗（1500 mg，第1天）、多柔比星（20 mg/m²，静脉注射，第1-3天）和异环磷酰胺（1.5 g/m²，静脉注射伴美司钠，第2-4天），每3周一次，最多4个周期；随后进行度伐利尤单抗单药维持治疗，直至疾病进展或出现不可接受的毒性，最长持续12个月。主要终点为客观缓解率（ORR），次要终点包括无进展生存期（PFS）、总生存期（OS）、缓解持续时间（DOR）和毒性。

Result: 研究因招募率低而提前终止，共入组20例患者（中位年龄63.5岁）。在18例可评估患者中，PD-L1阳性率为88.9%。结果显示：ORR为35.0%（95% CI, 17.7%-55.8%）；中位DOR为5.3个月；中位随访7.0个月后，中位PFS为4.8个月，中位OS为9.4个月。19例患者发生不良事件（AEs），其中10例发生严重不良事件。

Conclusion: 度伐利尤单抗联合多柔比星和异环磷酰胺在复发和/或转移性PSC中显示出一定的临床活性。然而，鉴于样本量小且研究提前终止，仍需开展更大规模的临床研究以确认其获益并优化治疗策略。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Pulmonary sarcomatoid carcinomas (PSCs) are rare, aggressive tumors with a poor prognosis. Conventional chemotherapy shows limited efficacy, whereas immune checkpoint inhibitors and doxorubicin have shown potential. This study evaluated the efficacy and safety of durvalumab, doxorubicin, and ifosfamide in recurrent and/or metastatic PSC.

PATIENTS AND METHODS: Patients with recurrent or metastatic PSC received durvalumab (1500 mg, day 1), doxorubicin (20 mg/m² intravenously, days 1-3), and ifosfamide (1.5 g/m² intravenously with mesna, days 2-4) every 3 weeks for up to four cycles, followed by durvalumab monotherapy until disease progression or unacceptable toxicity, for up to 12 months. The primary endpoint was objective response rate (ORR). Secondary endpoints included progression-free survival (PFS), overall survival (OS), duration of response (DOR), and toxicity.

RESULTS: The study was prematurely terminated due to low recruitment rates, with 20 patients enrolled (15 male, 5 female; median age: 63.5 years). Among 18 evaluable cases, 16 (88.9%) were programmed death-ligand 1 positive. Six patients (30.0%) had prior palliative chemotherapy. ORR was 35.0% (95% CI, 17.7%-55.8%). The median DOR was 5.3 months (95% CI, 1.7-not estimated). After a median follow-up of 7.0 months (1.2-37.6), the median PFS and OS were 4.8 months (95% CI, 2.0-6.5) and 9.4 months (95% CI, 5.5-26.8), respectively. Adverse events (AEs) occurred in 19 patients, with serious AEs in 10 patients.

CONCLUSION: Durvalumab combined with doxorubicin and ifosfamide suggested clinical activity in recurrent and/or metastatic PSC. Larger studies are warranted to confirm benefits and refine treatment strategies.

</details>


### [76] [Real-world survival outcomes with first-line chemoimmunotherapy and biomarker analysis in extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41171522/)
*Emmanouil Panagiotou,Ioannis Athanasios Vathiotis,Austin Montgomery,Camille Moeckel,Mirsini Livanou,Michail Mastrogeorgiou,Athanasios Trimis,Ioannis Trontzas,Nikolaos Konstantinos Syrigos,Ioannis Mouratidis,Georgios Evangelou,Ilias Georgakopoulos-Soares,Konstantinos Nikolaos Syrigos*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究通过回顾性分析188名广泛期小细胞肺癌（ES-SCLC）患者，评估了一线化疗联合免疫治疗在真实世界中的疗效。结果显示，阿替利珠单抗组的生存获益优于度伐利尤单抗组，但在多变量分析中差异不显著；研究确定了肺、骨、肝转移及ECOG评分等预后因素，并构建了具有良好区分度的新型预后评分模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-L1抑制剂的批准改变了ES-SCLC的一线治疗格局，但目前关于其临床获益的真实世界（real-world）证据仍然有限，因此需要进一步验证其在实际临床环境中的疗效及潜在生物标志物的作用。

Method: 采用回顾性研究设计，纳入希腊雅典Sotiria胸科医院接受一线化疗联合免疫治疗的SCLC患者。使用Kaplan-Meier曲线计算真实世界无进展生存期（rwPFS）和总生存期（rwOS），并利用Cox比例风险回归分析识别患者特征与预后之间的关联。

Result: 中位rwPFS为6.5个月，中位rwOS为11.2个月。单变量分析显示，阿替利珠单抗组的rwOS（12.0 vs 9.2个月）和rwPFS均显著优于度伐利尤单抗组；但在多变量分析中，两种药物间的差异无统计学意义。肺、骨、肝转移，ECG PS，LDH和NLR与死亡风险增加相关。基于此构建的新型预后评分模型具有良好的区分能力（C统计量：0.73）。

Conclusion: ES-SCLC患者一线化疗联合免疫治疗的真实世界疗效与随机临床试验结果相当。预后评分与生存结局之间的关联应在未来的前瞻性研究中进一步探索。

Abstract: PURPOSE: The approval of programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) inhibitors in the first line of treatment has transformed the therapeutic landscape of extensive-stage small cell lung cancer (ES-SCLC); real-world (rw) evidence of clinical benefit is currently limited. In this study, we investigated the rw efficacy of first-line chemoimmunotherapy and the role of potential biomarkers.

METHODS/PATIENTS: We retrospectively assessed patients with SCLC receiving first-line chemoimmunotherapy at Sotiria Thoracic Diseases Hospital of Athens, Athens, Greece. Kaplan-Meier curves were used to calculate real-world progression-free survival (rwPFS) and real-world overall survival (rwOS). Cox proportional hazards regression analysis was utilized to identify associations between patient characteristics and outcomes.

RESULTS: 188 patients were included. Median rwPFS was 6.5 months (95% CI 5.8-7.1 months) and median rwOS was 11.2 months (95% CI 9.1-12.0 months). rwOS was higher in the atezolizumab group compared with the durvalumab group (median, 12.0 vs 9.2 months; hazard ratio [HR], 1.51; 95% CI 1.06-2.15; p = 0.02), similar results were observed for rwPFS (median, 6.5 vs. 6.0 months, HR, 1.55; 95% CI 1.10-2.16; p = 0.01). In multivariate analysis, the difference between atezolizumab and durvalumab was not statistically significant, while lung, bone and liver metastases, ECOG PS, LDH and NLR were associated with an increased risk of death. Associations were utilized for the generation of a novel prognostic score with good discriminatory power (C-statistic: 0.73).

CONCLUSIONS: Real-world efficacy of first-line chemoimmunotherapy in patients with ES-SCLC is comparable to randomized trials. The association between prognostic scores and survival outcomes in ES-SCLC should be explored in prospective studies.Query.

</details>


### [77] [NIR-II fluorescence membrane probes for rapidly labelling hybrid of probiotic outer membrane vesicles and anti-PD-L1 scFv over-expressing cellular vesicles with targeted photothermal-immunotherapy of colon cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41092647/)
*Chen Yu,Ziwei Hu,Genwen Hu,Qingyun Jia,Yuchen Xiao,Haroon Ahmad,Daoming Zhang,Zonglin Liu,Muhammad Sohaib Iqbal,Wenjun Zeng,Qi Chen,Qingqin Tan,Bing Guo*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于BODIPY的近红外二区（NIR-II）荧光膜探针（BMPs），用于快速、无洗涤标记脂质双层结构纳米载体（LBSNs）。该探针不仅实现了高保真度的生物分布追踪和结肠癌诊断，还通过光热效应和免疫调节（阻断PD-1/PD-L1、重塑肿瘤微环境）实现了协同增强的癌症治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脂质双层结构纳米载体（如脂质体和仿生囊泡）常使用可见光或近红外一区（NIR-I）荧光探针进行标记以追踪生物分布，但这些波段成像保真度较低。鉴于近红外二区（NIR-II）荧光成像具有显著更高的组织穿透力和信噪比，研究旨在开发适用于LBSNs标记的NIR-II发射膜探针，以实现更精准的诊疗一体化。

Method: 1. **探针合成**：合成具有供体-受体结构的BODIPY基NIR-II荧光膜探针（BMP1-2），其结构包含十二烷基链和少量阳离子电荷。
2. **载体构建**：制备由免疫益生菌外膜囊泡和过表达抗PD-L1 scFv的细胞纳米囊泡组成的混合LBSNs。
3. **标记策略**：利用BMPs与带负电LBSNs之间的强静电和疏水相互作用，在机械搅拌下2分钟内实现快速、高信噪比（ON/OFF > 115）的无洗涤标记，形成BMP@Hybrid复合物。
4. **性能评估与治疗验证**：评估探针的光稳定性、光热转换能力及对LBSNs生物功能的影响；在结肠癌模型中验证其靶向成像、光热治疗及免疫治疗效果。

Result: 1. **标记特性**：BMPs对LBSNs生物功能无害，具备高光热转换效率、良好光稳定性和平衡的亲脂性；实现2分钟内快速染色及超高信噪比的无洗涤成像。
2. **成像与诊断**：BMP1@Hybrid能主动靶向肿瘤，实现高精度的生物分布追踪、结肠癌诊断及肿瘤微环境分子成像。
3. **治疗效果**：BMP1@Hybrid通过光热效应破坏原发性肿瘤，同时阻断PD-1/PD-L1轴，调节肿瘤微环境（促进炎症细胞因子分泌、M2向M1巨噬细胞重极化、树突状细胞成熟及CD8+ T细胞局部浸润），显著增强治疗效果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新型的NIR-II荧光膜探针策略，成功解决了LBSNs标记中的成像保真度和操作便捷性问题。BMP1@Hybrid系统集成了NIR-II成像引导、光热治疗和免疫检查点阻断功能，为精确的癌症诊疗一体化提供了新的有效手段。

Abstract: Lipid-bilayer structured nanovectors (LBSNs) (e.g., liposomes and biomimetic vesicles) are burgeoning for diagnosis and therapy, and fluorescence labelling is often conducted for biodistribution tracing with visible/NIR-I fluorescence membrane probes (MPs). As NIR-II fluorescence imaging exhibits significantly improved fidelity, we synthesize NIR-II emissive donor-acceptor structured BODIPY-based MPs (BMP1-2) to label LBSNs composed of immunogenic probiotic outer membrane vesicles and cellular nanovesicles over-expressing anti-PD-L1 scFv, yielding BMP@Hybrid with tandem-amplified immunotherapy. The compact BMPs contain dodecyl chain and few cationic charges, forming strong electrostatic and hydrophobic interaction with negatively charged LBSNs, respectively. This contributes to features including negligible harm to biofunction of LBSNs, high photothermal conversion capability, good photostability, balanced lipophilicity, fast staining on LBSN within 2 min under mechanical stirring with ultrahigh ON/OFF ratio (>115), which offering wash-free imaging. Notably, BMP1@Hybrid could actively target tumor, facilitating biodistribution tracing, colon cancer diagnosis, and molecular imaging of tumoral microenvironments. Remarkably, BMP1@Hybrid efficiently destructs primary tumor by photothermal effect, blockades PD-1/PD-L1 axis, and modulates tumoral microenvironment with promoting secretion of inflammatory cytokines, M2-to-M1 macrophage repolarization, dendritic cell maturation, local infiltration of CD8+ T cells, and boosted therapeutic outcomes. This study provides a new strategy of NIR-II fluorescence membrane probes for the labelling of LBSNs and precise cancer theranostics.

</details>


### [78] [Real-world effectiveness and safety of adjuvant atezolizumab in pathological stage IIA-IIIB non-small cell lung cancer following curative surgery.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41988310/)
*Akifumi Nakamura,Nobuyuki Kondo,Seiji Matsumoto,Masaki Hashimoto,Ayumi Kuroda,Kyoshiro Takegahara,Koji Mikami,Toshiyuki Minami,Kozo Kuribayashi,Kazuhiro Kitajima,Takashi Kijima,Soichiro Funaki*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究评估了辅助阿替利珠单抗在可切除II-III期非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）患者中的真实世界疗效与安全性。结果显示，该疗法耐受性良好，2年无病生存率（DFS）为63.8%，且在PD-L1不同表达亚组间结果可比，支持其在临床实践中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于IMpower010 III期试验，术后阿替利珠单抗已在日本获批用于PD-L1阳性NSCLC的辅助治疗，但目前关于该疗法在真实世界环境中的有效性和安全性数据仍然有限。

Method: 这是一项单中心回顾性队列研究，纳入2021年1月至2024年12月期间接受完全切除术且术后接受至少一个周期铂类化疗后辅助阿替利珠单抗治疗的病理分期IIA-IIIB期NSCLC患者。研究评估了临床特征、治疗完成情况、无病生存期（DFS）、总生存期（OS）及不良事件（AEs），并使用Kaplan-Meier法估算生存结局。

Result: 在646名手术患者中，25名符合标准并接受治疗。中位随访27.4个月，中位DFS未达到，2年DFS率为63.8%。PD-L1 1-49%组与≥50%组的DFS结果相当（72.9% vs. 57.7%, P=0.37）。60.0%的患者发生不良事件（含2例3级），无4-5级毒性或治疗相关死亡。60.0%的患者完成了1年治疗。

Conclusion: 在真实世界队列中，辅助阿替利珠单抗显示出良好的耐受性和具有临床意义的DFS，涵盖包括PD-L1 1-49%在内的各表达亚组。尽管结果主要为描述性性质需谨慎解读，但提供了支持该疗法在精选的可切除II-III期NSCLC患者中具有潜在临床实用性的真实世界证据。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: Postoperative atezolizumab is approved as adjuvant therapy for programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1)-positive non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) in Japan, based on the phase III IMpower010 trial. However, real-world data on this therapeutic approach remains limited. This study aimed to evaluate the real-world effectiveness and safety of adjuvant atezolizumab in patients with pathological stage II-III NSCLC.

METHODS: This single-institution retrospective cohort study included patients with completely resected pathological stage IIA-IIIB [Union for International Cancer Control/American Joint Committee on Cancer (UICC/AJCC) staging system version 8] NSCLC who received at least one cycle of adjuvant atezolizumab after platinum-based chemotherapy between January 2021 and December 2024. Clinical characteristics, treatment completion, disease-free survival (DFS), overall survival (OS), and adverse events (AEs) were evaluated. Survival outcomes were estimated using the Kaplan-Meier method.

RESULTS: Among 646 patients who underwent curative-intent surgery, 25 met the eligibility criteria and received adjuvant atezolizumab. PD-L1 expression assessed using the 22C3 assay was <1% in 1 patient, 1-49% in 11, and ≥50% in 13; notably, the patient with PD-L1 <1% on 22C3 testing was found to be positive on SP263 testing. Median follow-up was 27.4 months. Median DFS was not reached; the 2-year DFS rate was 63.8%, with comparable outcomes between PD-L1 subgroups (PD-L1 1-49%: 72.9% vs. ≥50%: 57.7%; P=0.37). AEs occurred in 60.0%, including two grade 3 events. No grade 4-5 toxicities or treatment-related deaths were observed. Fifteen patients (60.0%) completed 1 year of treatment.

CONCLUSIONS: In this real-world cohort, adjuvant atezolizumab demonstrated favorable tolerability and clinically meaningful DFS across PD-L1 expression subgroups, including patients with PD-L1 1-49%. These findings are descriptive in nature and should be interpreted with caution, nevertheless, they provide real-world evidence supporting the potential clinical utility of adjuvant atezolizumab in appropriately selected patients with resected stage II-III NSCLC.

</details>


### [79] [Iodine-125 brachytherapy triggers immunogenic cell death and potentiates anti-PD-L1 immunotherapy in bone metastatic triple-negative breast cancer.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41988184/)
*Min Li,Zhenzhen Cui,Jiaxin Yang,Qiyu Sun,Yanbo Hu,Juan Shen,Zhihao Li,Xiaoyang Han,Yixuan Wang,Jiageng Tang,Hengsong Yu,Xiaowen Ma,Jing Wang*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究证实碘-125（125I）近距离放疗能通过诱导持续的免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD），重塑三阴性乳腺癌（TNBC）骨转移的免疫抑制微环境并激活全身抗肿瘤免疫。相比传统外照射放疗，125I在局部控制和免疫激活方面表现更优，且“125I优先”联合PD-L1抗体及白蛋白结合型紫杉醇的序贯疗法显示出最佳协同疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三阴性乳腺癌（TNBC）骨转移因免疫抑制微环境及传统外照射放疗（EBRT）引起的免疫激活有限且短暂，治疗极具挑战性。虽然诱导免疫原性细胞死亡（ICD）有望重塑该微环境，但其系统性效应尚待验证，因此需要探索能诱导持续ICD并激活全身免疫的新策略。

Method: 建立4T1小鼠乳腺癌胫骨骨转移及远端皮下种植的双肿瘤模型。系统评估不同剂量（0.3-0.8 mCi）的125I粒子单独或联合抗PD-L1抗体的疗效。利用Western blot、qRT-PCR、免疫组化和流式细胞术分析ICD生物标志物（CALR, HMGB1）及免疫细胞浸润；通过转录组测序探索ICD及相关免疫通路变化；使用靶向CALR的双模态纳米探针实现ICD的体内动态成像。对比125I近距离放疗与EBRT在肿瘤控制和免疫激活上的差异，并进行治疗序贯研究以确定最佳组合策略。

Result: 125I粒子以剂量依赖方式抑制肿瘤生长（0.8 mCi效果最佳且无显著毒性），有效诱导持续ICD，显著上调DAMPs（如CALR和HMGB1）。机制上，125I促进CD8α⁺ T细胞浸润，激活ICD相关通路，并在双肿瘤系统中抑制远端肿瘤生长，证明其触发了全身抗肿瘤免疫。相比EBRT，125I实现了更优的局部控制和更强的免疫激活。靶向CALR纳米探针成功实现ICD动态成像。序贯研究显示，“125I优先”随后联合PD-L1抗体和白蛋白结合型紫杉醇的方案疗效最佳。

Conclusion: 125I近距离放疗通过诱导持续ICD、重塑肿瘤免疫微环境及激活全身抗肿瘤免疫，克服了传统放疗的局限性。它不仅抑制TNBC骨转移及远端肿瘤生长，还显著增强CD8α⁺ T细胞浸润。“125I优先”的序贯联合疗法具有显著协同效应，确立125I为优越的免疫启动模式，为克服骨转移TNBC耐药提供了机制依据和临床转化潜力。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Triple-negative breast cancer (TNBC) with bone metastasis is challenging to treat due to an immunosuppressive microenvironment and the limited, transient immune activation from conventional external beam radiotherapy. Inducing immunogenic cell death (ICD) offers a strategy to remodel this environment, but its systemic effects remain to be validated. Here, we investigate iodine-125 (125I) brachytherapy as a method to induce sustained ICD and activate systemic antitumor immunity in TNBC bone metastasis using transcriptomic analysis, a dual-tumor model, and CALR-targeted molecular imaging.

METHODS: A dual-tumor model of 4T1 mouse breast cancer with tibial bone metastasis and distal subcutaneous implantation was established. The therapeutic efficacy of ¹²⁵I seeds at different doses (0.3-0.8 mCi), alone or in combination with anti-PD-L1 antibody, was systematically assessed. ICD biomarkers (CALR, HMGB1) and immune cell infiltration were analyzed using Western blot, qRT-PCR, immunohistochemistry, and flow cytometry. Transcriptome sequencing was performed to explore changes in ICD and related immune pathways. In vivo dynamic imaging of ICD was achieved using a CALR-targeting bimodal nanoprobe. Comparative analyses were conducted between 125I brachytherapy and EBRT to assess differences in tumor control and immune activation. Treatment sequencing studies were performed to evaluate the optimal combination strategy.

RESULTS: This study confirmed that 125I seeds effectively induce sustained immunogenic cell death, significantly upregulating damage-associated molecular patterns (DAMPs) such as CALR and HMGB1. In the 4T1 bone metastasis model, local implantation of 125I (0.3-0.8 mCi) inhibited tumor growth in a dose-dependent manner, with the 0.8 mCi group showing the best therapeutic effect and no significant toxicity. Mechanistically, 125I promoted CD8α⁺T cell infiltration, activated ICD-related pathways, and triggered systemic antitumor immunity, as demonstrated by inhibition of distant tumor growth in the bilateral tumor system. Compared with EBRT, 125I brachytherapy achieved superior local control and more robust immune activation. Using a CALR-targeting nanoprobe, dynamic in vivo imaging of ICD was successfully achieved. Treatment sequencing studies revealed that initiating with 125I brachytherapy followed by combination therapy with PD-L1 antibody and Abraxane resulted in the most favorable outcome, confirming this "125I-first" approach as an effective immune priming strategy.

DISCUSSION: This study demonstrates that 125I brachytherapy overcomes the limitations of conventional radiotherapy by inducing sustained immunogenic cell death, remodeling the tumor immune microenvironment, and activating systemic antitumor immunity. 125I seeds not only inhibit the growth of TNBC bone metastases and distal tumors but also significantly enhance the infiltration of CD8α⁺T cells into tumor sites. Notably, the sequential regimen of 125I brachytherapy followed by PD-L1 antibody and Abraxane shows significant synergistic effects. These findings establish 125I brachytherapy as a superior immune-priming modality and provide a mechanistic rationale for integrating low-dose-rate brachytherapy with immunotherapy to overcome resistance in bone-metastatic TNBC, offering strong potential for clinical translation., iodine-125 seeds, triple-negative breast cancer.

</details>


### [80] [The clinical spectrum and causal relationship assessment of checkpoint inhibitor-associated autoimmune diabetes mellitus (CIADM): A retrospective observational study.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41917751/)
*Youjia Li,Kai Qu,Qianting Yang,Jinming Zhang,Yitao Xu,Haitao Wang,Kanghuai Zhang,Cheng Wang,Mohd H Abdul-Aziz,Jiao Xie*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 本研究分析了4382名接受PD-1/PD-L1抑制剂治疗的患者，发现7例（0.16%）发生检查点抑制剂相关自身免疫性糖尿病（CIADM）。CIADM起病急骤，86%以糖尿病酮症酸中毒（DKA）为首发表现，伴有严重胰岛β细胞功能衰竭，但多数患者胰岛自身抗体阴性。研究确认了药物与CIADM之间的因果关系，指出需对高危人群（如中年男性、肝癌患者）进行主动血糖监测，且在血糖稳定后多数患者可安全恢复免疫治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在全面表征检查点抑制剂相关自身免疫性糖尿病（CIADM）的临床谱系，阐明其时间关系，并评估在接受抗PD-1/抗PD-L1抑制剂治疗的患者队列中，免疫检查点抑制剂与CIADM之间的因果关系，以填补对该严重免疫相关不良反应（irAE）认知的空白。

Method: 采用回顾性队列研究方法，从2020年至2024年间接受PD-1/PDL1抑制剂治疗的4382名住院患者中筛选出确诊CIADM的个体。系统收集人口学特征、肿瘤病史、免疫检查点抑制剂方案、实验室参数（包括血糖控制、C肽和胰岛自身抗体）及临床结局数据。使用Naranjo算法评估药物与不良反应之间的因果关系。

Result: 在4382名患者中检出7例CIADM（发生率0.16%）。患者主要为男性，中位年龄52岁，最常见原发恶性肿瘤为肝细胞癌（3/7）。所有患者均在中位8个疗程后发病。86%（6/7）的患者起病时表现为糖尿病酮症酸中毒（DKA）。所有患者均出现严重的胰腺β细胞功能障碍（空腹及餐后C肽不可测），但仅1例患者胰岛自身抗体阳性。Naranjo评分显示所有病例均为“可能”至“高度可能”的因果关系。所有患者需终身胰岛素治疗，其中6患者在血糖稳定后成功重启免疫治疗且未加重高血糖。

Conclusion: CIADM是一种严重的免疫相关不良反应，特征为起病急骤、高比例DKA发生率和深度胰岛素缺乏，且常缺乏传统胰岛自身抗体。PD-1/PD-L1抑制剂与CIADM之间存在明确的因果联系。研究强调了对接受ICI治疗的患者（特别是具有特定人口学特征如中年男性肝癌患者）进行主动血糖监测的必要性。此外，通过多学科协作管理，在严格控制代谢状况下恢复免疫治疗是可行的，有助于优化肿瘤学和代谢预后。

Abstract: AIMS: This study aims to characterize the clinical spectrum, elucidate the temporal relationship and assess the causality of checkpoint inhibitor-associated autoimmune diabetes mellitus (CIADM) in a cohort of patients receiving anti-programmed cell death-1 (PD-1)/anti-programmed death ligand-1 (PD-L1) inhibitor therapy.

METHODS: Individuals who developed CIADM were identified among 4382 hospitalized patients receiving PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitor therapy between 2020 and 2024. Demographic characteristics, oncological history, immune checkpoint inhibitor regimens, laboratory parameters (including glycemic control, C-peptide and islet autoantibodies) and clinical outcomes were systematically collected. Causality was evaluated using the Naranjo algorithm.

RESULTS: Among 4382 patients who received PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitors, seven (0.16%) were diagnosed with CIADM during the study period. Seven patients were predominantly male, with a median age of 52 years (range: 44-62), and hepatocellular carcinoma was the most common primary malignancy (3/7). All patients developed CIADM after a median of 8 cycles (range: 5-23) of PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitors (sintilimab, camrelizumab, envafolimab or atezolizumab). Notably, 86% (6/7) of patients presented with diabetic ketoacidosis (DKA) at onset. Severe pancreatic β-cell dysfunction was universal, with undetectable fasting and postprandial C-peptide levels. Islet autoantibodies were positive in only one patient. The Naranjo score indicated a 'probable' to 'highly probable' causal relationship in all cases. All patients required lifelong insulin therapy. Following glycemic stabilization, immunotherapy was successfully reinitiated in six patients without exacerbating hyperglycemia.

CONCLUSION: CIADM is a severe immune-related adverse event (irAE) characterized by abrupt onset, a high rate of DKA, and profound insulin deficiency, often in the absence of conventional islet autoantibodies. A definitive causal link exists between PD-1/PD-L1 inhibitors and CIADM. Our findings underscore the necessity for proactive glycemic monitoring in patients undergoing ICI therapy, especially in populations such as middle-aged males with cancers known to have specific demographic profiles (e.g., hepatocellular carcinoma). Importantly, the resumption of immunotherapy is feasible with careful management, highlighting the critical role of a multidisciplinary approach to optimize oncological and metabolic outcomes.

</details>


### [81] [Phase 1 study of ceralasertib, an ATR kinase inhibitor, in combination with durvalumab in patients with recurrent or metastatic NSCLC or HNSCC.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41917211/)
*Juanita S Lopez,Kevin J Harrington,Seock-Ah Im,Keun-Wook Lee,Sophie Postel-Vinay,Jacob S Thomas,Natalia Lukashchuk,Sophie E Willis,Itziar Irurzun-Arana,Benjamin Webb,Jyoti Nehra,Alan Lau,Arsène-Bienvenu Loembé,Emma Dean,Matthew G Krebs*

Source: PubMed

Main category: PD-L1 Antibody

TL;DR: 这项多中心、模块化、1期临床试验评估了ATR抑制剂ceralasertib联合PD-L1抑制剂durvalumab在既往治疗过的晚期/转移性非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）或头颈部鳞状细胞癌（HNSCC）患者中的安全性和耐受性。研究确定了推荐2期剂量为durvalumab 1500 mg（第1天）加ceralasertib 240 mg BID（第15-28天），并观察到一定的抗肿瘤活性和药理学效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ATR抑制剂ceralasertib与免疫检查点抑制剂durvalumab联合使用的安全性、耐受性及初步疗效，旨在为晚期/转移性NSCLC和HNSCC患者提供新的治疗选择，并确定适合进一步研究的剂量方案。

Method: 采用递增剂量设计，患者接受不同剂量和给药频率的ceralasertib（80/160/240 mg BID或320 mg QD）联合固定剂量durvalumab（1500 mg）。主要终点是评估组合疗法的安全性和耐受性，次要终点包括客观缓解率、疾病稳定率及药代动力学/药效学指标。

Result: 共纳入60名患者，其中两名患者出现剂量限制性毒性（DLT）。总体不良事件发生率为98.3%，≥3级不良事件发生率为51.7%。推荐2期剂量为durvalumab 1500 mg + ceralasertib 240 mg BID。客观缓解率为8.3%，疾病稳定率为51.7%。在14对活检样本中，10例显示出pRAD50增加的药理学活性。

Conclusion: Ceralasertib联合durvalumab在晚期/转移性NSCLC和HNSCC患者中具有良好的耐受性，并表现出初步的抗肿瘤活性和药理学效应，支持进一步开展2期临床研究。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: This multicentre, modular, Phase 1 study evaluated escalating doses of ATR (ataxia telangiectasia and Rad3-related kinase) inhibitor ceralasertib plus PD-L1 inhibitor durvalumab in patients with previously treated advanced/metastatic non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) or head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC).

METHODS: Patients received ceralasertib 80/160/240 mg twice-daily (BID) or 320 mg once-daily (QD) for 7 (Days 22-28) or 14 (Days 15-28) days, plus durvalumab 1500 mg (Day 1), per 28-day cycle. The primary objective was to investigate the safety/tolerability of the combination.

RESULTS: Sixty patients were treated. Two patients had dose-limiting toxicities of: Grade 3 thrombocytopenia with Grade 3 anaemia (ceralasertib 320 mg QD for 14 days); and Grade 4 thrombocytopenia with Grade 3 neutropenia accompanied by systemic chest infection (ceralasertib 240 mg BID for 14 days). Overall, 59 (98.3%) patients had treatment-emergent adverse events; 31 (51.7%) had grade ≥3 events. The recommended Phase 2 dose was durvalumab 1500 mg (Day 1) plus ceralasertib 240 mg BID (Days 15-28). Five (8.3%) patients had objective responses; 31 (51.7%) had stable disease. Pharmacodynamic activity (pRAD50 increase) was observed in 10/14 paired biopsies.

CONCLUSION: Ceralasertib plus durvalumab was tolerated and associated with antitumour activity in advanced/metastatic NSCLC and HNSCC.

TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT02264678.

</details>
